# RESEARCH QUEUE — researcher → executor handoff

This file is the contract between two autonomous scheduled loops:

- **`rec-sota-research-loop`** (every 6h) — does deep web research, invents ONE novel,
  in-environment-feasible idea, and APPENDS a fully-specified proposal block below.
  It is research/plan-only: it never runs GPU jobs, never edits model code, never
  deletes anything. It only ever APPENDS to this file.
- **`rec-sota-hourly-loop`** (every 3h) — the executor. Before falling back to its own
  A/B/C directions, it consumes the oldest proposal here whose status is `QUEUED`:
  implements the flag in `run_sasrec_sbert.py` (default-OFF, zero-init, smoke-tested),
  runs the command, then flips the proposal's status to `DONE <result>` in place and
  logs the number in `NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md`.

## Proposal block format (researcher writes exactly this)

```
## PROPOSAL <YYYY-MM-DD>-<n> — <short title>  [status: QUEUED]
- **Idea (novel):** ...
- **Source(s):** <arxiv id / title / year>
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** ...
- **Mechanism / how to implement:** <where the flag goes; the math; zero-init no-op at start>
- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `... --out _bestrec_run/results_<NAME>_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_<NAME>_VG.log 2>&1`
- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN) → full 40ep; >0.0660 single-seed ⇒ 5-seed; 5-seed mean >0.0760 ⇒ strict review.
- **Kill criterion:** ...
- **Expected delta & rationale:** ...
```

Status lifecycle: `QUEUED` → (executor) `RUNNING` → `DONE <test NDCG@10 + one-line verdict>` (or `DEAD <reason>`).

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-1 — Causal learnable spectral filter (leak-free FMLP/BSARec adaptation)  [status: DONE — K50 test 0.0650, K8 test 0.0653 (seed 20260608, full-catalog n_eval=94,762). MARGINAL POSITIVE: both kernels beat prior single-seed best 0.0639 (+0.0011/+0.0014), consistent across K ⇒ real signal, not noise. First architectural lever in the campaign to nudge test up. But below the 0.0660 5-seed gate and far below the 0.0760 mandate. NOT DEAD (test rose, didn't hit val-up/test-down clause), NOT SOTA. Spectral/FIR inductive-bias axis = genuine small effect; the ~0.065 in-environment ceiling holds.]

- **Idea (novel):** Insert ONE strictly-causal, per-channel learnable temporal filter as a zero-init gated residual on the sequence embeddings, just before the HSTU stack. This is the **leak-free, causal** realization of the FMLP-Rec / BSARec "learnable frequency filter," a structurally new operation the model literally cannot otherwise represent. BSARec *proves* (Thm 3.1) that self-attention is inherently a **low-pass filter** that oversmooths and discards high-frequency (short-term-trend) signal; HSTU's pointwise attention has the same averaging character. A learnable per-channel FIR filter can re-introduce / sharpen exactly the high-frequency recency structure attention smooths away — a function class no probe in the ~50-probe ledger has ever added.

- **Source(s):** BSARec — "An Attentive Inductive Bias for Sequential Recommendation beyond the Self-Attention," Shin et al., AAAI 2024 (arXiv:2312.10325; NDCG@10 Amazon Beauty 0.0611 vs SASRec 0.0283, +116%). FMLP-Rec — "Filter-enhanced MLP is All You Need for Sequential Recommendation," Zhou et al., WWW 2022 (arXiv:2202.13556). Both implement the filter in **pure PyTorch via FFT**, and both are **bidirectional** (no causal mask) — see "Closest prior probe & why this differs."

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Closest is **c3 (continuous time-decay attention kernel, DEAD 0.0489)** — but c3 reweights *attention scores* by a learned function of the time-gap; it is still inside the attention/low-pass family and overfit the LLOO timing. This proposal operates in a **different signal space entirely**: a per-channel temporal/spectral *filter on the embeddings*, orthogonal to and outside the span of attention (attention cannot sharpen; a FIR filter can). Also distinct from `--time-bias` (bucketed additive score bias), `--pos-rab` (relative-position score bias), and EMA/CL4SRec (weight-/sequence-space regularizers). **Crucial defensibility difference vs the source papers:** FMLP-Rec and BSARec are both *bidirectional* — their FFT mixes future positions into each position's representation. Our training is **all-position causal next-item** (run_sasrec_sbert.py L158–159: "target=[i2..iL,pad]; loss counts positions where target!=pad"), so a bidirectional filter would **leak the label** into earlier-position predictions and inflate results illegitimately. We therefore use a **strictly causal** filter (left-padded long-kernel depthwise conv = the time-domain dual of a learnable per-channel spectral filter), which is leak-free by construction. The causal correctness fix is itself a defensible contribution the cited papers omit.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag `--causal-filter` (default OFF → bit-identical no-op) with optional `--filter-kernel K` (default K = max_seq_len = 50). In `SASRecSBERT.__init__`, when on, create `self.causal_filter = nn.Conv1d(d_model, d_model, kernel_size=K, groups=d_model, bias=False)` (depthwise = one learnable FIR filter per feature channel; K×d ≈ 3.2k params) and a scalar gate `self.filter_gate = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))`. **Init for clean no-op + smooth start:** zero-init `filter_gate` (this alone guarantees the exact no-op at init); init the conv kernel to a **causal delta** (weight 1.0 at the last/most-recent tap, 0 elsewhere) so the filter begins as all-pass and learns to deviate. In `encode()`, immediately **after L593 `x = self.drop(x)` and before the HSTU blocks**, insert:
  ```
  if self.causal_filter is not None:
      xt = x.transpose(1, 2)                       # (B, d, L)
      xt = F.pad(xt, (K - 1, 0))                   # LEFT-pad only  -> strictly causal
      y  = self.causal_filter(xt).transpose(1, 2)  # (B, L, d), out[t] depends only on positions <= t
      x  = x + self.filter_gate * (y - x)          # gate=0 at init -> x unchanged (exact no-op)
  ```
  Left-padding by K−1 (no right pad) makes output position t a function of input positions ≤ t only — no future leakage under the all-position loss. Re-zero padded input rows are already handled upstream by item_emb padding_idx; verify the (B,L,d) padding mask is reapplied after the filter if the HSTU block expects zeroed pads (it keys off `keep`/key-pad-mask, so a small nonzero leak into pad positions is masked out at attention time — but reapply `x = x * keep_mask` after the filter to be safe).

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --causal-filter --filter-kernel 50 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_V1_causalfilter_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_V1_causalfilter_VG.log 2>&1`

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN, full-catalog n_eval≈94,762) → full 40ep; single-seed best-by-val test NDCG@10 > 0.0660 ⇒ escalate to 5-seed (seeds 20260608–12); 5-seed mean > 0.0760 ⇒ executor enters strict code+paper review before any claim.

- **Kill criterion:** best-by-val test ≤ 0.0639 (ties/below current single-seed best H2) ⇒ DEAD — attention's low-pass smoothing is NOT the in-environment bottleneck and the filtered signal is redundant with the existing stack. ALSO DEAD if it reproduces the campaign's val≫test signature (validation rises toward/past 0.072 while test holds ≤ 0.064), i.e. the filter merely adds overfittable capacity like c3/EMA/dual-text. If K=50 overfits (val up, test down), one cheap follow-up with `--filter-kernel 8` (smoother, fewer params) is permitted before declaring dead.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — this is primarily a **high-information probe on a genuinely new (spectral / FIR-filter) inductive-bias axis** the campaign has never touched, and it is the single remaining lever with a *representational-impossibility* argument: a low-pass attention stack provably cannot synthesize the high-frequency/sharpening response a learnable FIR filter provides, so unlike c1/c2/EMA/CL4SRec (which reused signals the stack already captured) this adds a function class genuinely outside the model's current span. Mechanistically it could recover short-term-trend recency structure that smoothing attention discards. Realistic expected delta: **0 to +4%** (0.064 → ~0.064–0.067); most probable outcome is neutral given the hard ~0.064 ceiling, and it is **unlikely to reach 0.0760** (that residual is isolated to the unreproducible HSTU CUDA-kernel numerics). But it is cheap (~10 min/40ep, ~3.2k params), leak-free by causal design, and decisively tests whether "attention oversmoothing" explains any of the in-environment ceiling — a clean positive OR negative result either way. If it lifts test past 0.0660 it is the first non-neutral architectural addition of the campaign and warrants immediate 5-seed escalation.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-2 — Niche-overlap fitness-sharing crowding penalty (ecology → inter-item competition in the loss)  [status: DEAD — single-seed test 0.0652 (seed 20260608, full-catalog n_eval=94,762), ties/below the U2 single-seed 0.0653 and within seed-noise of the U2 5-seed mean 0.0649±0.0002; below the 0.0660 gate ⇒ kill criterion met, no 5-seed. KEY INTERPRETABLE READOUT: learned β = −7.42 (strongly NEGATIVE) — the model learned to BOOST, not penalize, crowded-popular-niche targets, the EXACT OPPOSITE of the fitness-sharing hypothesis. Confirms the analyst's popularity-over-reliance diagnosis at the mechanism level but REFUTES the remedy: anti-crowding hurts, the stack wants to lean further into popularity, and full-catalog NDCG already rewards popular hits so the signal was saturated. Symmetric/eval-side follow-up declined (gated on a POSITIVE β, which did not occur; eval-side popularity manipulation = inflation trap). First inter-item-interaction term in the campaign — a clean negative.]

- **Idea (novel):** Add a **default-OFF, zero-init learnable crowding penalty** to the full-softmax CE that makes semantically-redundant catalog items *compete* for the single next-item slot, instead of being scored independently. Grounds cross-field idea **XF-2026-06-16-1** (ecology: limiting-similarity / Goldberg–Richardson **fitness sharing**, where an individual's fitness is divided by the crowding of its niche). Every current operation scores each item in isolation — eval is `final = last_h @ all_items.T` (run_sasrec_sbert.py **L1016**), and the training CE gathers a per-item `target_scores` with no inter-item term. Under the strong popularity bias the analyst diagnosed (ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-1: "the winning stack over-relies on popularity and floods redundant popular clusters"), the model piles probability onto a tight cluster of near-identical popular items (franchise/sequel/re-skin). The fix borrowed from ecology: discount the reward a target earns when it sits in a **crowded, popular semantic niche**, forcing the encoder to earn probability via *distinguishing* signal rather than niche popularity. This is the **first probe in the ~55-probe ledger to introduce an inter-item interaction at scoring/loss time** — a genuinely new axis.

- **Source(s):** Fitness sharing — Goldberg & Richardson 1987, "Genetic algorithms with sharing for multimodal function optimization" (ICGA). Limiting similarity / species packing — MacArthur & Levins 1967 (Am. Nat.); May 1973. Honest CS prior art for "diversity in the training loss" (attributed, NOT claimed as ours): **DPP-Likelihood losses for sequential rec** — Liu, SIGIR 2022 (arXiv:2204.11562; DSL/CDSL); **PD/PDA popularity deconfounding** — Zhang et al., SIGIR 2021 (arXiv:2105.06067); **logit adjustment** — Menon et al., ICLR 2021 (arXiv:2007.07314). The red-bus/blue-bus IIA-violation framing: Debreu 1960; Tversky 1972.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probes are **`--text-sim-bias`** and **TAPE (`--text-prototypes`)** — both are *attractive* and act on *representation/attention* (text-sim adds `cos(text_i,text_j)` inside attention to pull similar history positions together, L657; TAPE adds shared learnable capacity to similar items' features, L588–592). This is the **opposite sign and a different locus**: a *repulsive* term in the *loss target* that makes semantically-similar *candidate* items split exposure. Also distinct from label-smoothing (U-series, current best): LS adds a *uniform* eps floor over all items at the target (flat, popularity-agnostic); this subtracts a *crowding-shaped, popularity-weighted* penalty that is large only for targets in dense popular niches. Vs the honest CS analogues: unlike **DPP-likelihood (Liu 2022)** it builds **no kernel and no determinant** — it is a single scalar `−β·log(1+crowd)` on the target logit, O(n·K) precompute vs per-step log-det over a set; unlike **PD/PDA (Zhang 2021)** it penalizes **niche-local** crowding (popularity *shared within a text cluster*, `A_i·(Aᵀpop)`), not raw global item popularity; unlike **logit adjustment (Menon 2021)** the offset is a *similarity-modulated* `log(1+crowd_i)` rather than a raw per-class `log π_i`. New: an inter-item competition term, attached to the loss, modulated by frozen text-niche co-membership.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag **`--niche-share-beta` (float, default 0.0 ⇒ exact no-op)**. Requires `--text-prototypes` (always on in the winning stack); assert it, else error like `--expert-heads` does (L566).
  1. **Train-only popularity vector (the leakage-critical step).** In `main()`, immediately after `user_seqs = build_user_sequences(train_inters)` (**L1240**), compute popularity from **TRAIN interactions only**: `pop = torch.bincount(torch.tensor([i for u in user_seqs for i in user_seqs[u]]), minlength=n_items+1).float()` (or equivalently bincount over `train_inters` item ids). `train_inters` is loaded from `train_csv` exclusively (L1230–1233); `valid_inters`/`test_inters` are separate and are **never** consulted here — so `pop` is leak-free by construction. (Defensive: a 5-core LLOO target item can still appear in *other users'* train histories, which is legitimate train signal; what must never enter `pop` is the val/test *target rows themselves* — they don't, since they live only in `valid_inters`/`test_inters`.)
  2. **Frozen niche-crowding vector.** The frozen TAPE assignment `A` is already a registered buffer `proto_assign_0`, shape `(n_items+1, K)`, pad row = 0 (built L1266–1273). Compute once at construction (pass `pop` into `SASRecSBERT.__init__`, or compute in `main()` and register): `crowd = A @ (A.t() @ pop)`  → shape `(n_items+1,)`, the soft count of popularity-weighted items sharing each item's prototypes (the fitness-sharing denominator, with cluster co-membership as the sharing kernel `sh(d)`). Store `self.register_buffer("niche_crowd", torch.log1p(crowd))` (frozen) and `self.niche_share_beta = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(1))` (**zero-init ⇒ exact no-op at start**). Cost: one O(n·K) matvec, K=512, done once — negligible.
  3. **Apply in the loss target only.** Thread `niche_share_beta=args.niche_share_beta` through the `train_one_epoch(...)` call (L1342–1354) and the `chunked_full_softmax_loss(...)` call (L836–839), and add a param `niche_share_beta: float = 0.0` to `chunked_full_softmax_loss` (L698). Inside it, after `target_scores` is fully gathered (after the chunk loop, **before L753**), subtract the penalty from the target logit only:
    ```
    if niche_share_beta != 0.0:
        pen = model.niche_share_beta * model.niche_crowd[pos_ids]   # (n_valid,)
        target_scores = target_scores - pen
    ```
    This enters both the hard-CE branch (L763, `-(target_scores - lse)`) and the label-smoothed branch (L761, where the `(1-eps)*target_scores` term carries it; the `lse`/`mean_score` normalizer terms are intentionally left unpenalized so the penalty is a pure target-margin discount, matching the fitness-sharing "divide the focal individual's fitness" form). β zero-init ⇒ `pen ≡ 0` at start ⇒ bit-identical to the current loss. **Defensibility bonus:** the penalty is applied at **training only** — eval scoring (L1016) is untouched, so there is **zero test-time popularity manipulation** (no risk of trivially up-/down-ranking head items at eval, the exact trap PD-style score adjustment courts). The learned sign/magnitude of β is itself an interpretable finding (β>0 ⇒ the stack was over-rewarding crowded popular niches).

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --niche-share-beta 1.0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_W1_nicheshare_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_W1_nicheshare_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **strongest confirmed config** (winning stack + label-smoothing 0.2, the only multi-seed-confirmed lever, 5-seed baseline **0.0649 ± 0.0002**). `--causal-filter` deliberately OFF (it is single-seed/unconfirmed; keeping it off gives clean attribution of the niche-share delta against the U2 baseline). `--niche-share-beta 1.0` sets the *initial* β scale only if implemented as a fixed scalar; per the mechanism β is a zero-init learnable `nn.Parameter`, so the flag value just needs to be any nonzero float to **enable** the path (the magnitude is learned). If the executor implements β as a fixed (non-learnable) hyperparameter instead, sweep `{0.5, 1.0, 2.0}`.

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog n_eval≈94,762; ep2 test in the ~0.040–0.044 band matching the LS0.2 trajectory) → full 40ep; single-seed best-by-val test NDCG@10 **> 0.0660** ⇒ escalate to 5-seed (seeds 20260608–12); 5-seed mean **> 0.0760** ⇒ executor enters strict code+paper review before any claim. Intermediate check: if single-seed beats the U2 single-seed (~0.0653) with non-overlapping behavior, still 5-seed to compare against U2's 0.0649 ± 0.0002 band.

- **Kill criterion:** best-by-val test ≤ **0.0653** (ties/below the LS0.2 single-seed `U2`) ⇒ DEAD — inter-item niche competition is redundant with the popularity signal the stack already captures, OR β simply learns ≈0 (report the learned β: if `|β|·max(niche_crowd)` ≪ the score scale, the model voted the penalty off — a clean negative). ALSO DEAD if it reproduces the campaign's signature (validation rises toward/past 0.072 while test holds ≤ 0.065), i.e. the penalty only reshapes the val-specific ranking. If β learns a clear positive sign but test is flat, one cheap follow-up applying the **symmetric** catalog-wide variant (subtract `β·niche_crowd[k]` from every chunk logit via the chunk sum, a niche prior at *eval* too) is permitted before declaring dead — but only if the target-only version showed a learned-β signal.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — a **high-information probe on a genuinely new axis** (the first inter-item competition term in the loss; every prior probe scored items independently), directly targeting the analyst's diagnosed failure mode (popularity over-reliance + redundant-popular-cluster flooding). Mechanistically, discounting crowded-popular-niche targets should push the encoder toward tail/distinguishing discrimination. **But two honest headwinds:** (1) full-catalog NDCG itself *rewards* popular hits, so a penalty that fights genuine popularity could be net-neutral or hurt — mitigated by β being learnable (it can shrink to 0); (2) the campaign's residual gap is attributed to unreproducible HSTU-kernel numerics, so the in-environment ceiling (~0.065 with LS) likely holds. Realistic expected delta: **−1% to +3%** (0.0649 → ~0.064–0.067); most probable outcome neutral, **unlikely to reach 0.0760**. Its value is largely *diagnostic*: it decisively tests whether explicit inter-item competition can convert the stack's known popularity over-reliance into test gain, and the learned β is an interpretable readout either way. Cheap (~7 min/40ep, **+1 scalar parameter**, O(n·K) one-time precompute), leak-free (popularity from TRAIN only; penalty at train only, eval untouched), cleanly attributed (ecology source + DPP/PD/logit-adjustment CS analogues all cited). If it lifts test past 0.0660 it is the first inter-item-interaction win of the campaign and warrants immediate 5-seed escalation.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-3 — Frequency-adaptive James–Stein shrinkage of item embeddings toward the TAPE semantic-prototype mean  [status: DEAD — single-seed test 0.0673 (seed 20260608, full-catalog n_eval=94,762), ties/just-below the confirmed V2 5-seed 0.0674±0.0003 and below the 0.0680 gate ⇒ kill criterion met, no 5-seed. KEY READOUT: learned c=0.0044 ⇒ λ_max≈0.0044 at min_freq=1 (only 0.4% shrinkage even for the rarest items; negligible for median freq=9) — the model VOTED THE SHRINKAGE NEARLY OFF (the c≈0/λ_max≪0.05 DEAD clause). Adaptive tail shrinkage toward the TAPE semantic mean transfers no test signal the additive TAPE term + LS + filter don't already capture. The "clear-positive-c" trigger for the fixed-c tail-slice follow-up was NOT met. 2nd consecutive queued lever the model voted off on V2 (after W1 niche-share β=−7.42) ⇒ the V2 stack has saturated the in-environment signal.]

- **Idea (novel):** Add a **default-OFF, zero-init learnable convex shrinkage** of each item's learned id-embedding toward its **semantic-cluster mean** (the already-computed TAPE prototype reconstruction `m_i = Σ_l A_{i,l}·P_l`), with the **shrinkage intensity modulated per item by its train-only interaction count** — heaviest for the rarest items, vanishing for head items. Grounds cross-field idea **XF-2026-06-16-4** (statistics: **Stein's paradox** / empirical-Bayes shrinkage; Efron–Morris). Each catalog item's learned 64-d id-vector is a per-item MLE estimated from that item's interactions; the catalog is dominated by long-tail items seen only a handful of times (the problem statement's "long-tail sparsity"). Stein's theorem (d=64 ≫ 3) *guarantees* these high-variance, few-sample estimates are improved in total risk by shrinking them toward a common group mean. We already have the ideal group mean for free — the TAPE prototype reconstruction `m_i` is the learnable capacity shared across an item's semantic neighborhood, i.e. "the mean of items like me." This is the **first probe in the ~57-probe campaign that adaptively REDUCES tail capacity** (rather than adding capacity), placing it in the *only proven-to-transfer class* — label smoothing, the lone multi-seed-confirmed gain, is itself an anti-overconfidence regularizer, and the causal filter is a *restricted* inductive bias; every "add-capacity" probe (decay-kernel, dual-text, experts, EMA, n_heads) inflated val without moving test.

- **Source(s):** Stein's paradox — Stein 1956 (3rd Berkeley Symp.); James & Stein 1961, "Estimation with quadratic loss." Empirical-Bayes / observed-shrinkage form — Efron & Morris 1973 (JASA, "Stein's estimation rule and its competitors"), 1975/1977 (the baseball-batting-average example; posterior mean `(1−λ)·MLE + λ·m`, `λ = σ²/(σ²+τ²)`). Honest CS prior art for "frequency-aware embedding regularization" (attributed, NOT claimed as ours, and mechanistically distinct — see below): **LambdaOpt** (Chen et al., KDD 2019, arXiv:1905.11596) and **adaptive/frequency-tiered weight decay** for low-frequency embeddings (ICLR 2026, arXiv:2511.06374) — both shrink the embedding **norm toward the origin**; cold-start **attribute→embedding mapping** (DropoutNet / Heater lineage) *replaces* a missing embedding. None shrink a *trained* per-item vector toward a *learned semantic-cluster mean* with a *Stein/empirical-Bayes, count-modulated* weight. Web search (2026-06-16) found no paper integrating James–Stein shrinkage, item embeddings, and a semantic-prototype mean for sequential rec.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probes are **TAPE (`--text-prototypes`)** and **text-init warm-start (`--text-init-emb`, S1, NEUTRAL 0.0636)**. TAPE *adds* a shared prototype term `m_i` (always-on, frequency-agnostic, additive) — it never *removes* capacity from the id-table. Text-init *initializes* the id-table from text PCA then lets it move freely (a one-time warm-start, no ongoing constraint). This is categorically different: an **ongoing, frequency-adaptive convex shrinkage** of the per-item id-vector *toward* that shared mean, with weight `λ_i = c/(c + n_i)` set by observed count `n_i` — a regularizer that **subtracts** effective tail capacity, modulated by sparsity. It is also distinct from **label smoothing** (acts on the loss *target*; this acts on the *representation*) and from the queued **niche-share-beta** (PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-2; that adds an inter-item *loss* penalty — this is a per-item *embedding* shrinkage, a different locus and sign). No probe in the campaign does adaptive, sample-size-weighted shrinkage of embeddings toward a semantic centroid.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag **`--js-shrink` (action='store_true', default OFF ⇒ exact no-op)**. Requires `--text-prototypes` (always on in the winning stack); assert it, mirroring `--expert-heads` at L566 (`assert self.use_prototypes, "--js-shrink requires --text-prototypes (TAPE)"`).
  1. **Train-only frequency buffer (the leakage-critical step).** In `main()`, build the per-item interaction count from **TRAIN interactions only** — reuse the exact leak-free construction already audited for PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-2: bincount over the train item ids (`user_seqs` built from `train_inters`; `valid_inters`/`test_inters` are never consulted), giving `item_freq` shape `(n_items+1,)`, pad row = 0. Pass it into `SASRecSBERT.__init__` and `self.register_buffer("item_freq", item_freq.float())` (frozen). (Note: a held-out target item legitimately still appears in *other users'* train histories — that is valid train signal; what must never enter `item_freq` is the val/test *target rows themselves*, and they don't.)
  2. **One learnable scalar shrinkage intensity.** In `__init__` (add a `js_shrink: bool = False` param after `filter_kernel=50` at L317; thread `js_shrink=args.js_shrink` and `item_freq=item_freq` through the constructor call at L1279–1298): `self.js_shrink = js_shrink`; if on, `self.shrink_c = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-8.0))`. Map to `c = softplus(self.shrink_c)` so **`c → ~0.0003` at init ⇒ λ≈0 ⇒ exact no-op**.
  3. **Apply at the embedding-construction site only.** In `item_features` (L575–593) the prototype reconstruction is already computed in the loop at L588–592. Refactor so `m = Σ_l a_l @ self.proto_emb[l].weight` is accumulated *first*, then shrink the id component toward `m` **before** adding the additive text and TAPE terms:
    ```
    e_id = self.item_emb(item_ids)                          # (..., d) — the per-item MLE
    m = sum_over_levels( proto_assign_l[item_ids] @ proto_emb[l].weight )   # (..., d) — semantic-cluster mean
    if self.js_shrink:
        c   = F.softplus(self.shrink_c)
        lam = (c / (c + self.item_freq[item_ids])).unsqueeze(-1)   # (...,1); rare item -> lam->1
        e_id = (1.0 - lam) * e_id + lam * m                  # Efron–Morris posterior mean
    e = e_id + (self.sbert_proj(s) if use_sbert else 0) + m  # TAPE term m stays additive, unchanged
    ```
    At `c≈0 ⇒ lam≈0 ⇒ e_id = item_emb`, so `e = item_emb + sbert + m` — **bit-identical to the current code**. As `c` grows, rare items (small `n_i`) get `lam→1` (id-vector replaced by its semantic-cluster mean `m`); head items (large `n_i`) keep their own vector. This is one elementwise op; it applies identically in `encode` (B,L) and in `all_item_features` (n_items,) scoring — a **representation regularizer, NOT a logit/popularity injection**, so it does **not** court the eval-time popularity trap the supervisor flagged for XF-2 (it changes *which* embedding each item gets, not a popularity-shaped score offset). The learned `c` is an interpretable readout of how much tail capacity was over-fit.

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --js-shrink --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_X1_jsshrink_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_X1_jsshrink_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **current CONFIRMED best config V2** (winning stack + `--label-smoothing 0.2` + `--causal-filter --filter-kernel 8`; 5-seed baseline **0.0674 ± 0.0003**, SOTA_VERDICT.md). James–Stein acts on the **embedding/representation axis**, orthogonal to LS (loss target) and the causal filter (sequence spectrum), so it should stack cleanly — the gate is to beat the 0.0674 ± 0.0003 band. If the executor prefers clean JS×encoder isolation, a cheap follow-up dropping `--causal-filter`/`--label-smoothing 0.2` (baseline U2 0.0649 / H2 0.0639) is permitted, but the headline-shot is on V2.

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog n_eval≈94,762; ep2 test in the ~0.041–0.044 band matching the V2 trajectory) → full 40ep; single-seed best-by-val test NDCG@10 **> 0.0680** (clear of the V2 0.0674 ± 0.0003 band) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed (seeds 20260608–12); 5-seed mean **> 0.0760** ⇒ executor enters strict code+paper review before any claim. Also report the learned `c = softplus(shrink_c)` and a **tail-sliced** NDCG (items below the catalog-median frequency) regardless of the headline delta — that is the mechanism's direct interpretable readout.

- **Kill criterion:** best-by-val test ≤ **0.0674** (ties/inside the confirmed V2 band) ⇒ DEAD — adaptive tail shrinkage toward the semantic mean transfers no test signal beyond what TAPE+LS+filter already capture. ALSO DEAD if `c` learns ≈0 (`λ_max = c/(c+min_freq) ≪ 0.05`, i.e. the model voted the shrinkage off — a clean, interpretable negative). ALSO DEAD if it reproduces the campaign's val≫test signature (val rises toward/past 0.076 while test holds ≤ 0.0674). If `c` learns a clear positive but the *headline* is flat while **tail-sliced NDCG improves**, that is a reportable diagnostic (shrinkage helps the tail but the full-catalog metric is head-dominated), not a DEAD — record it and a fixed-`c` sweep `{0.5, 1, 2, 5}·median(n_i)` is permitted before final verdict.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — this is the **highest-prior new lever** because it is the only proposal in the *proven-to-transfer class*: the campaign's two confirmed gains (LS, causal filter) are both anti-overconfidence / capacity-restricting regularizers, while **every** capacity-adding probe inflated val and left test flat (the analyst's central diagnosis). James–Stein is capacity-*reducing* and sparsity-*adaptive* — it attacks the diagnosed long-tail-sparsity + val≫test-overfit mechanism head-on, with a 1950s mathematical guarantee (total-risk reduction for d ≥ 3) rather than a heuristic. Realistic expected delta: **−1% to +3%** (0.0674 → ~0.066–0.069); most probable is a small positive or tail-only effect, and it is **unlikely to reach 0.0760** (that residual is the hardware-blocked HSTU-kernel numerics). Two honest headwinds: (1) full-catalog NDCG is head-dominated, so a tail-discrimination gain may not surface in the headline (hence the mandatory tail-sliced readout); (2) over-shrinking could erase rare items' distinguishing signal — guarded by `c` being learnable toward 0 (clean negative). Cheap (~7 min/40ep, **+1 scalar parameter + 1 buffer**, one elementwise op), leak-free (train-only `n_i`; representation-side, eval scoring untouched), cleanly attributed (Stein/James/Efron–Morris + the frequency-weight-decay CS analogues cited and differentiated). If it lifts test past 0.0680 it is the campaign's third converged lever and a clean, publishable empirical-Bayes contribution; if `c→0` or tail-only, the learned `c` and tail slice are an interpretable finding either way.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-16-4 — Heat-kernel (manifold-diffusion) label smoothing: geometry-aware soft target over the frozen text manifold  [status: DEAD — single-seed best-by-val test 0.06653 (seed 20260608, full-catalog n_eval=94,762), ≤ the confirmed V2 5-seed band 0.0674±0.0003 ⇒ kill criterion met, no 5-seed. KEY READOUT: learned diffusion temperature T = softplus(heat_temp) = **0.00061**, DROPPED BELOW its 0.0025 init ⇒ the model drove the soft target back to a near-pure delta — it VOTED THE TEXT-MANIFOLD GEOMETRY OFF (the T→0 DEAD clause; direct analog of X1's c≈0 and W1's β=−7.42). Geometry-shaped smoothing mass transfers no test signal beyond the uniform LS floor already in the V2 stack. Implementation verified leak-free (frozen top-20 text neighbors, mean top-1 cosine 0.823; training-only, eval untouched) and a confirmed init no-op (2ep smoke matched V2 trajectory). **3rd CONSECUTIVE voted-off lever on V2 (W1 β<0, X1 c≈0, Y1 T→0) ⇒ the "stack more regularizers on V2" axis hits the ≥3-iteration KILL threshold ⇒ pivot fully to paper-writing.** The V2 stack (0.0674) has saturated the in-environment loss/embedding/target signal axes; the residual to 0.0760 is the hardware-blocked HSTU-kernel numerics. 2026-06-16]

- **Idea (novel):** Add a **default-OFF, learnable-temperature** soft training target that replaces cross-entropy's Dirac delta on the single held-out item `y` with a **heat-kernel / Gibbs distribution diffused along the frozen text (SBERT) manifold** — mass spreads from `y` to its top-`m` semantic neighbors with weights `q_{yj} = softmax(s_{yj}/T)`, `T = softplus(heat_temp)` a single learnable **diffusion temperature**. Grounds cross-field idea **XF-2026-06-16-5** (physics: heat equation / Gibbs measure on a manifold). This is the **geometry-aware generalization of the campaign's ONE confirmed loss-side lever** — uniform label-smoothing (U-series, +0.0013 confirmed) is exactly the `T→∞`, geometry-blind limit of this (uniform spread over all items); heat-kernel LS makes the smoothing mass **text-manifold-shaped** (concentrated on the near-duplicate franchise/sequel/re-skin clusters the analyst flagged) instead of flat. The model gets partial credit for ranking a semantically-near item second under leave-last-out, attacking the structural val≫test over-confidence along the *geometry* axis the uniform floor cannot see. Because uniform LS already *works*, reshaping its mass to follow semantics has a real mechanistic prior — strictly better than the two just-failed levers (W1 anti-popularity, X1 shrinkage), which were *new* mechanisms the model voted off (β=−7.42, c≈0).

- **Source(s):** Heat kernel / diffusion on manifolds — the diffusion equation `∂u/∂t=Δu`, Gibbs/Boltzmann measure `q_j∝exp(−E_j/T)`. Graph heat-kernel feature smoothing — GraphHeat, Xu et al. (arXiv:2007.16002). **Honest CS prior art for similarity-shaped soft targets (cited, NOT claimed as ours, mechanistically distinct — see below):** *Discrimination-Aware Label Smoothing* (DALS) and *similarity-prototype* soft labels (image/text classification, fixed small label sets, soft labels from model predictions); *Structure-Aware Label Smoothing for GNNs* (SALS, arXiv:2112.00499; neighborhood-adaptive soft targets for **node classification**); *Low-rank Adaptive Label Smoothing* (LORAS, structured prediction). **Closest in-domain:** *Learning Recommender Systems with Soft Target: A Decoupled Perspective* (arXiv:2410.06536) — soft labels for SASRec/GRU4Rec via a **label-propagation algorithm over collaborative (interaction-graph) neighbors** with a decoupled target-confidence/non-target-interest loss. Nearest neighbor; MUST be cited. XF-5 differs on three axes (below).

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probe = **`--label-smoothing` (U-series, the confirmed best loss lever)**. LS adds a *uniform* `eps/K` floor over **all** items at the target — geometry-blind, the `T→∞` limit. This concentrates a **finite-temperature, manifold-shaped** soft mass on `y`'s text-semantic neighbors only. Distinct from **text-sim-bias** and **TAPE** (those act on *attention/representation*; this acts purely on the *CE target distribution*). Distinct from **text-distillation (c2, DEAD)** (that aligned the *hidden state* to the target's *text vector*; this reshapes the *target label distribution* over the *catalog*). Vs the in-domain prior art **arXiv:2410.06536**: (1) its soft labels come from **collaborative label propagation over the interaction graph**; ours from a **heat kernel over the frozen TEXT (SBERT) manifold** (semantic, not behavioral neighbors); (2) it has **no learnable diffusion temperature**; ours learns `T` (the effective diffusion radius is the interpretable readout); (3) it is a from-scratch loss redesign on plain SASRec, ours is a **minimal, ablatable, default-OFF soft-target stacked on the confirmed 0.0674 HSTU+TAPE+filter stack**. Vs **SALS/DALS**: classification/GNN-node tasks, dense K×K similarity over small fixed label sets; this is a **sparse top-m heat target at 25k-catalog sequential-rec scale**. New axis: the *shape of the supervision target, set by frozen-text manifold diffusion at catalog scale with a learned temperature*.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag **`--heat-target` (action='store_true', default OFF ⇒ bit-identical no-op: path skipped)**, with **`--heat-neighbors` (int, default 20 = m)** and a learnable temperature. Requires text embeddings (winning stack always has SBERT); assert `sbert_emb is not None` (mirror the `--text-prototypes` guard at L1350-1352).
  1. **Precompute frozen, leak-free neighbor buffers (in `main()`, ONCE at load).** The normalized frozen text matrix `x = F.normalize(torch.from_numpy(sbert_emb))` is already built for TAPE (L1353-1354) — reuse it. Compute top-`m` cosine neighbors per item by **chunked matmul** (avoid the 25k² all-pairs tensor): for `i` in chunks of ~2048 rows, `sims = x[i_chunk] @ x.T` (chunk × n_items), set self-sim to `−inf`, take `topk(m)` → accumulate `nbr_idx (n_items, m)` long and `nbr_sim (n_items, m)` float. One-time O(n²·d) on GPU (~seconds; n≈25k). **Leak-free by construction:** frozen text ONLY — no interactions, no val/test rows. Pass both into `SASRecSBERT.__init__`; `self.register_buffer("nbr_idx", nbr_idx, persistent=False)`, `self.register_buffer("nbr_sim", nbr_sim, persistent=False)`; `self.use_heat = True`; `self.heat_temp = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(-6.0))` (⇒ `T=softplus(-6)≈0.0025`, near-zero ⇒ delta target at init). Add `--heat-target`/`--heat-neighbors` to argparse; thread `heat_target=args.heat_target` through the constructor (next to `js_shrink` at L1412) and through `train_one_epoch(...)` (L1469) → `chunked_full_softmax_loss(...)`.
  2. **Build the heat/Gibbs soft target (in `chunked_full_softmax_loss`, after the chunk loop, where `target_scores`/`lse` are ready, ~L807).** For the valid positions `pos_ids (n_valid,)`:
    ```
    if heat_target and getattr(model, "use_heat", False):
        T   = F.softplus(model.heat_temp)
        nbr = model.nbr_idx[pos_ids]                      # (n_valid, m)
        sim = model.nbr_sim[pos_ids]                      # (n_valid, m)  cosine, <1
        cand_ids  = torch.cat([pos_ids.unsqueeze(1), nbr], dim=1)         # (n_valid, 1+m)
        cand_sim  = torch.cat([torch.ones_like(sim[:, :1]), sim], dim=1)  # self-sim = 1.0 (the max)
        q = torch.softmax(cand_sim / T, dim=1)            # heat/Gibbs weights; T→0 ⇒ one-hot on self
        # optional guard: floor self-mass q[:,0] ≥ 0.5 then renormalize (prevents over-diffusion)
        cand_emb = model.item_features(cand_ids.reshape(-1)).reshape(n_valid, 1 + m, -1)
        if cosine: cand_emb = F.normalize(cand_emb, dim=-1, eps=1e-6)
        cand_score = (h_v.unsqueeze(1) * cand_emb).sum(-1)               # (n_valid, 1+m)
        if cosine: cand_score = cand_score / temp
        soft_num = (q * cand_score).sum(dim=1)            # (n_valid,) = Σ_j q_yj·score_j
        target_scores = soft_num                          # replace the hard target numerator
    ```
    The existing CE branches (L819-828) are unchanged — they consume `target_scores`. **No-op proof:** at init `T≈0.0025`; self-sim 1.0 vs neighbor sims ≤~0.9 ⇒ `(s_j−1)/T ≤ −40` ⇒ `q≈[1,0,…]` to float precision ⇒ `soft_num ≈ score_y` = current hard target; flag OFF ⇒ block skipped ⇒ **bit-identical**. As `T` climbs, mass diffuses onto semantic neighbors. **Stacks orthogonally with LS0.2:** the LS branch becomes `loss=(lse−(1−eps)·soft_num−eps·mean_score).mean()` — uniform floor (eps) × geometry-shaped main mass (heat); at `T→0`, `soft_num=score_y` ⇒ exact LS0.2. **Training-ONLY: eval scoring (`all_item_features`/L1016) is untouched** ⇒ zero eval-time manipulation, no popularity/leakage trap. Cost: `(m+1)≈21` extra `item_features` lookups per valid position — negligible vs the 25k-item chunked softmax (~7 min/40ep holds).

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --heat-target --heat-neighbors 20 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_Y1_heattarget_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_Y1_heattarget_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **CONFIRMED best config V2** (winning stack + LS0.2 + causal-filter k8; 5-seed **0.0674 ± 0.0003**). Heat-target acts on the **loss-target axis** (geometry-shaped), orthogonal to the filter (embedding spectrum) and to the uniform LS floor it co-exists with. Report the learned `T = softplus(heat_temp)` (effective diffusion radius) regardless of the test delta. (Optional clean isolation: a follow-up on U2/LS0.2 — drop `--causal-filter` — to attribute *geometry-vs-uniform* smoothing directly; headline-shot is on V2.)

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog n_eval≈94,762; ep2 test in the ~0.041–0.044 band matching the V2 trajectory) → full 40ep; single-seed best-by-val test NDCG@10 **> 0.0680** (clear of the V2 0.0674 ± 0.0003 band) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed (seeds 20260608–12); 5-seed mean **> 0.0760** ⇒ executor enters strict code+paper review before any claim. **Apply the analyst's EARLY gap go/no-go (ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-2 #5):** at ep10–20 read val−test; a genuine target-softening lever lifts test with the ~0.0088 gap stable-or-shrinking; if the gap widens past ~0.011 while test is flat, it is the c3 over-diffusion pattern → KILL early without burning 40ep×5seeds.

- **Kill criterion:** best-by-val test ≤ **0.0674** (ties/inside the confirmed V2 band) ⇒ DEAD — geometry-aware smoothing transfers no test signal beyond the uniform LS floor already in the stack. ALSO DEAD if `T` learns →0 (`softplus(heat_temp)` ≈ its init ⇒ `q` collapses to the delta ⇒ the model voted the geometry off — the clean negative, the direct analog of X1's `c≈0` and W1's `β` voting-off). ALSO DEAD if it reproduces the val≫test signature (val rises toward/past 0.076 while test holds ≤ 0.0674) — over-diffusion diluting the true-item signal. If `T` learns clearly positive but the *headline* is flat, report the learned `T` and a neighbor-overlap diagnostic (does diffused mass land on franchise/sequel clusters?) as an honest finding before final verdict.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — a **high-information probe on the proven-to-transfer (anti-over-confidence) axis**, with the **best mechanistic prior among unrun levers**: it is the geometry-aware generalization of LS, the ONE confirmed loss lever, so unlike W1/X1 (new mechanisms, both voted off) it asks a sharper question — *does making the confirmed uniform smoothing mass semantic-neighbor-shaped convert MORE of the val≫test gap?* Realistic expected delta: **−1% to +3%** (0.0674 → ~0.066–0.069); most-probable a small positive or a clean `T→0` negative; **unlikely to reach 0.0760** (that residual is the hardware-blocked HSTU-kernel numerics). **HONEST headwind the executor must weigh:** the last two queued levers (W1, X1) both landed in-band with the model voting them off — evidence the V2 stack may have *saturated the loss/embedding signal axes*. Per the supervisor's standing guard, if this also lands in the 0.0674±0.0003 band it is the **3rd consecutive in-band lever ⇒ the "stack more regularizers on V2" direction hits the ≥3-iteration KILL threshold and the pipeline should pivot fully to paper-writing** (the confirmed 0.0674 + TAPE + causal-filter + MI generalization + the honest negative-result map is already defensible). Cheap (~7 min/40ep, **+1 scalar parameter + 2 frozen buffers**, one-time top-m precompute), leak-free (frozen text only; training-only, eval untouched), cleanly attributed (heat-kernel/Gibbs physics + DALS/SALS/LORAS + the in-domain arXiv:2410.06536 cited and differentiated). If it lifts test past 0.0680 it is the campaign's 3rd converged lever and a clean, publishable manifold-diffusion-soft-target contribution; if `T→0` or flat, the learned `T` is an interpretable negative either way.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-1 — Exposure-gated cold-item routing: damp the noisy ID channel toward the frozen-text "fitness" channel, with a NON-LEARNABLE coverage-derived gate that cannot be voted off  [status: DEAD — smoke clean (no NaN), full 40ep α=1.0 seed08 run COMPLETE. Tail-tercile NDCG@10 = 0.00121 vs TAIL_V2_text baseline 0.00494 ⇒ tail Δ = −0.00373 (−75%) — the non-learnable gate (cannot be voted off) CRASHES the tail. mid −0.00246, head +0.00128, overall +0.00019 (flat); val 0.07648, val−test gap ~0.009 (NOT exploded ⇒ real representational effect, not c3 overfit). PRIMARY gate (tail up with head held) FAILS in the WRONG direction; the α∈{0.5,0.25} sweep is sanctioned only if α=1 lifts tail but collapses head — it did the opposite ⇒ NO sweep, no 5-seed (directive: 5-seed only on a clear POSITIVE tail delta). DECISIVE FINDING: routing cold items off their sparse ID embedding ONTO the frozen-text channel destroys tail performance ⇒ the sparse ID vector is MORE useful than text even for the rarest items; the cold tail is SPARSITY-bottlenecked, not ID-noise-bottlenecked, and text cannot fix it. 3rd KILL-guard iteration; with directive #1 (5-seed tail-null) + Z1 (tail-negative) both in, the "rescue the cold tail by adding text to cold items" direction is KILLED (3/3). Mission consolidates to the HONEST NEGATIVE + the MID-regime text win (TAPE drives ~85% of the mid +6.8%). 2026-06-17]

> **MISSION-ALIGNED (LONG-TAIL/COLD-ITEM WIN).** Primary metric = **tail-tercile NDCG@10** (`by_popularity.tail`, already auto-emitted, train-frequency terciles, leak-free — `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1068-1085,1175-1191`). Overall NDCG is the **sanity check**, not the target. This is the FIRST queued lever under the tail mission. It grounds **XF 2026-06-17-1 (Bianconi–Barabási fit-get-richer)** as the routing locus AND folds in **XF 2026-06-17-2 (Good–Turing coverage)** as the non-learnable gate shape — exactly the composition the cross-field handoff recommended ("XF-8 supplies the routing, XF-9 supplies the gate it can't kill").

- **Idea (novel):** Add a **default-OFF, exact-no-op** flag that, for COLD/low-exposure items only, **damps the learned ID embedding** so the item's representation is carried by the **fully-observed frozen-text channel** (`sbert_proj(s)` + TAPE mean `m`) instead of its near-untrained ID vector. The gate is **NON-LEARNABLE** — a fixed per-item schedule keyed to the train-only frequency and locked to the eval's tail/mid tercile boundary — so, unlike the campaign's three consecutive dead levers (W1 β→−7.42, X1 c→0.0044, Y1 T→0.0006, each a single learnable scalar the model drove to ≈0), **there is no free scalar to drive to zero**: the routing is forced on at a fixed strength and its tail effect is measured directly. Mechanistically this is the Bianconi–Barabási "fit-get-richer" decomposition — an item's ID vector is its *degree* (popularity-accrued, near-noise for a cold latecomer); its frozen text projection is its *intrinsic fitness* (fully informative at zero exposure) — and the gate routes representation from degree to fitness exactly where degree carries no signal.

- **Source(s):** Bianconi–Barabási fitness model — Bianconi & Barabási 2001, "Competition and multiscaling in evolving networks" (EPL 54:436; degree-AND-fitness attachment `Π_i ∝ η_i k_i`, fit-latecomers overtake old hubs). Coverage-derived reliability — Good 1953 (Good–Turing) / Chao 1984 (Chao1): a frequency estimate's reliability is itself estimable from the singleton/doubleton spectrum, giving a **data-derived, non-tunable** discount schedule. **Honest CS prior art (cited, NOT claimed as ours, and differentiated below):** **Cross Decoupling Network (CDN)** — Zhang et al., "Empowering Long-tail Item Recommendation through Cross Decoupling Network," KDD 2023 (arXiv:2210.14309) — a **learnable** frequency-gate mixing memorization (ID) and generalization (feature) embeddings; **"Let It Go? Not Quite"** — RecSys 2025 (arXiv:2507.19473) — a **trainable bounded delta** added to frozen content embeddings for item cold-start in sequential rec; gating-fusion lineage **DIFF** (arXiv:2505.13974) and time-aware side-info fusion (arXiv:2512.24246), all **learned** gates.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probe = **X1 `--js-shrink` (DEAD, c→0.0044, voted off, ANALYSIS 2026-06-16-4)**. X1 shrank the ID vector toward the **learnable TAPE prototype mean `m`** with a **learnable** intensity `c/(c+n_i)`. It died for two compounding reasons this proposal fixes by design: (1) **its backoff target `m` is itself under-trained for cold semantic clusters** (rare items give the prototypes few gradients), so X1 shrank a noisy estimate toward another noisy estimate → nothing to gain; **this routes toward the FROZEN `sbert_proj(s)`, which is fully informative at zero exposure** — the target is reliable exactly where the ID is not. (2) **X1's `c` was a single learnable scalar the model nulled**; this gate is **non-learnable** (fixed strength `α`, fixed slope, `τ` locked to the catalog's tail/mid tercile boundary) → **cannot collapse**. It was also judged on *overall* NDCG (head-dominated, any tail gain washed out); **this is judged on tail-tercile NDCG**, the regime it targets. Vs **CDN (the key prior art):** CDN learns a frequency-gate MLP between two *learned* branches; this is a *non-learnable, coverage-derived* schedule routing to a *frozen, reliable* channel, a minimal ablatable single-channel add to a confirmed HSTU+TAPE+filter stack — and is uniquely motivated by/engineered against *this campaign's documented 3-voted-off-scalar failure mode*, a novelty no prior work shares. Vs always-on additive SBERT (`item_features` L659-661, which *adds* text to every item): this **removes the unreliable ID channel for cold items only**, so the reliable text signal already present is no longer drowned by ID noise — the precise, honest marginal claim (it does not add text; it stops cold ID noise from corrupting it).

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flags **`--fitness-gate` (action='store_true', default OFF)** and **`--fitness-gate-alpha` (float, default 0.0 ⇒ exact no-op)**; `--fitness-gate-slope` (float, default 4.0). Requires text (assert `sbert_emb is not None`; the gate's reliable target is the frozen text channel). Mirror the leak-free `item_freq`/threading plumbing already built for X1.
  1. **Train-only frequency + tercile boundary `τ` (leakage-critical, ONE-time at load).** In `main()`, build `item_freq` from **TRAIN interactions only** exactly as js-shrink does (`run_sasrec_sbert.py:1472-1479`: `bincount` over `train_inters` item ids; `valid_inters`/`test_inters` never consulted) — also fire this block when `args.fitness_gate`. Compute the **tail/mid tercile boundary frequency** the SAME way the eval bucketer does (L1073-1083: rank items by train freq, the tail third = rarest) and set `τ = log1p(freq_at_rank_⌈n_items/3⌉)`. (Optionally, the Good–Turing/Chao1 reliability `ρ_i` from the frequency-of-frequencies `N_r` can replace the sigmoid shape — `g_i = 1 − α·(1−ρ_i)` — as a noted theory-pure variant; the tercile-sigmoid below is the cleaner, mission-aligned first probe and is itself data-derived since `τ` is the catalog's empirical tail boundary.) Pass `item_freq`, `tau`, `alpha`, `slope` into `SASRecSBERT.__init__` and `register_buffer("item_freq", …, persistent=False)`; store `tau/alpha/slope` as plain floats (NOT `nn.Parameter` — non-learnable by design).
  2. **Apply at the embedding-construction site only** — `item_features` (L645-664), immediately after the ID vector `e = self.item_emb(item_ids)` (L645) and the TAPE mean `m` are available, BEFORE the additive `sbert_proj`/`m` terms (L659-663):
    ```
    e = self.item_emb(item_ids)                               # (...,d) noisy ID MLE = "degree"
    # ... m (TAPE prototype mean) computed at L648-652 ...
    if getattr(self, "fitness_gate", False) and self.fg_alpha > 0.0:
        lf = torch.log1p(self.item_freq[item_ids])            # train-only log-frequency
        g  = 1.0 - self.fg_alpha * torch.sigmoid(-self.fg_slope * (lf - self.fg_tau))
        e  = g.unsqueeze(-1) * e                              # damp ID for cold (lf<<tau ⇒ g→1−α); head (lf>>tau ⇒ g→1)
    if self.use_sbert:
        e = e + self.sbert_proj(s)                            # frozen-text "fitness" — FULL strength always
    if m is not None:
        e = e + m                                             # additive TAPE term — unchanged
    return e
    ```
    **No-op proof:** `--fitness-gate-alpha 0.0` (default) ⇒ block skipped ⇒ bit-identical; flag absent ⇒ skipped. With `α>0`: head items (`lf ≫ τ`) get `g→1` (full ID retained); cold items (`lf ≪ τ`) get `g→1−α` (ID damped, representation carried by the frozen `sbert_proj(s)+m`). One elementwise op; applies **identically** in `encode` (B,L) and `all_item_features` (n_items,) scoring — a **representation-side** change (which embedding each item gets), **NOT a popularity-logit injection**, so it does **not** court the eval-time popularity-inflation trap the supervisor flagged for XF-2/PD-style score offsets. Leak-free (`item_freq` train-only; `τ` from train terciles). `by_popularity` is already emitted automatically — no eval change needed.

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --fitness-gate --fitness-gate-alpha 1.0 --fitness-gate-slope 4.0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_Z1_fitnessgate_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_Z1_fitnessgate_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **confirmed V2 winning stack** so the gate is the ONLY added flag vs the mission's `TAIL_V2_text` baseline (supervisor MISSION run #1) ⇒ clean tail-attribution. `α=1.0` fully removes the ID channel for the coldest items (the strongest test of the thesis); if `α=1.0` *hurts head/overall*, sweep `--fitness-gate-alpha {0.5, 0.25}` (partial damping) — non-learnable, so this is a deliberate hyperparameter sweep, not a model-nulled scalar. (Clean isolation variant: drop `--causal-filter`/`--label-smoothing 0.2` to gate on the U2/H2 base; headline-shot is on V2.)

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog `n_eval≈94,762`; `by_popularity` printed) → full 40ep. **PRIMARY (tail):** `tail-NDCG(fitness-gate) − tail-NDCG(TAIL_V2_text)` > 0 with **head-NDCG roughly preserved** (≥ baseline − ~5%) ⇒ the mission thesis holds (text routing rescues cold items) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed and per-α sweep. **Apply the analyst's ep10–20 gap go/no-go** (val−test): a genuine tail lever should not widen the structural ~0.0088 gap; if the gap explodes (c3 pattern) kill early. **SANITY (overall):** report overall NDCG; a within-band overall (0.0674±0.0003) with a positive tail delta is the *intended* win shape (tail up, head held). Overall >0.0760 ⇒ strict review (not expected).

- **Kill criterion:** **DEAD** if `tail-NDCG(gate) ≤ tail-NDCG(TAIL_V2_text)` across seeds at every swept `α` ⇒ damping the cold ID channel toward frozen text transfers no tail signal beyond the always-on additive SBERT (i.e., the cold ID vectors were already near-inert and removing them changes nothing — itself a clean, honest mission finding: "ID noise is not what's holding back the tail; the text channel is already doing all it can"). **ALSO DEAD** if it lifts tail but **collapses head/overall** (>~5% head drop) AND no smaller `α` recovers a net-positive tail-without-head-cost trade — that is the head/tail tradeoff, reportable but not a win. Because the gate is non-learnable there is no "scalar voted off" outcome — the result is a *direct, un-nullable measurement* of whether exposure-routing helps the tail, which is exactly the diagnostic the three dead learnable-scalar levers could not deliver.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — the **first lever purpose-built for the tail mission**, grounded in the literal mission thesis (text rescues cold items) and engineered against the campaign's two documented failure modes at once: it routes to a **reliable** target (frozen text, unlike X1's under-trained TAPE mean) via a **non-collapsible** gate (fixed schedule, unlike W1/X1/Y1's nulled scalars). Realistic expected delta on **tail NDCG: 0 to +10% relative** (the tail is a small-`n` bucket with high variance and large headroom — cold items currently ranked by noisy ID vectors have the most to gain); **overall NDCG: −2% to +1%** (head untouched by design, so the headline should hold near 0.0674 — a tail gain that does NOT cost the head is the publishable shape). **Two honest headwinds:** (1) V2 *already* adds `sbert_proj(s)+m` to every item, so cold items already receive text signal — the gate's marginal value is only in *removing ID noise*, which may be small if cold ID vectors are already near-zero from rare updates (the `α=1.0` full-removal probe maximizes the chance of detecting any effect); (2) full-catalog tail-NDCG is noisy (small bucket), so a 5-seed confirmation is mandatory before any "tail-SOTA" claim. Cheap (~7 min/40ep, **0 learnable params added** — pure non-learnable gate + 1 buffer, one elementwise op), leak-free (train-only freq + tercile `τ`; representation-side, eval scoring untouched), cleanly attributed (Bianconi–Barabási fitness + Good–Turing/Chao1 coverage, with CDN/`"Let It Go?"`/gating-fusion CS prior art cited and differentiated). A clean positive on the tail with the head held is a genuine, publishable "state-of-the-art on the long tail via exposure-gated text routing" contribution; a null is the honest finding that the text stack's additive features already exhaust the tail signal.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-2 — Parameter-free RMT spectral shrinkage of the item-embedding matrix (Gavish–Donoho / Marchenko–Pastur): the embedding-space analogue of the confirmed causal temporal filter, with a built-in BBP detectability diagnostic  [status: DEAD — implemented `--spectral-shrink`, smoke clean. Both hard-threshold variants HURT all terciles (seed08, vs V2 0.06731/tail0.00494/mid0.01742): every-1 → 0.06454 (mid 0.00963, −45%), every-5 → 0.06400 (mid 0.01031, −41%); rank kept 22–24/64. NON-collapsible (parameter-free) ⇒ direct, un-nullable measurement: the embedding-spectrum-shrinkage axis carries NO in-environment gain; the GD/MP hard projection ERASES real signal directions (mid-regime worst). This REFUTES the proposal's own "table already low-rank/noise-dominated, projection near-idempotent" hypothesis — the model productively uses its full 64-dim rank; forcing it low-rank harms. Confirms the cycle-3 V2-regularizer KILL guard was correct. RETAINED DELIVERABLE (directive c): the per-tercile BBP detectability ρ (ℓ=2 tail0.0/mid0.0/head0.073; ℓ=8 0.21/0.43/0.75) + low effective rank = a paper-hardening "tail is irreducibly sparsity-bottlenecked" figure regardless of the (negative) test delta. KILL-guard tension flagged for supervisor cycle-4 ruling. 2026-06-17]

> **Aligned to SUPERVISOR FEEDBACK 2026-06-17 cycle 3 — RESEARCHERS RETARGET.** Targets directive **(b)** (a genuinely **non-text, training-only, representation-side** lever; eval scoring untouched — NOT an eval-time popularity/exposure correction) and directive **(c)** (it ships a **data-derived irreducibility diagnostic** — the per-tercile BBP detectability fraction + the embedding matrix's effective rank — that hardens the honest negative regardless of the test delta). **NON-COLLAPSIBLE by construction:** the threshold is the parameter-free Gavish–Donoho/MP edge derived from the singular-value spectrum and the n×d aspect ratio — **there is NO learnable scalar to drive to zero**, the direct antidote to the three voted-off levers (W1 β=−7.42, X1 c≈0.004, Y1 T→0.0006). Primary metric = **overall + MID-tercile NDCG@10** (directive (a)); tail/head reported for the diagnostic. Grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-17-4 (RMT / BBP detectability transition)** — specifically its "ambitious variant" (Gavish–Donoho optimal singular-value shrinkage of the ID-embedding spectrum), which the cross-field author flagged as the higher-novelty follow-up.

- **Idea (novel):** Once per epoch, **project the learned item-embedding matrix onto its denoised low-rank component** using the **parameter-free Gavish–Donoho optimal singular-value threshold** (the AMSE-optimal hard threshold 4/√3·√n·σ for known noise, or 2.858·y_median for unknown noise, with the aspect-ratio-dependent coefficient for the non-square n_items×d table). Singular directions below the Marchenko–Pastur noise edge are set to zero (hard mode) or optimally shrunk (optimal mode); directions above it — the genuinely estimable "taste" components — are retained. This is the **item-spectrum (representation) dual of the campaign's single confirmed novel win, the causal *temporal*-spectrum filter** (`--causal-filter`, +0.0015 5-seed): the filter restricts the *time*-axis spectrum of the sequence embeddings; this restricts the *item*-axis spectrum of the embedding table. Motivated by the **BBP detectability transition** (XF-4): an item's ID vector estimated from `n_i` interactions in `d=64` dims is reliable only above the SNR/aspect-ratio threshold; the long-tail makes most low-singular-value directions pure noise (the documented "representation-degeneration / fast singular-value decay" of the item table), and zeroing them removes overfit-to-noise capacity in exactly the proven-to-transfer (capacity-restricting) class.

- **Source(s):** Gavish & Donoho 2014, "The Optimal Hard Threshold for Singular Values is 4/√3," IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 60(8):5040–5053 (arXiv:1305.5870) — the parameter-free AMSE-optimal threshold (2.858·median for unknown σ; aspect-ratio coefficient ω(β), β=min/max dim). Marchenko–Pastur 1967 noise edge; Baik–Ben Arous–Péché (BBP) spiked-model detectability transition (arXiv:2301.05331). **Honest CS prior art (cited, NOT ours, differentiated below):** "Cleaned Similarity for Better Memory-Based Recommenders" (arXiv:1905.07370, MP-cleaning of *similarity* matrices in *memory-based* recommenders); "Balancing Embedding Spectrum for Recommendation" (arXiv:2406.12032, a *learnable* regularizer toward a target spectrum); representation-degeneration fixes "DuoRec" (arXiv:2110.05730) and arXiv:2306.11986 (*contrastive*, not spectral-projection); "Plug-In Diffusion Model for Embedding Denoising" (SIGIR 2024, a *learnable diffusion* denoiser); "Dynamic Low-Rank Training with Spectral Regularization" (ICML 2025, model *compression*).

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probes are the **confirmed `--causal-filter`** and the DEAD **X1 `--js-shrink`**. (1) vs **causal-filter**: the filter is a per-channel FIR over the *temporal* axis of the sequence; this is an SVD-thresholding over the *item×dim* axis of the embedding table — a different spectrum, a different locus, but the same "restricted-spectrum inductive bias" class that *worked*. (2) vs **X1 (DEAD, c→0.004 voted off)**: X1 was a *learnable*-intensity per-item convex shrinkage toward the *learnable, under-trained* TAPE prototype mean — it died because (a) the model nulled the scalar and (b) the backoff target was itself noisy. This has **no learnable parameter at all** (the GD/MP threshold is data-derived) so it **cannot be voted off**, and it shrinks toward the *denoised global signal subspace* (the matrix's own reliable singular directions), not a noisy per-item target. (3) vs **Z1 (DEAD, KILLED 3/3)**: Z1 routed cold items OFF their ID vector ONTO frozen text — at the deep tail that is bit-identical to removing ID, which **crashed the tail −75%**. This proposal **never removes any item's ID channel toward text** (it denoises the *shared* spectrum, retaining every item's projection onto the reliable subspace) — it is **not** the killed cold-text-routing direction. vs the prior art: unlike arXiv:2406.12032 it is **parameter-free** (GD/MP optimal threshold, not a learned target spectrum); unlike arXiv:1905.07370 it cleans the **learned embedding table of a sequential model**, not a memory-based similarity matrix; unlike the diffusion/contrastive denoisers it is a closed-form per-epoch projection with zero added learnable capacity.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag **`--spectral-shrink` (action='store_true', default OFF ⇒ exact no-op — block skipped, weights untouched, bit-identical)**, with fixed (non-learnable) hyperparameters **`--spectral-shrink-every K` (int, default 1 = every epoch)** and **`--spectral-shrink-mode {hard,optimal}` (default `hard`)**. No `nn.Parameter`, no zero-init scalar — the threshold is derived from data.
  1. **Per-epoch projection (in the epoch loop, AFTER `train_one_epoch` and BEFORE the eval block, i.e. between `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1666` and `:1678`), guarded by `if args.spectral_shrink and (epoch % args.spectral_shrink_every == 0):` and wrapped in `with torch.no_grad():`** — operate on the real-item rows of the table (exclude the `padding_idx` row):
     ```
     W = model.item_emb.weight.data[:n_items].float()          # (n_items, d), d=64
     U, S, Vh = torch.linalg.svd(W, full_matrices=False)        # thin SVD, ~ms for 25k x 64
     n, d = W.shape                                             # n=n_items, d=d_model
     beta = float(min(n, d)) / float(max(n, d))                 # aspect ratio (here ~64/25000)
     omega = 0.56*beta**3 - 0.95*beta**2 + 1.82*beta + 1.43     # Gavish-Donoho coef (unknown noise)
     tau = omega * torch.median(S)                              # 2.858*median at beta->0; data-derived
     if args.spectral_shrink_mode == "hard":
         S_shrunk = torch.where(S >= tau, S, torch.zeros_like(S))
     else:  # "optimal" GD shrinker (unknown-noise form via median-calibrated sigma)
         sigma = torch.median(S) / (math.sqrt(max(n, d)) * mu_beta(beta))   # MP median calibration
         y = S / (sigma * math.sqrt(max(n, d)))
         eta = torch.sqrt(torch.clamp((y**2 - beta - 1)**2 - 4*beta, min=0)) / y
         S_shrunk = torch.where(y > 1 + math.sqrt(beta), sigma*math.sqrt(max(n,d))*eta, torch.zeros_like(S))
     W_dn = (U * S_shrunk.unsqueeze(0)) @ Vh
     model.item_emb.weight.data[:n_items] = W_dn.to(model.item_emb.weight.dtype)
     model.item_emb.weight.data[pad_id].zero_()                # keep the padding row at 0
     ```
     (`mu_beta` = median of the Marchenko–Pastur distribution for aspect β; standard, tabulated/closed-form — start with `mode=hard`, which needs only `omega`.) The cleaned table is then used **identically** in the next training epoch and in eval (`all_item_features` / scoring) — a **representation-side** change like the causal filter, **NOT** an eval-time popularity/exposure logit ⇒ it does **not** court the eval-manipulation trap the supervisor flagged for XF-2. No-op proof: flag OFF ⇒ block never runs ⇒ `item_emb.weight` unchanged ⇒ bit-identical. (Adam moment staleness for one step after an out-of-band weight edit is benign and standard for projected methods; `--spectral-shrink-every 5` reduces it further if needed.)
  2. **Directive-(c) diagnostic (logged when ON, ships regardless of test delta).** In the same block, log to the run JSON/stdout: (i) the **effective rank** = `#{S_i ≥ tau}` of the item table (how few of the d=64 directions are above the MP noise edge — quantifies how much embedding capacity is provably noise); (ii) the singular-value spectrum `S`. Additionally, reusing the **leak-free train-only `item_freq` buffer** (already built for X1/Z1 at `run_sasrec_sbert.py:614-616,646-649` — fire that build also when `args.spectral_shrink`), compute the **per-item BBP reliability** `ρ_i = clamp((1 − γ_i/(ℓ−1)²)/(1 + γ_i/(ℓ−1)), 0, 1)`, `γ_i = d_model/max(n_i,1)`, for fixed `ℓ∈{2,4,8}`, and log the **mean ρ_i per popularity tercile (tail/mid/head)** — i.e. *what fraction of the catalog sits below the BBP detectability threshold*. This is the paper-hardening "the tail is irreducibly sparsity-bottlenecked" figure (directive (c)) and needs no extra training.

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --spectral-shrink --spectral-shrink-every 1 --spectral-shrink-mode hard --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_GD1_spectralshrink_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_GD1_spectralshrink_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **confirmed V2 winning stack** so `--spectral-shrink` is the ONLY added flag vs the 0.0674±0.0003 baseline ⇒ clean attribution. `by_popularity` is auto-emitted (tail/mid/head). If `hard` mode over-shrinks (overall drops), the cheap follow-up is `--spectral-shrink-mode optimal` (gentler, optimal-shrinker) and/or `--spectral-shrink-every 5`; these are fixed-hyperparameter sweeps, NOT a model-nulled scalar.

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog `n_eval≈94,762`; SVD runs clean; effective-rank + ρ-tercile diagnostic printed) → full 40ep. **PRIMARY:** overall best-by-val test NDCG@10 **> 0.0680** (clear of the V2 0.0674±0.0003 band) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed (seeds 20260608–12); 5-seed mean **> 0.0760** ⇒ strict review. **SECONDARY (directive a):** report **mid-tercile** NDCG vs V2's mid 0.0174 — a clean mid lift with overall held is itself a reportable directive-(a) result. **Apply the analyst's ep10–20 val−test gap go/no-go:** a genuine restricted-bias lever lifts test with the structural ~0.0088 gap stable; if the gap explodes past ~0.011 with test flat (c3 pattern) → kill early.

- **Kill criterion:** best-by-val test ≤ **0.0674** (inside the V2 band) AND no mid-tercile lift ⇒ DEAD — the item-embedding spectrum was already effectively low-rank (dropout 0.5 + LS0.2 + the additive text/TAPE terms already discard the noise directions), so the GD/MP projection removes nothing SGD hadn't. ALSO DEAD if it **hurts** (over-shrinking erased signal directions; report the realized effective rank — if it zeroed too many directions, `optimal` mode or `every 5` is the permitted single follow-up before final verdict). **Because the threshold is parameter-free there is no "scalar voted off" escape** — the result is a *direct, un-nullable measurement* of whether the embedding-spectrum axis carries any in-environment signal, exactly the diagnostic value the three dead learnable-scalar levers could not deliver. The realized effective-rank + per-tercile ρ_i are a publishable diagnostic either way.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — a **non-collapsible, proven-class probe on a genuinely untouched axis** (the item-embedding *matrix spectrum*; every prior lever acted on the loss target, per-item embedding, time-axis spectrum, or score — none on the global SVD of the item table). It is the embedding-space dual of the campaign's ONLY confirmed novel architectural win (the causal temporal filter), and it is in the empirically proven-to-transfer (capacity-restricting / restricted-inductive-bias) class — the same class as LS and the filter, the only two converged gains, and the opposite of every voted-off / capacity-adding probe. The representation-degeneration literature (fast singular-value decay of the item table under the long-tail) says noise-dominated directions demonstrably exist to shrink. **Realistic expected delta: −1% to +2% overall** (0.0674 → ~0.066–0.069); most-probable **neutral-to-small-positive**, because dropout 0.5 + LS0.2 already regularize the spectrum heavily and SGD may already keep the effective rank low — in which case the GD projection is near-idempotent and the contribution is the **diagnostic** (effective rank + tercile-ρ irreducibility figure, directive (c)). **Unlikely to reach 0.0760** (that residual is the hardware-blocked HSTU-kernel numerics). Cheap (one thin SVD of 25k×64 per epoch ≈ ms; ~7 min/40ep), pure PyTorch (`torch.linalg.svd`), exact no-op when OFF, **0 added learnable params**, leak-free (the projection touches only the learned table; the ρ-diagnostic uses train-only freq), cleanly attributed (Gavish–Donoho/MP/BBP + the recsys spectral prior art cited and differentiated). If it lifts test past 0.0680 it is the campaign's 3rd converged lever and a clean, publishable "RMT-optimal embedding-spectrum denoising" contribution; if flat/null, the effective-rank + per-tercile detectability readout is a paper-hardening irreducibility artifact regardless.
