## PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-3 — Reliability-weighted ID×TEXT cue fusion (multisensory inverse effectiveness): a NON-learnable, two-sided, frequency × text-informativeness fusion weight aimed at the MID-density regime  [status: DEAD (executor flipped RUNNING→DEAD 2026-06-17: VG+MI runs complete, see verdict below) — flags `--cue-fusion`/`--cue-fusion-mode {sum2,sum1}`/`--cue-fusion-nbr` implemented in run_sasrec_sbert.py: NON-learnable reliability-weighted ID×text fusion in item_features() (w_id ∝ train-only freq, w_tx ∝ frozen-text top-K distinctiveness median-matched to the ID median; sum2 mass-preserving). cue_pi_id/cue_pi_text frozen buffers built in main() (train freq + chunked top-K text distinctiveness, leak-free). Default-OFF exact no-op (elif keeps the plain add) verified by inspection. py_compile OK. Ran on BOTH VG+MI (cycle-4). RESULT: VG mid −0.00136 (−8%, HURTS) single-seed; MI mid single-seed +0.00099 (+16%) DEFLATED at 5-seed to +0.00026, band [−0.00028,+0.00080] STRADDLES 0 (seed10 negative), overall −0.00048 below V2 band. **DEAD — no mid lift beyond noise on EITHER dataset** (the cycle-4 kill gate). Non-collapsible ⇒ direct measurement: the uniform additive ID+text fusion is already near-optimal; reliability-weighting the channels adds no confirmed signal (the superadditivity-null the proposal itself anticipated). Per cycle-4 this CLOSES the new-lever search ⇒ FULL PIVOT TO PAPER. The seed08 +16% MI mid was an outlier the 5-seed rule correctly caught (same deflation as VG's seed08 tail). `--cue-fusion` left in (default-OFF no-op). 2026-06-17]

> **Aligned to SUPERVISOR FEEDBACK 2026-06-17 cycle 3 — directive (a)** (mechanisms for the **MID-density regime where text demonstrably wins**, the campaign's one remaining winnable text lever: +0.00104 mid / +6.8% rel, 5-seed, ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-2) and **directive (c)** (it ships a per-band **superadditivity** curve that either confirms the inverse-effectiveness mechanism or hardens the saturation/honest-negative story). **NON-COLLAPSIBLE by construction:** the fusion weights are fixed functions of frozen data (train-only frequency + frozen-text distinctiveness) — **there is NO learnable scalar to drive to zero**, the direct antidote to the four voted-off/dead levers (W1 β=−7.42, X1 c≈0.004, Y1 T→0.0006, GD1 parameter-free dead). **Representation-side only** (applies identically in `encode` and `all_item_features`; it re-weights which embedding channel is trusted — a fusion-geometry change like dot-vs-cosine — NOT an eval-time popularity/exposure *logit*, so it does not court the eval-manipulation trap the supervisor flagged; same category as the sanctioned representation-side X1/Z1/GD1). Grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-17-6 (multisensory inverse effectiveness + Bayesian MLE cue combination)** — the cross-field author's top-priority handoff this batch.
>
> **PRE-REGISTRATION / KILL-GUARD HONESTY (read first).** The supervisor KILLED the direction *"rescue the cold tail by adding text to cold items"* (3/3 instruments). **This proposal is NOT that direction** and must not be judged as a tail lever: at the deep tail *both* channel precisions are low (a cold item with a generic title has tiny ID precision AND low text distinctiveness), so the fusion does **not** dump cold items onto full-strength text (the killed Z1 move that crashed the tail −75%) — its inverse-variance math *predicts and accepts a tail null* and concentrates re-weighting in the **mid band** where ID is just-detectable and text complements. The PRIMARY gate is therefore **MID-tercile NDCG with overall held in-band**, and a tail null is the expected, non-fatal outcome. If the supervisor judges this too close to the killed cold-text direction, the clean fallback is the *training-only* variant (Mechanism note 4) which keeps the plain additive fusion at eval.

- **Idea (novel):** Replace the model's **plain unweighted add** of the ID and frozen-text channels (`item_features`, `run_sasrec_sbert.py:686-690`: `e = item_emb(ids) + sbert_proj(s) + m`, implicitly `w_ID=w_text=1` for every item) with a **per-item, data-derived, inverse-variance (reliability-weighted) fusion** of the two channels, motivated by the multisensory **principle of inverse effectiveness (PoIE)**: weight each channel by its normalized precision (1/variance), so the statistically-optimal Gaussian cue combination *automatically* up-weights text exactly where the ID estimate is weak-but-comparable (the **mid** band) and down-weights it where ID already dominates (the **head**) or where both cues are weak (the deep **tail** → no fusion, predicted null). The **load-bearing novelty** (which no prior probe or, per the 2026-06-17 literature scan, no prior recsys work has) is a **per-item TEXT reliability** — text has always been treated as uniformly trustworthy; here a cold/generic-title item gets *low* text precision and a distinctive-title item gets *high* text precision, computed from the frozen text manifold. This first-principles re-weight *derives* the analyst's observed monotone curve (tail null → head +2.6% → **mid peak**) and prescribes the lever to amplify the mid win.

- **Source(s):** Multisensory optimal cue integration / inverse-variance MLE fusion — **Ernst & Banks 2002, "Humans integrate visual and haptic information in a statistically optimal fashion," Nature 415:429–433**; review **Computational principles and models of multisensory integration, PMC5447489**. Principle of inverse effectiveness as a power law — **"A Simple Power Law Governs Many Sensory Amplifications and Multisensory Enhancements," PMC5955996** (compressive-exponent enhancement of weak unisensory responses). **Honest CS prior art (cited, NOT ours, differentiated below):** reliability/uncertainty-weighted multimodal fusion exists but uses **LEARNED** uncertainty heads — evidential/Dirichlet (UEFN, Appl. Intell. 2025, doi:10.1007/s10489-024-06113-6; Dual-level Deep Evidential Fusion, Inf. Fusion 2023, S1566253523004293), Bayesian uncertainty-weighted fusion (Uncertainty-Weighted Image-Event Fusion, arXiv:2505.02393), and gated/attention fusion; learned **side-info fusion** for sequential rec — DIF-SR / **Decoupled Side Information Fusion (SIGIR 2022, doi:10.1145/3477495.3531963)**, Aligned Side Information Fusion (WWW 2024). Kendall & Gal 2017 (learned heteroscedastic uncertainty) is the nearest ML form.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest ledger probes = **X1 `--js-shrink` (DEAD, c→0.004 voted off)** and the always-on additive SBERT baseline. Four concrete distinctions: (1) **Non-learnable** — X1 used a learnable intensity `c` the model nulled; every weight here is a fixed function of frozen data, so there is no scalar to vote off (the documented W1/X1/Y1/GD1 failure mode). (2) **Two-sided** — X1 shrank ID *one-sidedly* toward the learnable (under-trained) TAPE mean `m`; this fuses ID against a **NEW per-item TEXT reliability**, the half X1/XF-4 lacked, which is what lets it *re-balance toward text in the mid band* (the directive-(a) amplifier) rather than only damp ID. (3) **Reliable target** — it weights the **frozen** `sbert_proj(s)` (fully observed at zero exposure), not X1's noisy `m`. (4) **Judged on the MID/tail bands** (X1 on head-dominated overall, where any mid/tail effect washes out). **Vs Z1 `--fitness-gate` (DEAD, KILLED 3/3 cold-text routing):** Z1 forced cold items' representation ONTO full-strength text (`g→1−α`), which at the deep tail crashed accuracy −75%; this is the opposite — at the deep tail *both* precisions are low so it does **not** push cold items onto full text (no reliable fusion exists there), and its target band is mid, not tail. **Vs the prior-art uncertainty-fusion line:** all of UEFN/evidential/gated/Kendall-Gal use a **learned** uncertainty/gate (collapsible here, and not the campaign's novelty); the defensible narrow novelty is the **frozen-data-derived (non-learnable) per-item ID+text inverse-variance fusion with an explicit per-item text-informativeness reliability, motivated by PoIE and evaluated on popularity-banded NDCG** — not "uncertainty-weighted fusion" in general.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag **`--cue-fusion` (action='store_true', default OFF ⇒ block skipped ⇒ bit-identical no-op)**, with fixed (non-learnable) hyperparameters **`--cue-fusion-mode {sum2,sum1}` (default `sum2`)** and **`--cue-fusion-nbr K` (int, default 20)**. Requires text (assert `self.use_sbert`, like Z1 at L645). No `nn.Parameter`, no zero-init scalar.
  1. **ID precision (leak-free, train-only) — reuse existing plumbing.** `π_ID,i = item_freq[i]` (Fisher info of a mean estimate ∝ sample size). The train-only `item_freq` buffer + its build block already exist for X1/Z1 (`run_sasrec_sbert.py:1531-1554` build; `:616/649` register) — **also fire that build when `args.cue_fusion`** (one-line `or args.cue_fusion` on the `if args.js_shrink`/`if args.fitness_gate` guards) and register the buffer if absent (mirror L646-649). Leak-free by the same audited construction (bincount over `train_inters` ids; val/test never consulted).
  2. **TEXT precision (NEW; frozen, leak-free) — distinctiveness, NOT norm.** The SBERT table rows are **L2-normalized** (`:752`, "L2-normalized rows"), so `‖sbert‖²≈1` is degenerate — use **text-manifold distinctiveness** instead: `d_i = 1 − mean(top-K cosine to item i's K nearest frozen-text neighbors)` (a generic title in a crowded text region → high mean cosine → low precision; a distinctive title in a sparse region → low mean cosine → high precision). Compute once at load by **reusing the heat-target chunked-topK machinery verbatim** (`:1570-1581`: `xh = F.normalize(sbert_emb); for s0 in range(0,n_items,2048): sims = xh[s0:e0] @ xh.T; exclude self; tv,_ = sims.topk(K)`), then `d = 1 - tv.mean(dim=1)` (n_items,), pad row 0. Fire this block when `args.cue_fusion`.
  3. **Median-match calibration (data-derived crossover, non-learnable).** Scale text precision so its catalog median equals the median ID precision, locking the channel crossover (`π_ID≈π_text`) to the median-frequency item ≈ the **mid band**: `pi_text = d * (median(item_freq[item_freq>0]) / (median(d[d>0]) + 1e-8))`. Register `self.register_buffer("cue_pi_id", item_freq.float())` and `self.register_buffer("cue_pi_text", pi_text)` (both frozen). (This median-match is the only "tuning" and it is a closed-form data statistic, not a learnable parameter.)
  4. **Fuse at the existing site** — in `item_features`, replace the additive block (`:686-690`) so the two channels carry data-derived weights while the TAPE term `m` stays additive and untouched:
    ```
    if getattr(self, "cue_fusion", False) and self.use_sbert:
        pid  = self.cue_pi_id[item_ids]                         # (...,) train freq
        ptx  = self.cue_pi_text[item_ids]                       # (...,) text distinctiveness (calibrated)
        denom = pid + ptx + 1e-8
        scale = 2.0 if self.cue_mode == "sum2" else 1.0         # sum2 preserves total channel mass
        w_id = (scale * pid / denom).unsqueeze(-1)
        w_tx = (scale * ptx / denom).unsqueeze(-1)
        e = w_id * e + w_tx * self.sbert_proj(self.sbert_table(item_ids))
    elif self.use_sbert:
        e = e + self.sbert_proj(self.sbert_table(item_ids))     # current plain add (unchanged)
    if m is not None:
        e = e + m                                               # additive TAPE term — unchanged
    return e
    ```
    **No-op proof:** `--cue-fusion` absent ⇒ the `elif` runs the current plain add ⇒ bit-identical. **Mass-preservation (the head-inflation guard):** `sum2` makes `w_id+w_tx = 2` per item — identical total channel mass to the current "both weight 1" add — so OFF→ON changes only the *relative* ID/text split, **not** a global rescale; this isolates the re-weighting from a magnitude confound. (`sum1` = the canonical Bayesian weights-sum-to-1 form, halving channel mass; the noted follow-up.) **Honest eval-side note:** the fused embeddings are used at eval (representation-side, like X1/Z1/GD1). The residual risk is that the frequency-keyed `w_id` could amplify head items' ID channel and trivially lift head NDCG — guarded by (i) `sum2` mass-preservation, (ii) the PRIMARY gate being **mid-tercile** NDCG with overall held *in-band* (a head-only spike with flat mid = the confound, not a win), and (iii) the superadditivity falsification test below. **Clean fallback if head inflates:** a `--cue-fusion-trainonly` switch that applies the fused weights only inside `chunked_full_softmax_loss` scoring and keeps the plain add in `all_item_features` (training-only, eval untouched — the supervisor's preferred safe form). Cost: 2 frozen buffers + 2 elementwise weights + one extra `sbert_table`/`sbert_proj` call already in the path — negligible; ~7 min/40ep holds.

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cue-fusion --cue-fusion-mode sum2 --cue-fusion-nbr 20 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_CF1_cuefusion_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_CF1_cuefusion_VG.log 2>&1`
  - Stacks on the **confirmed V2 winning stack** so `--cue-fusion` is the ONLY added flag vs the 0.0674±0.0003 / mid-0.0174 baseline ⇒ clean attribution; `by_popularity` (tail/mid/head) is auto-emitted. If `sum2` shows a mid signal but overall dips from a head re-balance, the cheap follow-ups are `--cue-fusion-mode sum1` and the `--cue-fusion-trainonly` fallback (fixed-hyperparameter sweeps / safe variant, NOT a model-nulled scalar).
  - **Built-in superadditivity falsification (cheap secondary, the directive-(a)/(c) deliverable):** compare per band `NDCG_fused − max(NDCG_ID, NDCG_text)` using the **on-disk** ID arm `results_TAIL_idonly_seed08_VG.json` (= `--no-sbert`) and a one-shot text arm `--sbert-only` (`results_CF1_textonly_VG.json`); PoIE predicts the superadditivity is **largest in mid, ≈0 in tail**. A positive mid superadditivity confirms the mechanism (paper asset for the mid-win section); a ≈0 result is the clean negative "the uniform add is already near-optimal."

- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; full-catalog `n_eval≈94,762`; `by_popularity` printed) → full 40ep. **PRIMARY (directive a):** **mid-tercile** NDCG@10 > the V2 mid baseline 0.0174 (5-seed mean, ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-2) **with overall held in the 0.0674±0.0003 band** (NOT a head-only spike) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed on the mid metric. **SECONDARY (overall):** overall best-by-val > 0.0680 (clear of the V2 band) ⇒ 5-seed; 5-seed mean > 0.0760 ⇒ strict review (not expected — overall is saturated). **Apply the analyst's ep10–20 val−test gap go/no-go:** a genuine fusion lever lifts (mid) test with the structural ~0.0088 gap stable; gap-explosion past ~0.011 with flat test = c3 pattern → kill early.

- **Kill criterion:** **DEAD** if mid-tercile NDCG ≤ the V2 mid baseline 0.0174 (within ±noise) across the run ⇒ the plain unweighted add is already near the inverse-variance optimum (consistent with the analyst's finding that ID/text are sub-additive substitutes) — a clean negative, with the per-band superadditivity curve retained as the directive-(c) "uniform fusion is near-optimal / channels saturated" artifact. **ALSO DEAD** if overall rises ONLY via a head-tercile spike while mid is flat (the frequency-weight head-inflation confound) — report it as a confound, not a win, and the `--cue-fusion-trainonly` fallback is the permitted single follow-up. **Because every weight is data-derived there is no "scalar voted off" outcome** — the result is a direct, un-nullable measurement of whether reliability-weighting the two channels beats the uniform add, exactly the diagnostic the four dead learnable-scalar/parameter-free levers were meant to deliver. The realized per-item `w_text` distribution (where the model is told to trust text) is an interpretable readout that should **peak in the mid tercile** if PoIE holds.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Honest framing — a **non-collapsible, mid-targeted probe** on the campaign's one remaining winnable text regime (mid +6.8%, 5-seed), with a first-principles mechanism (inverse-variance cue fusion) that *derives* the observed tail<head<mid ordering and a NEW per-item text-reliability that no prior probe or recsys work has. **Realistic expected delta: mid-tercile +0% to +8% rel; overall −1% to +1%** (most-probable a small mid lift with the head-dominated overall held in-band). **Two honest headwinds:** (1) the analyst found ID and text are **sub-additive substitutes** (singles-sum +0.0039 vs full +0.0018) — strong prior that the channels are *already* well-combined and the uniform add is near-optimal, so the most likely outcome is a small-or-null mid effect; (2) the mid bucket is itself noisy (±0.0007 5-seed band), so a mid claim needs multi-seed. **Ceiling is clearly <0.0760** (overall is saturated) — this proposal qualifies under the "unless a cheap high-information probe" clause: its core deliverable is the **per-band superadditivity curve**, a *falsifiable* PoIE test that is paper-valuable either way — it either (a) confirms the directive-(a) mid-win mechanism and prescribes the fusion weight, or (b) cleanly shows "the uniform add is already near the inverse-variance optimum," hardening the saturation/honest-negative story. Cheap (~7 min/40ep, **0 added learnable params** — 2 frozen buffers + 2 elementwise weights), pure PyTorch, exact no-op when OFF, leak-free (train-only `item_freq`; frozen-text distinctiveness; representation-side), cleanly attributed (Ernst–Banks/PoIE + the learned-uncertainty-fusion CS prior art cited and differentiated). If it lifts mid NDCG multi-seed with overall held, it is the campaign's first **directive-(a) mid-regime win** and a clean, publishable "reliability-weighted ID×text fusion" contribution; if null, the superadditivity curve is the honest "fusion is already optimal" artifact.

---

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4 — Per-tercile "text-signal share" `s_text`: a variance-partition (GREML/Haseman–Elston) of the next-item signal between TEXT-kinship and ID-kinship, as a pre-registered, falsifiable PREDICTOR of the dataset-conditional tail law  [status: DONE (artifact ran clean) — PREDICTOR REFUTED, ordering INVERTED. Saved/ran measure_text_signal_share.py (CPU-only, read-only, leak-free; ran beside the live Beauty GPU job, no contention). results_TEXTSHARE_law.json + run_TEXTSHARE_law.log. RESULT: s_text^tail VG=0.645(p=.005) | MI=0.043(p=.005) | Beauty=0.719; sanity OK (s_text^head low everywhere .002/.000/.038, s_text∈[0,1]). Pre-registered prediction was VG-LOW/MI-HIGH (text-share tracks the tail benefit: VG 5-seed tail-null, MI 5-seed tail-win). ACTUAL is the OPPOSITE — VG s_text^tail HIGH (0.645) where text does NOT help the tail; MI LOW (0.043) where text DOES. The two SETTLED datasets INVERT the hypothesized rank order ⇒ per the kill criterion ("DEAD as a predictor if the ordering inverts"), s_text^tail does NOT predict the tail benefit. Honest finding: text's SHARE of the existing tail next-item signal does not predict the MARGINAL benefit of adding text — plausibly because where text-signal is already high (VG) the always-on additive SBERT already captures it (no marginal tail gain), while MI's ID-starved tail benefits from even weak text. Beauty s_text^tail also HIGH (0.719); its tail Δ (running) would further test, but the VG/MI inversion already refutes the simple law. Honest negative for the analyst: the dataset disagreement is NOT explained by this text-signal-share partition (rules out an over-claimed law). 3-point caveat respected (rank check, not slope). 2026-06-17]

> **READ THIS FIRST — format/role note.** This is **NOT a model-lever / `run_sasrec_sbert.py --flag` proposal** — the supervisor (FEEDBACK 2026-06-17 cycle 4) and the analyst (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-5, missing-ablation #2) **CLOSED the new-lever search** (CF1 dead on both datasets; W1/X1/Y1/Z1/GD1 all dead) and **explicitly retargeted researchers to "propose a concrete, measurable text-distinctiveness / ID-density statistic that PREDICTS where text helps the tail, so a 3rd-dataset test can confirm a LAW, not just 'two datasets disagree'."** The correct deliverable now is a **read-only, CPU, NO-training, NO-model-edit MEASUREMENT artifact** (a standalone analysis script over the cached frozen `cache_5core/sbert_titles_*.npy` + the TRAIN interaction CSVs) — exactly the form the supervisor and analyst asked for. It does **not** create a model flag, touches no `.py` in the model, and cannot be "voted off" (there is no parameter). It directly serves the win-condition ("≥2 datasets → a dataset-property LAW") and runs **on CPU alongside the live Beauty GPU job** without GPU contention. It grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-17-8** (variance-component "heritability" / GREML + Haseman–Elston + partial-Mantel), with a refinement (phenotype ≠ ID-kinship measure, see Mechanism) that hardens it against circularity.

- **Idea (novel to this campaign):** Treat the catalog as a "phenotype with two competing kinship matrices." Build, per dataset, a **TEXT-kinship** `K_text` (frozen-SBERT cosine, already cached) and an **ID/collaborative-kinship** `K_id` (train-only user-set Jaccard), and define the **phenotype** `y_ij` as the realized *next-item transition* signal (train adjacency-PMI: how much more than chance items `i,j` occur **consecutively** in time-sorted user histories). Then **partition the variance of `y` between `K_text` and `K_id`** with a Haseman–Elston / GREML-style joint regression, **per popularity tercile**, yielding a scalar **`s_text` = (text partial-R²)/(text partial-R² + id partial-R²)** — *"how much of the next-item signal is carried by text BEYOND collaborative co-occurrence,"* measured separately on tail / mid / head item pairs. The decisive number is **`s_text^tail`** (rare×rare pairs, where `K_id`≈0 so text has room to explain). The mechanism hypothesis (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-4/5) says *text rescues the tail exactly where text-signal exceeds ID-signal* — so `s_text^tail` is that hypothesis made **measurable and falsifiable**: it should **rank-order the three datasets in step with the measured text−ID tail Δ** (VG −0.00022 [5-seed null] < Beauty [running] < MI +0.000335 [5-seed, CI excludes 0]). A rank-match converts "two datasets disagree" into a **predictive law**; a rank-mismatch refutes the statistic as the law (a clean, honest finding either way). Folded-in corroborating axis (from XF-9 / the analyst's own missing-ablation #2): also emit a cheap **embedding-based text-distinctiveness scalar** (mean top-1 nearest-neighbour SBERT cosine; lower = more distinctive) + **users/item** (ID-density), so the law is tested on a 2-axis `(text-distinctiveness, ID-density)` predictor that — unlike density alone, which fails to separate VG≈Beauty (3.70≈3.51) — can discriminate them.

- **Source(s):** Variance-component heritability / **GREML–GCTA** — Yang et al. 2011, *Am. J. Hum. Genet.* (GCTA). Method-of-moments regression form — **Haseman & Elston 1972**; "Haseman and Elston revisited," Elston et al. 2000 (PubMed 10861893): regress the mean-centred phenotype cross-product `(y_i−ȳ)(y_j−ȳ)` on relatedness `A_ij`; with two relationship matrices entered jointly the partial slopes partition the trait covariance — *confirmed by web search this cycle*. Two-distance-matrix correlation controlling for a third — **partial-Mantel** (Mantel 1967; Legendre & Fortin), with the **autocorrelation caveat** that raw Mantel inflates type-I error (Guillot & Rousset 2013, arXiv:1112.0651; Legendre 2010, *Mol. Ecol. Resources*) ⇒ this proposal uses the **partial-regression form + a within-stratum permutation null**, NOT a raw Mantel significance claim. Recsys context for "decoupling collaborative vs content signal" (cited as related, NOT the same method): CollectiveKV (decoupling collaborative information), and content×CF variance-blending lines — none partition the *next-item choice variance between a text-kinship and an ID-kinship matrix per popularity tercile as a cross-dataset predictor*, which is the defensible novelty.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest existing artifacts are (a) the analyst's **missing-ablation #2** ("mean top-1 SBERT cosine / intrinsic dimensionality") and (b) **GD1 / BBP-ρ** (ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-3). Three decisive differences from #2: it measures the text channel's **predictive share of the actual next-item signal** (not a text-only spread blind to whether that geometry predicts choices); it **jointly controls for the ID channel** (isolating *text signal beyond ID* — the exact quantity the hypothesis names); and it is **per-tercile** (gives the tail-specific predictor). Vs **GD1/BBP-ρ**: those analysed the **ID spectrum alone** (one channel, one matrix) and produced the *irreducibility* figure for the VG tail-null; this is a **two-matrix partition of the choice variance** that explains *which datasets* escape that null and *why* (text-side axis the BBP figure lacks). It is also **not a model lever** (no parameter ⇒ immune to the W1/X1/Y1 voted-off failure mode) and is **read-only** (no GPU, no `.py` edit), so it neither duplicates nor revives any KILLed direction (it is not "add text to cold items," not a V2-regularizer, not forced routing, not an eval-time popularity term — it touches the model not at all).

- **Mechanism / how to implement (a NEW standalone CPU script — the experiment agent saves it to `_bestrec_run/measure_text_signal_share.py` and runs it; it does NOT edit `run_sasrec_sbert.py`):**
  - **Index alignment (correctness-critical):** the SBERT cache rows are aligned to the model's item index = `sorted(union of parent_asin over train+valid+test)` (`run_sasrec_sbert.py:93-101 reindex`). The script rebuilds that exact `i2idx` from the three split CSVs (using the *asin vocabulary* only — item identities are not leakage), but computes **all statistics (frequency, co-occurrence, transitions) from the TRAIN CSV only** — so it is **leak-free** (val/test target rows never enter any statistic), while staying row-aligned to `cache_5core/sbert_titles_{ds}.npy`.
  - **Refinement vs raw XF-8 (anti-circularity):** XF-8 listed "adjacency-PMI OR Jaccard" for *both* the phenotype and `K_id`, which would be tautological if the same measure is used for both. This grounding fixes the phenotype = **adjacency-transition PMI** (consecutive items in time-sorted train sequences) and `K_id` = **user-set Jaccard** (co-occurrence *anywhere* in a history) — distinct objects, so `s_text` legitimately asks "does text predict transitions *beyond* general collaborative co-occurrence." (Robustness: optionally also report the partition with the phenotype defined as user-set Jaccard and `K_id` as co-occurrence-count cosine; confirm `s_text` ranks the datasets the same way under both — XF-8 risk #2.)
  - **Full script (runnable verbatim once saved):**
    ```python
    #!/usr/bin/env python
    # READ-ONLY artifact (NO GPU, NO training, NO model-code edit). Grounds XF 2026-06-17-8.
    # Per-popularity-tercile "text-signal share" s_text that PREDICTS the dataset-conditional tail law.
    import csv, json, math
    from pathlib import Path
    from collections import defaultdict
    import numpy as np
    ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    SPLIT = ROOT / "data_5core" / "5core" / "last_out"
    EMB   = ROOT / "cache_5core"
    DATASETS = ["Video_Games", "Musical_Instruments", "Beauty_and_Personal_Care"]
    RNG = np.random.default_rng(20260617); M = 40000; NPERM = 200
    def rd(p):
        out = []
        with open(p, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fp:
            for r in csv.DictReader(fp):
                out.append((r["user_id"], r["parent_asin"], int(r["timestamp"])))
        return out
    def z(v):
        v = v - v.mean(); s = v.std(); return v / s if s > 1e-12 else v
    def r2(X, y):
        b, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(X, y, rcond=None); res = y - X @ b
        sst = ((y - y.mean())**2).sum(); return 1.0 - (res**2).sum()/sst if sst > 0 else 0.0
    def partition(y, x1, x2):
        yz, a, b = z(y), z(x1), z(x2); one = np.ones_like(yz)
        Rf = r2(np.column_stack([one, a, b]), yz)
        Rid = r2(np.column_stack([one, b]), yz); Rtx = r2(np.column_stack([one, a]), yz)
        pr_t = max(Rf - Rid, 0.0); pr_i = max(Rf - Rtx, 0.0)
        s = pr_t/(pr_t+pr_i) if (pr_t+pr_i) > 1e-12 else float("nan")
        ge = 1
        for _ in range(NPERM):
            if max(r2(np.column_stack([one, RNG.permutation(a), b]), yz) - Rid, 0.0) >= pr_t: ge += 1
        return dict(R_full=Rf, pr_text=pr_t, pr_id=pr_i, s_text=s, perm_p=ge/(NPERM+1))
    def distinct(T, samp=2000, cstep=20000):
        n = T.shape[0]; idx = RNG.integers(0, n, min(samp, n)); Q = T[idx]; best = np.full(len(idx), -1.0)
        for c0 in range(0, n, cstep):
            S = Q @ T[c0:c0+cstep].T
            for k, i in enumerate(idx):
                if c0 <= i < c0+cstep: S[k, i-c0] = -1.0
            best = np.maximum(best, S.max(axis=1))
        return float(best.mean())
    def analyze(ds):
        tr = rd(SPLIT/f"{ds}.train.csv"); asins = set(a for _, a, _ in tr)
        for sp in ("valid", "test"):
            for _, a, _ in rd(SPLIT/f"{ds}.{sp}.csv"): asins.add(a)
        items = sorted(asins); i2 = {a: j for j, a in enumerate(items)}; n = len(items)
        T = np.load(EMB/f"sbert_titles_{ds}.npy").astype(np.float32)[:n]
        T /= (np.linalg.norm(T, axis=1, keepdims=True) + 1e-8)
        seqs = defaultdict(list); iu = defaultdict(set); freq = np.zeros(n, np.int64)
        for u, a, t in tr:
            j = i2[a]; seqs[u].append((t, j)); iu[j].add(u); freq[j] += 1
        adj = defaultdict(int)
        for u, lst in seqs.items():
            lst.sort()
            for (_, p), (_, q) in zip(lst, lst[1:]):
                if p != q: adj[(p, q) if p < q else (q, p)] += 1
        Ntr = max(int(freq.sum()), 1); pf = freq/Ntr
        order = np.argsort(freq, kind="stable"); b1, b2 = n//3, 2*n//3
        strata = {"tail": order[:b1], "mid": order[b1:b2], "head": order[b2:], "all": order}
        def sample(pool):
            ii = pool[RNG.integers(0, len(pool), M)]; jj = pool[RNG.integers(0, len(pool), M)]
            keep = ii != jj; ii, jj = ii[keep], jj[keep]
            x1 = np.einsum("kd,kd->k", T[ii], T[jj]); x2 = np.empty(len(ii)); y = np.empty(len(ii))
            for k in range(len(ii)):
                i, j = int(ii[k]), int(jj[k]); A, B = iu[i], iu[j]
                inter = len(A & B) if A and B else 0
                x2[k] = inter/(len(A)+len(B)-inter) if inter else 0.0
                c = adj.get((i, j) if i < j else (j, i), 0)
                y[k] = math.log(((c+1e-9)/Ntr)/(pf[i]*pf[j]+1e-12)+1e-12)
            return y, x1, x2
        res = {nm: partition(*sample(pool)) for nm, pool in strata.items()}
        res["meta"] = dict(n_items=n, n_train=Ntr, users_per_item=round(len(seqs)/n, 3),
                           mean_top1_text_cos=distinct(T))
        return res
    if __name__ == "__main__":
        out = {ds: analyze(ds) for ds in DATASETS}
        (ROOT/"_bestrec_run"/"results_TEXTSHARE_law.json").write_text(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
        for ds, r in out.items():
            print("%-26s s_text tail=%.3f (p=%.3f) mid=%.3f head=%.3f all=%.3f | upi=%.2f top1cos=%.3f"
                  % (ds, r["tail"]["s_text"], r["tail"]["perm_p"], r["mid"]["s_text"],
                     r["head"]["s_text"], r["all"]["s_text"],
                     r["meta"]["users_per_item"], r["meta"]["mean_top1_text_cos"]))
    ```
  - Cost: O(M·d) per stratum + an O(samp·n) chunked NN scan — **seconds on CPU per dataset** (Beauty's 207k items: the chunked `distinct()` caps memory; the pair sampling is O(M) regardless of `n`). Output: `_bestrec_run/results_TEXTSHARE_law.json` (a NEW results file — does not overwrite any existing `results_*`) + a one-line-per-dataset stdout table for the analyst.

- **Exact command (executor runs this AFTER saving the script above to `_bestrec_run/measure_text_signal_share.py`):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/measure_text_signal_share.py > _bestrec_run/run_TEXTSHARE_law.log 2>&1`
  - CPU-only, read-only inputs (cached `.npy` + train/valid/test CSVs), no GPU ⇒ **safe to run concurrently with the live Beauty training job** (per the compute policy, CPU side-jobs do not contend with the GPU). No `--flag`, no model run.

- **Gate (this is a measurement artifact, not an NDCG run — gate is the LAW's confirmation logic):** "smoke" = the script runs clean and emits finite `s_text^{tail,mid,head}` + permutation p for all 3 datasets (sanity: `s_text^head` should be LOW everywhere — head pairs are ID-dominated; `s_text` ∈ [0,1]; tail permutation p reported). **LAW CONFIRMED** if the per-dataset **`s_text^tail` rank-orders in step with the measured text−ID tail Δ** — i.e. `s_text^tail(VG) < s_text^tail(MI)` (the two settled 5-seed endpoints) AND Beauty falls on the predicted side of its (running) tail Δ. **Decisive Beauty discrimination:** density alone predicts Beauty≈VG (upi 3.51≈3.70) ⇒ tail-null; if Beauty's `s_text^tail` is HIGH (and its tail Δ comes out positive) ⇒ **text-distinctiveness, not density, governs the law** (the strong result); if Beauty's `s_text^tail` is LOW (tail Δ null) ⇒ the density framing holds. **Either Beauty outcome sharpens the law** — hand the table + the `(s_text^tail, users/item, mean_top1_text_cos, tail Δ)` cross-dataset matrix to the analyst as the paper's dataset-conditional-law figure (complementing the BBP-ρ irreducibility figure with the missing text-side axis).

- **Kill criterion:** the artifact is "DEAD as a predictor" (NOT dead as an analysis) if **`s_text^tail` does NOT rank-order with the tail Δ** across the 3 datasets (e.g. VG and MI come out indistinguishable, or the ordering inverts) — then the statistic is not the law's mechanism, and the honest report is "the measured text-signal-share does not predict the tail benefit; the dataset disagreement is not explained by this partition" (a valid finding that still rules out an over-claimed law). **Binding honesty caveats (do NOT over-claim):** (1) **3 datasets = a 3-point fit** ⇒ report a **rank/ordering check**, never a regression slope (the same skepticism applied to the val=0.0765 and seed08 traps, now on the dataset axis); each extra AR2023 category the experiment agent later runs (Office/Tools/Arts) adds a point and hardens it. (2) Use the **partial-regression + permutation null**, never a raw-Mantel significance claim (autocorrelation inflates type-I error — Guillot–Rousset). (3) Confirm `s_text` ranks the datasets the same under both phenotype definitions (adjacency-PMI and user-Jaccard) before reporting the ordering as robust.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** This is the **highest-value non-GPU artifact in the pipeline right now** — it is the supervisor's literal #1 retarget ask and the analyst's missing-ablation #2, and it is the one thing standing between "two datasets disagree" and a **publishable predictive LAW** ("text-augmentation's long-tail benefit is governed by the dataset's text-signal-to-ID-signal ratio"). Pre-registered prediction (mechanism hypothesis, ANALYSIS 2026-06-17-5): `s_text^tail(VG)` LOW (text explains little of rare-item transitions beyond ID ⇒ matches the 5-seed VG tail-null and all-3-components-tail-negative) and `s_text^tail(MI)` HIGH (text explains rare-item transitions where ID is starved ⇒ matches the 5-seed MI tail-WIN, TAPE-driven), with `s_text^tail` rank-correlating with the tail Δ. **It cannot be "voted off"** (no parameter), runs **now** on CPU beside the live Beauty job, is **leak-free by construction** (TRAIN interactions + frozen text only), and yields a paper figure regardless of which way the law breaks — if the ordering holds it is the law; if it breaks it is an honest "the disagreement is unexplained by text-signal-share." The realized per-tercile `s_text` per dataset is itself a quantitative artifact that **completes** the dataset-conditional contribution (the text-side complement to the BBP-ρ tail-irreducibility figure). Cheap, read-only, no GPU contention, no model edit, no KILL violated.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-1 — CONTROLLED density-titration (rarefaction series): causally locate the tail-law crossing density on ONE dataset by thinning VG training interactions, instead of correlating 3 natural datasets  [status: RUNNING — GPU freed (Beauty job done). Implemented --subsample-train-frac/--subsample-mode/--subsample-seed (default 1.0 no-op, zero new params): thins ONLY train interactions; eval conditioning histories + tail/mid/head terciles FROZEN at full density (eval keeps full user_seqs; only the training dataset uses the thinned set). py_compile OK. 2ep smoke PASS (no NaN; frac=0.66 -> train interactions/item=16.11 ≈ MI-density, full=24.41; terciles frozen; by_popularity emitted; healthy trajectory). Launched the decisive rho=0.66 BOTH-ARMS probe (TEXT=V2 stack; ID-only=+--no-sbert) seed 20260608 40ep. Will compute text-ID tail Delta vs the on-disk rho=1.0 VG Delta (-0.000148±0.000179); clearly-more-positive => escalate to full rho ladder. STATUS NOW: DONE (see below). 2026-06-18]
> **DONE — DECISIVE-RUNG RESULT: density-causation NOT supported; pre-registered prediction DISCONFIRMED (2-seed).** At rho=0.66 (train interactions/item=16.11 ≈ MI-density), text-ID tail Delta = **-0.000594 (seed08) / -0.000305 (seed09), 2-seed mean -0.000450** — i.e. MORE NEGATIVE than the rho=1.0 VG baseline (-0.000148±0.000179) and the OPPOSITE sign of MI's natural +0.000335. **Thinning DENSE VG down to MI-density does NOT reproduce MI's positive tail Delta** (both seeds negative, trend moving the WRONG way as density drops). ⇒ Global training density ALONE does not cause the dataset-conditional tail law; MI's tail-win is driven by category/text-structure, not by its lower density (the category-confound the experiment was built to rule out is, here, the actual driver). The positive-flip escalation gate is NOT met, so the full rho ladder was NOT auto-run (left to supervisor scope). FINDING FOR ANALYST: combined with PROPOSAL 2026-06-17-4 (text-signal-share predictor REFUTED/inverted), BOTH candidate governing statistics — text-signal-share AND global density — are now refuted on the settled/controlled axes ⇒ the dataset-conditional tail benefit has no confirmed single-variable predictor yet; honest-negative map strengthened. CAVEAT (binding): single decisive rung × 2 seeds on a noisy ~small tail bucket; a full dose-response ladder (rho 0.50/0.38/0.28) + 2nd-mode (user) check remains available if the supervisor wants the complete curve, but the core MI-density prediction is disconfirmed. Files: results_TITRATE_{text,idonly}_rho066{,_seed09}_VG.json. 2026-06-18]

> **READ THIS FIRST — format/role note.** This is a **DATA-ABLATION EXPERIMENT, not a model lever.** The new flag is a **train-data-loader subsampling knob** (zero new parameters, `--subsample-train-frac 1.0` ⇒ bit-identical no-op); it adds NO architecture/loss/scoring change, so it is **fully compliant with the closed-lever directive** (FEEDBACK 2026-06-18 cycle 5: "NO new model levers"). It is **NOT** a V2-regularizer (GD1-class), **NOT** forced ID→text routing (Z1-class), **NOT** eval-time popularity/exposure manipulation, **NOT** a learnable scalar that can be "voted off." It directly executes the supervisor's own cycle-5 RESEARCHERS-#2 ask ("more density points … a SMALL-CATALOG, low-interactions/item … 4th data point") in its **controlled** form: rather than scavenge a new natural dataset (and re-pay Beauty's 62× full-eval cost), it **manufactures an entire density axis from the FAST VG dataset** by thinning training interactions, holding category/encoder/eval-protocol/catalog fixed. This is the **interventional** complement to the campaign's three **observational** threshold artifacts (XF-4/BBP single-spike variance; the per-item Spearman–Brown/Allan `n*` derivations XF-18-1/-2 still QUEUED-as-NEW; the collective `α` recovery-transition XF-18-3) — none of which manipulate the governing variable, so none can move the law from a 3-point correlation to a causal dose–response.

- **Idea (novel):** The campaign's deepest credibility hole is that the dataset-conditional tail law rests on **N=3 natural points** (VG/MI/Beauty), compared **at unequal global sampling depth** (interactions/item 24.5 / 16.2 / 24.9) — and no amount of threshold *theory* upgrades a 3-point cross-dataset correlation to causation (a reviewer's first objection: "MI differs from VG in a hundred ways besides density — category, vocabulary, brand structure, review style"). Import the methodology three non-CS fields use for exactly this situation — **ecology rarefaction** (don't compare communities sampled at unequal effort; rarefy to a common depth), **pharmacology dose–response / Hill EC50** (titrate the dose on a ladder, fit the sigmoid, read off the critical dose), and **condensed-matter finite-size scaling** (sweep the control parameter, locate the crossing, confirm data-collapse). Concretely: take the DENSE, **confirmed-tail-NULL** VG dataset, hold the model (V2 stack), the text/ID contrast, the encoder, the eval users + their conditioning histories + targets, the eval protocol, and the catalog **ALL fixed**, and **thin ONLY the training interactions** down a ladder of fractions ρ so the global density `α = (interactions/item)/d_eff` slides from VG's supercritical ≈1.07 down through MI's ≈0.70 and below. At each rung measure the **text−ID tail Δ** (the "effect") vs density (the "dose"). The pre-registered prediction (from matrix-completion sample-complexity + XF-18-3's collective recovery transition): the tail Δ is ≈0/negative at full VG density and rises **monotonically, crossing zero into positive** as density drops below `α_c≈1` — and the crossing density should land near where MI naturally sits. **If VG, thinned to MI-like density, flips its tail Δ from null to positive, "global ID-density CAUSES the tail law" is demonstrated causally, on one dataset, with every confounder held constant** — and the 3 natural datasets become 3 dots that should lie on the titration curve at their own densities. That converts the campaign's weakest link into its most reviewer-proof figure.

- **Source(s):** Grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-18-4** (rarefaction + dose–response + finite-size scaling). Methodology: rarefaction — Sanders 1968; Hurlbert 1971; Gotelli & Colwell 2001 ([Hill-number rarefaction/extrapolation, *Methods Ecol. Evol.* 2012](https://besjournals.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/full/10.1111/j.2041-210x.2011.00178.x)); dose–response/Hill EC50 — Hill 1910, Chou–Talalay median-effect ([EC50 determination, *J. Med. Chem.* 2024](https://pubs.acs.org/doi/10.1021/acs.jmedchem.4c02052)); finite-size scaling — Fisher–Barber 1972. Theory the prediction rests on (verified this cycle): matrix-completion exact-recovery is a **sharp phase transition in a global observation density** — a threshold `p*` on observation probability above which error→0, below which it does not, with ~`r(m+n)` (≈`r·d·log d`) samples needed ([On the Fundamental Limits of Matrix Completion, arXiv:2109.05408](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2109.05408); [Matrix Completion: Theory, Algorithms, Empirical Evaluation, arXiv:2512.08689](https://arxiv.org/html/2512.08689v1)); collective low-rank recovery transition — Lesieur–Krzakala–Zdeborová ([IOPscience](https://iopscience.iop.org/article/10.1088/1742-5468/aa7284), arXiv:1701.00858); SBM/KS detectability — Decelle et al. 2011; Abbe ([JMLR 16-480](https://jmlr.org/papers/volume18/16-480/16-480.pdf)). Recsys context (qualitative cold-start, NOT this method — confirms the gap): DenseRec (arXiv:2508.18442), "Let It Go? Not Quite" (arXiv:2507.19473), Unifying Generative+Dense Retrieval (arXiv:2411.18814) — all show "content helps cold items" but none titrate training density to locate a crossing density of the text−ID **differential**; recsys "scaling-law" work (Climber arXiv:2502.09888; Wukong) varies model/sequence size monotonically, never a controlled density titration of a differential cold-start effect.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest are the three threshold artifacts: **XF-4/BBP** (GD1 spectral, on disk), the per-item `n*` derivations (**XF-18-1 Spearman–Brown, XF-18-2 Allan** — read-only, still NEW), and **XF-18-3** (collective `α` recovery transition — read-only, still NEW). **All are OBSERVATIONAL**: each derives/measures a threshold and then *passively correlates* it against the 3 natural datasets. **This is the first artifact that MANIPULATES the governing variable** (global training density) to test the law causally — it converts XF-18-3's *predicted* `α_c` from a number checked against 3 dots into a *measured crossing density* with a CI, traced within ONE dataset where category/encoder/eval/catalog are held constant. It is the experimental complement those derivations structurally cannot be (they have no manipulation). It is also distinct from any recsys data-scaling ablation: those plot a **monotone** "more data → higher NDCG" learning curve; this traces a **differential** (text−ID tail Δ) expecting a **sign-flip at a threshold**, against a held-fixed contrast. No ledger probe varies training density at all.

- **Mechanism / how to implement (a train-loader knob in `run_sasrec_sbert.py`; NO model/loss/scoring edit; `frac=1.0` ⇒ exact no-op):**
  - **New args** (next to `--eval-subsample`/`--eval-stratify-head`, ~`run_sasrec_sbert.py:1316`): `--subsample-train-frac` (float, default **1.0**), `--subsample-mode` (choices `interaction`/`user`, default `interaction`), `--subsample-seed` (int, default 0). At `1.0` the loader is untouched ⇒ bit-identical to current behaviour.
  - **Where it goes** — between `reindex(...)` (`:1558`) and `build_user_sequences(train_inters)` (`:1565`):
    1. **Build the FULL-density artifacts FIRST (these are FROZEN across the ladder, the control):** `user_seqs_full = build_user_sequences(train_inters)`. Compute the tail/mid/head tercile bucket from **full-density** train frequency exactly as `evaluate()` does (`:1137-1150`) and assign it to the model before the first eval: `model._eval_item_bucket = <full-density bucket>` (so every rung's eval uses the **same** terciles, not the thinned ones — this is the comparability requirement that makes the manipulation clean).
    2. **Thin a COPY of the train interactions** (training only):
       - `interaction` mode: `rng = np.random.RandomState(args.subsample_seed); mask = rng.random(len(train_inters)) < args.subsample_train_frac; train_inters_sub = [r for r, k in zip(train_inters, mask) if k]` (drops each train interaction i.i.d. — lowers interactions/item AND users/item together = the ecology "rarefy to common depth" move).
       - `user` mode: randomly keep a fraction `frac` of *users*, keep all their interactions, drop the rest (lowers users/item — the KS mean-degree axis — more cleanly; perturbs history length differently → a built-in robustness check).
    3. `user_seqs_train = build_user_sequences(train_inters_sub)` → this is what feeds `SASRecDataset` (TRAINING ONLY).
    4. **Hold eval conditioning histories FIXED:** pass `user_seqs_train` to the dataset/trainer, but pass **`user_seqs_full`** (full-length histories) to `evaluate()` as the conditioning sequences. This isolates the manipulated variable to *global training density* and removes the sequence-length confound for the eval users (XF-18-4 risk #1). `user_times` (for `--time-bias`) must be built to match whichever sequence set it is paired with (full for eval, thinned for train).
    5. **Do NOT re-5-core and do NOT re-`reindex`** — `n_items`/catalog stay fixed from the full reindex (items falling below 5 interactions IS the manipulation; terciles are frozen from full density so buckets stay comparable). SBERT/TAPE caches are item-id aligned and untouched.
    6. **Print the realized density:** `interactions/item = len(train_inters_sub)/n_items` at each rung so the analyst can compute `α = (interactions/item)/d_eff` (d_eff≈23 from the GD/BBP figure).
  - **Leak-free by construction:** val/test target rows never enter training at any ρ (only `train_inters` is thinned); terciles are train-frequency only and frozen from full density; eval still ranks each user against the full `n_items` catalog. No new learnable parameter ⇒ nothing to vote off.

- **Exact command (executor runs this — the DECISIVE first probe: ONE thinned rung at ρ=0.66 ≈ MI-density, BOTH arms, seed08; compare its tail Δ to the on-disk full-density ρ=1.0 VG baseline `results_TAIL_V2_{text,idonly}_VG.json`):**
  TEXT arm: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --subsample-train-frac 0.66 --subsample-mode interaction --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TITRATE_text_rho066_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_TITRATE_text_rho066_VG.log 2>&1`
  ID-only arm (same minus text): `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --no-sbert --subsample-train-frac 0.66 --subsample-mode interaction --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TITRATE_idonly_rho066_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_TITRATE_idonly_rho066_VG.log 2>&1`
  **Sequencing (binding): run ONLY when the GPU is free — must NOT contend with the live Beauty 3rd-point job (supervisor cycle-5 TOP PRIORITY).** Each thinned run trains on LESS data than full VG ⇒ *cheaper* than a standard VG run.
  **If the ρ=0.66 rung's text−ID tail Δ is clearly positive (sign-flipped vs the ρ=1.0 null):** escalate to the full ladder — ρ ∈ {1.0 (on disk), 0.85, 0.66, 0.50, 0.38, 0.28} × {text, idonly} × seeds {20260608, 20260609}, and repeat the bracket in `--subsample-mode user` to confirm the crossing is robust to the thinning scheme (interaction vs user mode perturb history length differently; agreement ⇒ density, not length, is the driver). Hand the `(ρ, interactions/item, α, tail Δ ± seed band)` table to the analyst for the Hill/log-logistic sigmoid fit + crossing density α₀ (bootstrap CI) and the overlay of the 3 natural datasets at their measured α.

- **Gate:** This is a CAUSAL/MEASUREMENT experiment, not a SOTA-chasing lever — the standard ">0.0660→5-seed→0.0760 strict-review" gate does NOT apply (overall NDCG falls under thinning by design; that is not a regression). Gate = the **sign-of-slope** check: smoke 2ep (no NaN, eval emits `by_popularity` with the FROZEN full-density terciles, realized interactions/item printed) → full 40ep on both arms at ρ=0.66 → compare text−ID tail Δ to the on-disk full-density ρ=1.0 VG Δ (−0.000148 ± 0.000179, 5-seed). **LAW-CAUSAL CONFIRMED (escalate to full ladder)** if the ρ=0.66 tail Δ is clearly more positive than the ρ=1.0 Δ (a monotone move toward MI's positive regime). The fitted α₀/EC50 is a SECONDARY readout with a CI; the PRIMARY claim is the monotone trend + sign-flip.

- **Kill criterion:** (1) If the tail Δ stays flat/negative across the WHOLE ladder down to and below MI-density, "global ID-density CAUSES the tail flip" is **REFUTED on the controlled axis** → density alone is insufficient and the governing statistic is still open (both text-side predictors already refuted) — a clean, publishable negative that *strengthens* the honest-negative map. (2) If `interaction`-mode and `user`-mode give OPPOSITE crossing behaviour, the effect is a **sequence-length artifact, not global density** → report as the finding that refines the law from "global density" to a length mechanism (still publishable). (3) Honesty caveats (binding): a ~110-hit VG tail bucket + 6-rung sigmoid is noisy ⇒ report the **monotone trend + sign-flip as the primary claim** (robust), α₀ as secondary-with-CI; require the crossing to replicate across both subsampling modes before any "α_c measured at X" statement; require a 2nd dense dataset's ladder before any *universality / data-collapse* claim. Do NOT re-core after thinning; do NOT let the thinned terciles drift (freeze from full density).

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Not an NDCG-improvement lever — its value is **causal identification of the dataset-conditional law**, the campaign's stated #1 deliverable. Pre-registered prediction (matrix-completion `p*` sharp threshold + XF-18-3 collective `α` transition): text−ID tail Δ rises monotonically as VG density drops, **crossing 0 into positive near α_c≈1** (≈ρ where interactions/item ~16, i.e. MI-density, ρ≈0.66); at MI-density expect a tail Δ on the order of MI's confirmed +0.0003. The payoff figure — *tail Δ vs global training density, one titration curve with VG/MI/Beauty sitting on it at their own densities* — is the single most reviewer-proof object the campaign can produce: it kills the "category confound" objection by holding everything but density fixed, and it is the controlled-experiment form of the supervisor's "more density points / 4th sparse dataset" ask, obtained from the FAST dataset at *below*-full-VG cost. Pair it with the in-field's natural-variation 4th-dataset hunt (observational + interventional together identify the causal effect). Compliant with the closed-lever pivot (data-loader knob, zero new parameters, leak-free, `frac=1.0` no-op); sequenced strictly behind the live Beauty job.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-2 — Pre-registered curve-SHAPE prediction for the density-titration: refine the ρ-ladder to bracket the predicted crossing ρ_c≈0.94 and adjudicate "sharp collective transition vs smooth per-item crossover" by Hill-slope + plateau + finite-size collapse  [status: DONE — REFUTED (interaction-mode ladder 5-seed complete, s08–s12 both arms; pre-registered positive-plateau-below-ρ_c / crossing REFUTED: tail Δ flat/trend-free across ρ∈{1.0…0.66}, never crosses toward MI's +0.000335 win, ρ=0.66 5-seed −0.000108±0.000503 1/5 pos; HEAD monotone CONFIRMED +0.00354 5/5 ⇒ density = head CAUSE / tail CORRELATE-ONLY). — original RUNNING note: GPU free; uses 2026-06-18-1's already-implemented --subsample-train-frac (no new code). Running the 5 ADDED bracket rungs ρ∈{0.97,0.94,0.91,0.88,0.78} × {TEXT(V2), ID-only(--no-sbert)} at seed08 first (10 runs), to fill the 1.0→0.66 gap and trace tail-Δ vs density near the predicted crossing ρ_c≈0.94. NOTE: 2026-06-18-1's two endpoints already trend MORE NEGATIVE as ρ drops (ρ=1.0 −0.000148 → ρ=0.66 −0.00045), opposite the predicted positive-plateau-below-ρ_c ⇒ these rungs likely complete a pre-registered-but-REFUTED curve (kill-criterion-1, still a publishable honest negative). Escalate a rung to seed09 only if it shows a positive Δ. Analyst-side Hill/AIC/plateau fit is read-only CPU. 2026-06-18]
> **DONE — NO CLEAN DENSITY CROSSING; pre-registered transition SHAPES REFUTED (multi-seed).** Ran the 5 bracket rungs; seed08 showed a broad positive hump (ρ=0.94..0.78 all +) BUT it was largely SEED08 NOISE — multi-seed collapsed it. **5-seed/2-seed text-ID tail Δ vs ρ (α=ρ·1.061):** ρ=1.00(α1.06) −0.000148(5s) | ρ=0.97 −0.000045(1s) | ρ=0.94(α1.00) −0.000010(2s) | ρ=0.91 +0.000044(2s) | **ρ=0.88(α0.93) +0.000537±0.000346 (5s, 4/5 positive, ~3 SE>0)** | ρ=0.78(α0.83) +0.000056±0.000494 (5s, scattered ≈0; seed08 +0.00087 was an OUTLIER) | ρ=0.66(α0.70) −0.000450(2s). **Verdict:** the ONLY surviving positive is an ISOLATED weak blip at ρ=0.88 (α≈0.93, just below the predicted α_c≈1.0); its neighbors ρ=0.78 & 0.66 (also below α_c) are ≈0 and NEGATIVE — so the pre-registered "positive plateau below α_c" / monotone-crossing / step shapes (Mechanisms A/B-cont/B-1st) are ALL REFUTED. There is NO clean density-driven tail-crossing; at most a narrow ~α0.93 window where text marginally helps the VG tail, not a transition. Combined with 2026-06-18-1 (density-causation not supported at MI-density) and 2026-06-17-4 (text-share predictor inverted), the dataset-conditional tail benefit has NO confirmed clean governing law on the controlled/observational axes — a strengthened honest-negative for the paper. Handed the full (ρ, interactions/item, α, tail Δ ± seed band) table to the analyst for the formal Hill/AIC fit (pre-register: shape under-determined; report the ρ=0.88 blip + CI, not a transition). Optional finite-size (--subsample-items-frac) 2nd ladder NOT run (gated low-priority; the single-catalog result already shows no clean shape). Files: results_TITR_{text,idonly}_rho{097,094,091,088,078}_s{08,09,10,11,12}_VG.json. 2026-06-18]

> **READ THIS FIRST — format/role note.** This is the **quantitative-prediction LAYER** for the already-RUNNING PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-1 (density-titration), NOT a new experiment and NOT a model lever. It grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-18-5** and is the supervisor's **verbatim cycle-6 RESEARCHERS-#1 binding ask**: *"make the threshold theory QUANTITATIVELY PREDICTIVE of the density-titration curve — don't just argue a sign; specify the predicted curve SHAPE (crossing density α_c, slope, whether a sharp transition or a smooth bias–variance crossover) so the titration can confirm/refute a NUMBER, not just a direction."* Its two executor-actionable deliverables are: **(1)** REFINE the 2026-06-18-1 ρ-ladder (its current {1.0, 0.85, 0.66, 0.50, 0.38, 0.28} has a 0.15-wide gap straddling the predicted crossing — it literally cannot see ρ_c≈0.94) by **adding 5 fine rungs** that bracket ρ_c and confirm a low-ρ plateau; **(2)** an OPTIONAL, lower-priority finite-size 2nd ladder. The analyst-side fit/AIC/Hill-slope/plateau/collapse protocol is read-only CPU (no code in `run_sasrec_sbert.py`). It uses ONLY the existing `--subsample-train-frac` knob (already implemented & smoke-passed per 2026-06-18-1) — zero new parameters, `frac=1.0` no-op, leak-free, non-collapsible. Fully compliant with the closed-lever directive (it is the *prediction* for a sanctioned DATA-ablation, adds no architecture/loss/scoring change).

- **Idea (novel):** The three live mechanisms on the bus each predict a **different functional form** for the titration's *tail Δ vs density* curve, so the curve's SHAPE — not just its sign — can *discriminate which mechanism is true*, which is exactly the supervisor's "sharp transition vs smooth crossover" question. To leading order the text−ID tail Δ is a decreasing function of the ID channel's recovery quality `q(α)` (text wins the tail by the margin ID *fails* to recover it), and the three mechanisms give three distinguishable `q(α)` shapes:
  - **Mechanism A — per-item bias–variance crossover (grounds XF-18-1):** ID error ≈ σ²/n ∝ 1/α (smooth), text error = b² (flat) ⇒ **tailΔ(α) ≈ c/α − b²**: a SMOOTH, ANALYTIC hyperbola — monotone, finite slope everywhere, NO plateau, NO cusp, crossing 0 at α*=c/b² with an ordinary (Hill `n≈1`) slope; the "transition" is the whole ladder wide.
  - **Mechanism B-continuous — collective spectral recovery transition (grounds XF-18-3, dense prior):** ID overlap q=0 for α<α_c (ID is pure noise ⇒ text wins by a CONSTANT margin = a FLAT POSITIVE PLATEAU), then q ∝ √(α−α_c) above ⇒ tailΔ is **plateau-positive below α_c, a √-CUSP (infinite-slope kink) at α_c, then a decline crossing 0** — a non-analytic point the hyperbola lacks (BBP overlap law `|⟨v₁,x⟩|² = 1 − 1/λ²`, mean-field exponent β=½).
  - **Mechanism B-first-order — all-or-nothing (sparse spiked prior, Barbier–Macris–Rush NeurIPS 2020):** q JUMPS 0→≈1 at α_c ⇒ tailΔ is a near-vertical STEP (flat-positive plateau, sharp drop, flat-negative) = a Hill sigmoid with coefficient n→∞.
  **Decisive single discriminator = the Hill slope `n` at the zero-crossing:** `n≈1` ⇒ smooth crossover (A); `n≫1` ⇒ collective transition (B-cont); `n→∞`/step ⇒ all-or-nothing (B-1st). Plus: a statistically-present **flat plateau below ρ_c** is the collective-transition signature absent in the hyperbola.

- **Source(s):** Grounds **CROSSFIELD XF 2026-06-18-5** (Landau order-parameter scaling + BBP √-cusp vs all-or-nothing step + Fisher–Barber finite-size collapse). Critical-shape theory: BBP overlap `1−1/λ²` / β=½ — Benaych-Georges–Nadakuditi; [RMT/BBP notes, Barbier](https://jeanbarbier.github.io/jeanbarbier/docs/part1_rmt_bbp_beamer.pdf). All-or-nothing step (sparse prior) — [Barbier–Macris–Rush, *All-or-nothing phase transitions in sparse spiked matrix estimation*, NeurIPS 2020, arXiv:2006.07971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2006.07971) and [arXiv:1911.05030](https://arxiv.org/pdf/1911.05030) (MMSE jumps from max→0 at a sharp SNR threshold for Bernoulli/Bernoulli–Rademacher spikes). Finite-size scaling / data-collapse — Fisher–Barber 1972; [data-collapse quality measure, Bhattacharjee–Seno, cond-mat/0102515](https://arxiv.org/pdf/cond-mat/0102515). Global-density recovery transition the prediction rests on — Lesieur–Krzakala–Zdeborová [IOPscience](https://iopscience.iop.org/article/10.1088/1742-5468/aa7284) (arXiv:1701.00858); matrix-completion sharp `p*` threshold — [On the Fundamental Limits of Matrix Completion, arXiv:2109.05408](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2109.05408); [Matrix Completion with Hypergraphs: Sharp Thresholds, LoG 2025 / arXiv:2401.08197](https://arxiv.org/html/2401.08197) (confirms a phase transition in observation density, achievable above / impossible below a sharp threshold). Per-item-crossover mechanism A = the bus's XF-18-1 (Spearman–Brown `n*=σ²/b²`).

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest is **PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-1** (the titration) and its cross-field parent **XF-18-4**. 2026-06-18-1 explicitly hedges to "monotone trend + sign-flip as the primary claim, fitted EC50 secondary" — it runs the dose but predicts NO functional form, NO crossing number, and NO mechanism discriminator, and its ladder does not even sample near the predicted crossing (no rung in (0.85, 1.0)). This proposal is the **missing quantitative layer**: (i) it derives three distinguishable curve SHAPES from the three live mechanisms, (ii) gives a **pre-registered falsifiable crossing `ρ_c≈0.94` on VG** (from α_c≈1 and the measured d_eff≈23), and (iii) supplies the **Hill-slope + plateau + finite-size-collapse** tests that turn the titration from a sign-confirmation into a *mechanism-adjudication*. Distinct from XF-18-3 (which asserts a collective transition *exists* but predicts no curve shape) and from any ML "scaling-law" ablation (monotone power-law fit, never a critical-exponent / data-collapse shape test on a *differential* effect). It does not duplicate 2026-06-18-1; it makes it sharper and pre-registers what 2026-06-18-1 left open.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:**
  - **(1) REFINE THE LADDER (executor; no code change — uses 2026-06-18-1's existing `--subsample-train-frac`).** Using VG full interactions/item ≈ 24.4 (smoke log) and d_eff ≈ 23 (GD/BBP figure), α(ρ) = ρ·24.4/23 = ρ·1.061. Predicted crossing α_c≈1.0 ⇒ **ρ_c ≈ 23/24.4 ≈ 0.94**; MI-density α≈0.70 ⇒ ρ≈0.66. To bracket ρ_c finely AND sample a low-ρ plateau, run BOTH arms (TEXT=V2 stack; ID-only=+`--no-sbert`, drop `--text-prototypes`/`--text-sim-bias`) at the **5 ADDED rungs** ρ ∈ {**0.97, 0.94, 0.91, 0.88, 0.78**} (α ≈ {1.029, 0.997, 0.966, 0.934, 0.828}), ≥2 seeds/rung — which, merged with 2026-06-18-1's {1.0, 0.85, 0.66, 0.50, 0.38, 0.28}, gives a ladder that has FOUR rungs in α∈[0.90,1.03] straddling the predicted crossing and a clean low-density tail {0.66, 0.50, 0.38, 0.28} for the plateau test. Each rung prints realized interactions/item ⇒ analyst computes α. Leak-safety = identical to 2026-06-18-1 (terciles frozen from full train; eval histories/targets/catalog fixed; verify `--subsample-train-frac 1.0` reproduces VG bit-for-bit). Run `--subsample-mode interaction`; repeat the 4 bracket rungs {0.97,0.94,0.91,0.88} in `--subsample-mode user` as the robustness cross-check.
  - **(2) ANALYST-SIDE FIT (read-only CPU, ≈60 lines NumPy/scipy; the analyst runs this over the titration's `(ρ, interactions/item, α, tail Δ ± seed band, tail-HR)` table — NOT a training step):** pre-register and least-squares fit the THREE forms with shared α_c — (A) hyperbola `tailΔ = A/α − B`; (B-cont) plateau+√-cusp `tailΔ = P − C·√(max(α−α_c,0))` (flat = P for α≤α_c); (B-1st) logistic `tailΔ = D + L/(1+e^{k(α−α_c)})` with free Hill `k`. Report **AIC/BIC** per form, the **fitted α_c (⇒ ρ_c) with a bootstrap CI** on the ~110-hit tail bucket, the **Hill slope n at the crossing** (the single A-vs-B discriminator), and a **plateau-presence test** (is there a statistically-flat positive segment below ρ_c?). Overlay the **3 natural datasets (VG/MI/Beauty)** at their measured α and test whether they fall within the fitted curve's CI (observational law ⊕ interventional titration agreeing = the reviewer-proof object).
  - **(3) OPTIONAL finite-size collapse (LOWER priority than Beauty AND the primary ladder; needs one compliant data-loader knob):** add `--subsample-items-frac` (float, default **1.0 ⇒ exact no-op**; randomly keep that fraction of the *catalog* before reindex — a DATA-ablation knob in the same class as `--subsample-train-frac`, NOT a model lever, leak-free), then re-run the bracket rungs at items-frac=0.5 (N→N/2). Prediction: a genuine finite-size-rounded transition WIDENS by ≈√2 (width ∝ N^{−1/2}) and the two ladders **data-collapse** when plotted vs `(α−α_c)·N^{1/2}` (score with Bhattacharjee–Seno); width insensitive to N ⇒ a true smooth crossover. If GPU-unaffordable, SKIP — the single-catalog Hill-slope + plateau test already discriminates A from B.

- **Exact command (executor runs this — the 5 ADDED bracket rungs, interaction mode, seed 20260608; repeat each with `--seed 20260609`, and run the ID-only twin of each by adding `--no-sbert` and dropping `--text-prototypes 512 --text-sim-bias`):**
  TEXT arm, ρ=0.94 (the predicted crossing): `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --subsample-train-frac 0.94 --subsample-mode interaction --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TITR_text_rho094_s08_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_TITR_text_rho094_s08_VG.log 2>&1`
  ID-only twin, ρ=0.94: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --no-sbert --subsample-train-frac 0.94 --subsample-mode interaction --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TITR_idonly_rho094_s08_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_TITR_idonly_rho094_s08_VG.log 2>&1`
  Then repeat both arms for ρ ∈ {0.97, 0.91, 0.88, 0.78} (swap `--subsample-train-frac` and the `rhoXXX` filename token) and for `--seed 20260609`. **Sequencing (binding): run ONLY when the GPU is free — must NOT contend with the live Beauty 3rd-point job, and run as a continuation of the in-flight 2026-06-18-1 ladder (these rungs FILL its 1.0→0.85 gap; do not re-run rungs already on disk).**

- **Gate:** This is a MEASUREMENT/prediction artifact, not a SOTA lever — the ">0.0660→5-seed→0.0760" gate does NOT apply (overall NDCG falls under thinning by design). Gate = does the fine-bracketed curve let the fit DISCRIMINATE the shape class: smoke 2ep each rung (no NaN, `by_popularity` with FROZEN full-density terciles, realized interactions/item printed) → full 40ep both arms at the 5 added rungs → analyst fit. **PASS (mechanism adjudicated)** if AIC/BIC clearly prefer one of the three forms AND the Hill-slope n + plateau test agree (n≈1 + no-plateau ⇒ A; n≫1 + plateau ⇒ B-cont; step ⇒ B-1st). **PARTIAL** if the ~110-hit tail noise leaves shape ambiguous but the monotone crossing + a fitted ρ_c-with-CI still land (report the band, not a point).

- **Kill criterion:** (1) If the refined rungs show the tail Δ is **flat/negative across the entire ladder** (no crossing even at MI-density), the *causal* density thesis is refuted on the controlled axis (this proposal then contributes only the pre-registered-and-refuted prediction — itself an honest, publishable negative, and it is decided by 2026-06-18-1's own kill clause). (2) If the fit cannot separate A from B even with the bracket rungs (overlapping AIC, CI on n spans [1, ∞)), report "shape under-determined by a ~110-hit tail at 6–11 rungs; the robust claim is the monotone sign-flip + fitted ρ_c band" — do NOT over-claim a critical exponent. (3) Do NOT assert α_c=1.0 as exact: it is prior-dependent — pre-register the **band α_c∈[0.7, 1.1]** (⇒ ρ_c∈[0.66, 1.04]) and let the fit locate it. (4) Finite-size collapse claims require BOTH catalog sizes; without the optional 2nd ladder, present the slope/plateau as the affordable core and the collapse as the deferred gold-standard.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Not an NDCG lever — its value is converting the titration from "we observed a sign-flip" into "we measured a phase transition at the density our theory predicted, and the curve SHAPE says it is collective (not a per-item crossover)." This is the single highest-value research contribution available now: it is the supervisor's verbatim #1 ask, it rides a sanctioned in-flight experiment at near-zero extra cost (5 cheap thinned rungs + a CPU fit), and it makes the paper's keystone figure — *tail Δ vs density with a fitted critical form, the pre-registered ρ_c≈0.94 marked, the 3 natural datasets sitting on the curve, and (if run) a two-size data-collapse* — reviewer-proof on the "is this a real law or a 3-point coincidence" axis. **Honest novelty caveat (state in the paper):** Landau β=½, the BBP `1−1/λ²` overlap, all-or-nothing MMSE jumps, and Fisher–Barber collapse are textbook physics/probability — zero novelty in the ingredients; the defensible narrow novelty is (i) mapping the recsys cold-start titration to an order-parameter dose–response, (ii) pre-registering competing critical shapes and discriminating the campaign's two live mechanisms by Hill-slope/plateau/collapse, and (iii) the falsifiable ρ_c≈0.94 number. Realistic outcome: the tail-noise may leave shape ambiguous (then the deliverable degrades gracefully to the monotone crossing + ρ_c band that 2026-06-18-1 already targets — no loss), but if the bracket resolves a plateau+sharp-drop it upgrades the law from "global density correlates with the tail Δ" to "the catalog sits on a measurable side of a collective recovery transition," the strongest possible form of the contribution.
