## PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-3 — Natural-variation 4th LAW POINT: a SMALL-catalog, globally-SPARSE AR2023 category (Digital_Music primary) as a 2nd subcritical dataset predicted tail-WIN  [status: DEAD 2026-07-02 — KILL-CRITERION-1 FIRED (proposal's own "no small+sparse AR2023 category clears the stats gate ⇒ report 'no clean 2nd-sparse natural point at small catalog size' and rely on the titration"). Stats-gate results (CPU, this cycle): (a) Digital_Music raw jsonl NOT on disk; external download FORBIDDEN by global rules ⇒ primary category unrunnable. (b) All_Beauty 5-core (on disk) = 253 users / 356 items / 2,535 inter — FAILS (n_items 356 < 3K, total 2,535 < 25K = degenerate). (c) Amazon_Fashion preprocessed to 5-core this cycle (preprocess_5core_standard.py, 10.3s): recursive 5-core COLLAPSES TO 0/0/0 (empty — AR2023 Fashion is too sparse to survive 5-core; nearly all users have 1 review) — FAILS. (d) Books = 19GB, not a small catalog. ⇒ No on-disk small+sparse AR2023 category yields a clean 2nd subcritical natural point; the one that might (Digital_Music) is download-blocked. Empty Amazon_Fashion split files removed (self-created this cycle, degenerate, not a result JSON). The dataset-conditional tail law's positive arm remains anchored on the controlled within-VG density titration (2026-06-18-1/-2) + the single MI natural endpoint, as the proposal itself prescribes on this outcome. HONEST NEGATIVE — a valid result. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-02.]

> **READ THIS FIRST — role note.** This grounds the supervisor's **cycle-5/-6 RESEARCHERS-#2 ask** ("a 4th small-catalog SPARSE AR2023 point … pick a SMALL catalog so full-catalog eval is cheap — the Beauty 62× lesson"). It is the **natural-variation** complement to the **interventional** titration (2026-06-18-1) — observational + interventional together identify the causal density effect. It is NOT a model lever (it runs the EXISTING V2 text-vs-ID tail contrast on a NEW dataset, same as the MI/VG/Beauty arms already on disk) ⇒ closed-lever-compliant. It does require a one-time SBERT/TAPE text-feature encode for the new category (the same precompute already done for MI/VG/Beauty), which is inference-side, leak-free.

- **Idea (novel):** The law currently has THREE points — MI sparse→tail-WIN (5/5), VG dense→null, Beauty dense→null — a 1-win/2-null density ordering. Its weakest empirical feature is that the lone *positive* endpoint is a single dataset (MI). A **second globally-sparse dataset** that ALSO shows a tail-WIN would convert "MI is special" into "sparse catalogs win the tail" — directly hardening the law's positive arm, and giving XF-18-3's phase diagram a 2nd subcritical point. AR2023 full-corpus statistics ([amazon-reviews-2023.github.io](https://amazon-reviews-2023.github.io/)) identify several categories far sparser than VG/Beauty: **Digital_Music** (≈70.5K users / ≈130.4K ratings — ratings/user ≈1.85, the lowest of the usable benchmarks), Gift_Cards, Magazine_Subscriptions, Subscription_Boxes. Digital_Music is the strongest candidate: a *real, commonly-used* recsys benchmark (unlike the near-degenerate Gift_Cards/Subscription_Boxes), globally sparse, and — crucially — its 5-core catalog is SMALL (≈thousands of items, ≪ Beauty's 207K) ⇒ full-catalog masked eval is CHEAP, avoiding Beauty's 62× cost.

- **Source(s):** AR2023 per-category statistics — [Amazon Reviews'23 (McAuley Lab / Hou 2024)](https://amazon-reviews-2023.github.io/), [HF dataset card](https://huggingface.co/datasets/McAuley-Lab/Amazon-Reviews-2023). The sparse→tail-WIN prediction = the campaign's own dataset-conditional law (global ID-density governs; MI 16.2 interactions/item → win, VG 24.5 / Beauty 24.9 → null) + XF-18-3's recovery-transition `α=(interactions/item)/d_eff` (MI subcritical ≈0.70). Eval protocol/baseline = the on-disk MI/VG/Beauty tail-contrast methodology (5-core LLOO, full-catalog masked NDCG@10, `by_popularity` train-frequency terciles, paired text−ID).

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** The MI/VG/Beauty tail contrasts (on disk) are the same machinery; this differs only in the DATASET — a new natural sample at a new global density. It complements 2026-06-18-1 (controlled, within-VG density axis) by adding an *independent* natural point: if a thinned-VG titration and an unrelated sparse category (Digital_Music) BOTH land tail-positive at MI-like density, the confound objection ("MI's category is special") dies twice over. Not a duplicate of any queued proposal.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** No code change. (1) **Encode the new category's text features** (one-time, the same script used for MI/VG/Beauty: `encode_blair_5core.py` / the richtext encoder over the category's 5-core item descriptions) so the SBERT/TAPE caches exist. (2) **Stats gate FIRST (cheap, decides go/no-go and which category):** run the loader's stats print on Digital_Music 5-core and confirm **(a) catalog small enough for cheap full eval (target n_items ≲ 50K, ideally ≲ 20K)** and **(b) globally sparse (interactions/item < ~18, i.e. ≤ MI's 16.2, predicting subcritical)**. If Digital_Music's 5-core is too tiny/noisy (n_items < ~3K or total interactions < ~25K ⇒ a ~30-hit-class tail like MI but noisier), fall back to the next small+sparse AR2023 category meeting the gate (Magazine_Subscriptions, else a small low-interactions/item category); if NONE clears the gate, report that as the finding (no clean 2nd-sparse natural point exists at small catalog size) and defer to the titration. (3) **Run the paired text−ID tail contrast, ≥3 seeds** (a corroborating point, like Beauty — does not need MI's 5-seed bar).

- **Exact command (executor runs this — TEXT and ID-only arms, seed 20260608; repeat for seeds 20260609/20260610; replace `Digital_Music` with the gated category if it falls back):**
  TEXT: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Digital_Music --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TAIL_V2_text_DigitalMusic_s08.json > _bestrec_run/run_TAIL_text_DigitalMusic_s08.log 2>&1`
  ID-only: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Digital_Music --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --no-sbert --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_TAIL_idonly_DigitalMusic_s08.json > _bestrec_run/run_TAIL_idonly_DigitalMusic_s08.log 2>&1`
  **Sequencing (binding): LOWEST priority — strictly behind the live Beauty 3rd point AND the 2026-06-18-1/-2 VG titration (the titration is the higher-value causal artifact). Run only when the GPU is otherwise idle.**

- **Gate:** Stats gate (above) FIRST — abort/fall-back if the catalog isn't small or isn't sparse. Then: smoke 2ep (no NaN, `by_popularity` emitted, full-catalog n_eval = the category's user count) → full 40ep × ≥3 seeds both arms → report tail/mid/head **NDCG + HR + seed-level CI** and the paired text−ID **tail Δ**. Not a SOTA lever — the ">0.0660/0.0760" gate does NOT apply.

- **Kill criterion:** (1) No small+sparse AR2023 category clears the stats gate ⇒ report "no clean 2nd-sparse natural point at small catalog size" and rely on the titration (still a clean outcome). (2) If a sparse category clears the gate but its tail Δ is NULL/negative (≥3-seed, CI straddles 0) ⇒ **density alone is insufficient to predict the tail win** — MI's win is then NOT purely global-ID-density-driven, the governing statistic is still open, and that REFUTES (or at least complicates) the law's positive arm — a decisive, publishable negative the paper must report honestly (do NOT fish for a favorable bucketing or seed). (3) Honesty: a small catalog ⇒ a tiny (~tens-of-hits) tail bucket ⇒ report tail HR + bootstrap/seed CI and the absolute-vs-relative framing (per the standing FIX); a large RELATIVE tail lift on a tiny base is not "text rescues the tail" unqualified.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** Not an NDCG lever — its value is a **2nd independent subcritical (sparse) data point** to harden the law's lone positive endpoint (currently MI-only) and to give XF-18-3's `(α, d·λ²)` phase diagram a 2nd below-threshold dataset. Pre-registered prediction (the law + α≈0.70-class density): Digital_Music shows a **positive paired text−ID tail Δ** (sign-matching MI), with text's largest ABSOLUTE lift still on the head/overall (per the standing absolute-vs-relative honesty FIX). Most-probable outcome: a sparse category that clears the gate confirms the sparse→win arm (strengthens the law) OR cleanly refutes it (density insufficient — equally publishable). Cheap by construction (small catalog ⇒ cheap full eval, the explicit Beauty-lesson design goal); lowest-priority sequencing so it never contends with the higher-value Beauty/titration work. Honest novelty caveat: this is a *replication-on-a-new-dataset* contribution (no new method), valuable precisely because the law currently leans on N=3 with a single positive — its worth is in breadth/robustness, not novelty.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1 — USER-MODE density titration (thin USERS, not interactions): a pre-registered head/tail × interaction/user DOUBLE-DISSOCIATION to test users/item (collaborative connectivity) as the tail-law's binding resource  [status: DONE — connectivity (users/item) CONFIRMED PARTIAL tail cause (user-mode titration 5-seed, matched-R1 ρ_user=0.66, both arms s08–s12; tail Δ +0.000178±0.000137, 5/5 pos, t≈2.6; 5×5 (mode×stratum) diff-of-diffs +0.000761, opposite-sign ⇒ DOUBLE-DISSOCIATION; non-monotone collapse below MI-connectivity + lands ≈halfway to MI's +0.000335 ⇒ residual content factor). THE ONE FINAL sanctioned mechanism experiment ⇒ mechanism search CLOSED. — original RUNNING note: GPU freed (Beauty job closed). No-op gate satisfied by code (user-mode block is inside `if subsample_train_frac<1.0`). 2ep smoke (rho=0.40 user) PASS: no NaN, mode=user interactions/item 9.74 (full 24.4), eval histories+terciles FROZEN, by_popularity emitted. > **READ THIS FIRST — role note.** This grounds cross-field idea **XF 2026-06-19-1** (Liebig's Law of the Minimum + Tilman resource-ratio R\* + the ecology co-limitation form taxonomy) AND it is the **analyst's verbatim #1-ranked next experiment** (ANALYSIS_LOG -7/-8 missing-ablation #1: *"the `--subsample-mode user` VG titration … the single highest-value next experiment"*) AND the supervisor's cycle-6 RESEARCHERS retarget (*"what MI-specific property, co-varying with but distinct from global interactions/item, drives the TAIL win? … lead candidate users/item, testable by a `--subsample-mode user` titration"*). It is a **DATA-ablation on an ALREADY-IMPLEMENTED loader flag** (`--subsample-mode user`, `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1333,1596-1598`) — zero new params, no arch/loss/scoring change ⇒ **closed-lever-compliant** (the *user-mode sibling* of the already-sanctioned interaction-mode titration 2026-06-18-1, which the supervisor explicitly classed a data-ablation, NOT a model lever). Higher-value than the queued natural-variation Digital_Music point (2026-06-18-3) because it is **causal** (within ONE dataset, category/encoder/eval/catalog all fixed) and tests a **different, untested resource axis** (connectivity R2, not count R1).

- **Idea (novel):** The interaction-mode titration (2026-06-18-1) **REFUTED** global interactions/item as the *cause* of the tail win (thinning VG interactions toward MI's density drove the text−ID tail Δ *more negative*, 0/2 positive at the MI-equiv rung ρ=0.66) while **confirming** it as the cause of the *head/overall* text-complementarity (monotone, 11/11 cells positive). So the MI tail win is a **density-IRREDUCIBLE, MI-specific residual**, and its mechanism is open. The lead candidate the analyst flagged is **users/item** (MI 2.34 ≪ VG 3.70 ≈ Beauty 3.51) — a *different* data resource that interaction-thinning **barely moves**. The ecological **two-resource co-limitation** frame (Liebig/Tilman) makes this a sharp, falsifiable hypothesis: ID-embedding learnability is fed by two non-substitutable resources — **R1 = interactions/item** (per-item sample count) and **R2 = users/item** (collaborative connectivity / bipartite item-degree = the co-occurrence edges that carry gradient signal between items). The interaction ladder relieved **R1** and moved the **HEAD** but not the tail ⇒ by Liebig (depleting a *non-binding* resource yields no response), **R1 is NOT the tail's binding resource** — so the tail's binding resource must be **R2 = connectivity**. A USER-thinning ladder lowers R2 (drops whole users ⇒ fewer distinct users bridge each item) while **preserving each surviving user's full history** (no sequence-length confound), directly testing it. This reconciles three facts no single prior framing explains together: (i) tail-LOCAL exposure is FLAT at the 5-core floor (4/4/5 — refuted as a predictor), (ii) interactions/item drives the head but not the tail, (iii) users/item ranks with the tail Δ — by saying *the head and the tail are limited by different data resources* (Tilman supply-ratio switch: head items sit at a count-limited supply ratio, tail items at a connectivity-limited one).

- **Source(s):** Liebig's Law of the Minimum — Sprengel 1828 / von Liebig 1840 (the "barrel," single scarcest resource gates growth). Tilman resource-ratio (R\*) theory — Tilman 1980/1982 (which resource limits is set by the *supply ratio*; the binding resource *switches* along a gradient). Co-limitation taxonomy & empirical form-selection — Harpole et al. 2011, *Ecology Letters* (simultaneous/independent/serial co-limitation); Tang & Riley 2021, *Ecological Applications* "Finding Liebig's law of the minimum" (AIC model-selection of Liebig-MIN vs additive vs synthesizing-unit forms from fertilization assays); Sperfeld et al. 2012, *Ecology Letters*. The recsys connectivity-as-resource reading is the campaign's own measurement (ANALYSIS_LOG -6/-7/-8: users/item ranks with the tail Δ; tail-local exposure flat) + the bipartite item-degree / co-occurrence-graph literature ([topology-aware graph-CF analysis, arXiv:2308.10778]; [promoting cold-start items via bipartite degree, arXiv:1404.4936]). **Honest CS prior-art caveat:** cold-start work routinely bins *users* by interaction count and reports per-group accuracy (single-scalar sparsity); **no recsys work runs a user-thinning vs interaction-thinning DOUBLE-DISSOCIATION to identify which data resource limits which popularity stratum** — that two-resource co-limitation framing is the narrow defensible novelty (the ecology ingredients are textbook; frame it as the mapping + the pre-registered dissociation, not "ecology for recsys").

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** **(1) PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-1 (interaction-mode titration, DONE-refuted)** — same loader knob, but `--subsample-mode interaction` (drops random interactions, keeps all users, *shortens* histories) thins **R1**; this uses `--subsample-mode user` (drops whole users, keeps full histories) to thin **R2**. Different resource axis, different (untested) prediction. **(2) PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-3 (Digital_Music 4th point, QUEUED)** — that is *observational* natural-variation (a new sparse dataset); this is *interventional/causal* on VG with every confounder fixed. They are complementary (observational + interventional), not duplicates. **(3) The MI/VG/Beauty tail contrasts (on disk)** — same eval machinery; this adds the *causal connectivity axis* none of them isolate. No queued or on-disk artifact thins users or tests R2.

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** **NO code change** — the flag exists and is verified. `--subsample-mode user` (`run_sasrec_sbert.py:1596-1598`) keeps each user with prob `--subsample-train-frac` and **retains all of that user's interactions** (full history preserved); the TITRATION print (`:1606-1610`) reports kept interactions/item; **eval `user_seqs`/`user_times` and the tail/mid/head terciles stay FROZEN at full density** (`:1590-1593`, `train_user_seqs` is a *separate* thinned object; eval is untouched) ⇒ leak-free by construction. `--subsample-train-frac 1.0` ⇒ `train_user_seqs = user_seqs` ⇒ **bit-identical no-op** (`:1592-1593`). Math of the ladder: dropping fraction (1−ρ) of users scales **users/item ≈ ρ·3.70** and **interactions/item ≈ ρ·24.5** (both fall together — handled by the matched-R1 contrast below). Pre-register the rung ladder so users/item brackets MI's 2.34 and goes below: **ρ_user ∈ {1.0(on-disk baseline), 0.85, 0.66, 0.50, 0.40}** ⇒ users/item ≈ {3.70, 3.15, **2.44≈MI**, 1.85, 1.48}. Fix `--subsample-seed 0` (same thinned population across model seeds ⇒ isolates model-init noise); run **BOTH arms × ≥2 model seeds** {20260608, 20260609} per rung; escalate a rung to seed 20260610 only if its tail Δ turns positive (mirrors the interaction-ladder protocol). **The decisive comparison is the matched-R1 contrast at ρ=0.66:** user-mode ρ=0.66 and interaction-mode ρ=0.66 keep the *same* interactions/item (≈16, both MI-equiv on R1) but user-mode has *lower* users/item (≈2.44 vs interaction-mode's ≈3.4, which barely moved) — so any tail-Δ *difference* between the two modes at ρ=0.66 is attributable to **R2/connectivity alone** (the recsys analogue of a matched negative-control design). Report **R1 (interactions/item) AND R2 (users/item) per rung** (both printed by the loader) so the analyst can build the matched contrast and the co-limitation fit.

- **Exact command (executor runs this — the matched-R1 headline rung ρ=0.66, BOTH arms, seed 20260608; then sweep the ladder and seed 20260609 by substituting the two values; ρ=1.0 is the on-disk V2 baseline, no rerun needed):**
  TEXT (V2): `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --subsample-train-frac 0.66 --subsample-mode user --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_USERTITR_text_rho066_s08_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_USERTITR_text_rho066_s08_VG.log 2>&1`
  ID-only: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --no-sbert --subsample-train-frac 0.66 --subsample-mode user --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_USERTITR_idonly_rho066_s08_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_USERTITR_idonly_rho066_s08_VG.log 2>&1`
  Ladder: repeat both arms for `--subsample-train-frac` ∈ {0.85, 0.50, 0.40} and `--seed` ∈ {20260608, 20260609} (rename `rho066`/`s08` in `--out` accordingly). **Sequencing (binding): runs AFTER the live Beauty 3rd point closes; ONE GPU job at a time; must NOT contend with Beauty. Higher priority than the Digital_Music 4th point (2026-06-18-3) because it is causal; comparable to / ahead of finishing optional titration rungs.** Each thinned run trains on *less* data ⇒ cheaper than full VG (~7–9 min/40ep).

- **Gate:** (1) **No-op check FIRST** — confirm `--subsample-train-frac 1.0 --subsample-mode user` reproduces the on-disk VG V2 result bit-for-bit (true no-op; the code guarantees it, but verify once). (2) Smoke 2ep at the lowest rung ρ=0.40 (no NaN; `by_popularity` emitted; the TITRATION print confirms users/item dropped to ≈1.48 and terciles frozen). (3) Full 40ep × both arms × ≥2 seeds per rung. Report per rung: **R1, R2, tail/mid/head NDCG@10 + HR@10 + seed-level CI**, and the paired **text−ID tail Δ** vs users/item. **This is NOT a SOTA lever — the >0.0660 / >0.0760 escalation gate does NOT apply** (the metric is the text−ID tail Δ as a function of connectivity, not an absolute NDCG record).

- **Kill criterion / decision (pre-registered DOUBLE-DISSOCIATION — both outcomes decisive & publishable):**
  - **CONFIRM (connectivity is the tail's binding resource):** user-mode tail Δ **RISES toward positive** as users/item falls toward/below MI's 2.34 (a null→positive crossing the interaction ladder MISSED), AND the **matched-R1 contrast at ρ=0.66 is positive** (user-mode tail Δ > interaction-mode tail Δ at equal R1, ≥2 seeds, with the head dose-response comparatively flat) ⇒ **users/item (collaborative connectivity), not interactions/item, is the tail's binding resource** — a CAUSAL tail law resurrected on the correct axis, the single most valuable repair the campaign can make. Escalate the crossing rung to a 3rd seed and report the (R1,R2) Tilman-plane figure + the co-limitation form-fit.
  - **REFUTE (clean negative, equally publishable):** user-mode tail Δ stays NULL/negative across the ladder AND at the matched-R1 ρ=0.66 contrast (≥2 seeds, CI straddles 0) ⇒ **neither global density resource binds the tail** ⇒ the MI tail win is driven by a non-density, MI-idiosyncratic property (text-domain/category structure), which HARDENS the honest-negative section and redirects the in-field search away from density toward MI content. Do NOT fish a crossing from a single noisy rung/seed; the ~110-hit VG tail bucket demands the sign + monotone trend + the dissociation as the robust claim.
  - **DEAD (process):** if `--subsample-mode user` is found to also shorten eval histories or shift tercile membership (it must not — verify the TITRATION print + an eval n_eval unchanged from full VG), STOP and report the leak risk rather than proceeding.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** This is a **causal data-ablation, not an NDCG lever** — its value is identifying *which data resource limits the tail*, the open mechanism the paper now needs. Mechanistic prior (Liebig/Tilman): interaction-thinning relieved the head's binding resource (R1=count) but not the tail's, *exactly* matching the observed head-moves/tail-doesn't split ⇒ the tail's binding resource is the other one (R2=connectivity), which user-thinning targets. **Realistic outcome, honestly:** the VG tail bucket is ~110 hits/seed and noisy — the ROBUST deliverable is the **shape (does user-mode move the tail where interaction-mode didn't?) + the matched-R1 double-dissociation table**, with the fitted R2\* threshold and the AIC-selected co-limitation form (Liebig-MIN vs multiplicative vs additive, pooling both ladders, analyst-side read-only ≈40-line CPU fit) as *secondary* with seed CIs. Two honest headwinds: (1) user-thinning lowers R1 as well as R2 (not a pure knob) — handled by the matched-R1 contrast; (2) the prediction may simply fail (user-mode also nulls the tail) — that is the clean REFUTE branch above, equally publishable and arguably more valuable (it closes density entirely and points at MI content). Either branch yields the paper's **(R1,R2) Tilman resource-supply plane** figure (VG/MI/Beauty placed by their measured resources, with the titration arrow) and resolves the titration's otherwise-puzzling head-not-tail result. Cheap (thinned VG, ~7–9 min/run), leak-free (train-only thinning; eval + terciles frozen), non-collapsible (no learnable scalar), closed-lever-compliant (loader knob, no model change).

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-2 — RIGIDITY/ISOSTATIC ADDENDUM to 2026-06-19-1: a sharp-knee read-out + a parameter-free per-item floppiness predictor `φ_i` (NO new GPU run; closed-lever-, convergence-compliant)  [status: PARTIAL DONE (CPU φ_i artifact run today; knee read-out deliverables 1-2 await 2026-06-19-1's GPU titration). Ran measure_floppiness_phi.py (read-only, train-CSV only, no GPU — alongside the busy supervisor job). φ_i=max(0,r_eff−indep_i)/r_eff, indep_i=distinct train users/item, r_eff∈{22,23,24}. (1) ORDERING test (tail-mean φ, r_eff23): MI 0.8223 > VG 0.8148 > Beauty 0.8008 — MI (the tail-WIN dataset) IS most floppy, Beauty least, WEAKLY matching the tail-Δ ordering. BUT the tail is 100% floppy in ALL datasets (indep≈5 ≪ r_eff=23 ⇒ saturated) so tail-φ barely discriminates (2% spread). Clearer separation at the MID tercile: MI 0.659 ≫ VG 0.592 / Beauty 0.562 — consistent with the campaign's MID-regime text win (TAPE-driven). (2) STRUCTURAL FINDING undercutting the framing's premise: distinct-users/item EXACTLY equals interactions/item (VG 24.49=24.49, MI 16.16=16.16, Beauty 24.91=24.91) ⇒ these 5-core LLOO splits have NO repeat user-item pairs ⇒ indep_i ≡ freq_i. So the rigidity "interaction-count = redundant repeat-user self-stress" account (deliverable 2) has NO purchase here, and interaction-mode vs user-mode thinning both strip distinct-user constraints (they differ only in scattered-vs-whole-user removal). Tests (i)/(iii) (per-item φ vs per-item text−ID advantage) NOT runnable — needs per-item NDCG not in result JSONs. Verdict: φ_i WEAKLY supports the rigidity ordering (MI most floppy, esp. mid) but is saturated at the tail and its self-stress premise doesn't hold on dedup'd data; a mild paper-grade predictor + an honest caveat, not a strong law. Files: measure_floppiness_phi.py, run_FLOPPINESS_phi.log. 2026-06-19]

> **READ THIS FIRST — role note (convergence-compliant, NOT a new experiment).** Per SUPERVISOR cycle-7 (2026-06-19) the lever search is CLOSED, the **user-mode titration (PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1) is THE ONE FINAL sanctioned mechanism experiment**, and RESEARCHERS are retargeted to **paper-grade support only** ("turn the co-limitation framing into the (R1,R2) Tilman-plane figure caption + the threshold theory's predicted curve SHAPE for whichever branch the user-mode titration takes"). This block obeys that directive literally: it grounds cross-field idea **XF 2026-06-19-2** (Maxwell–Calladine constraint counting / rigidity percolation / Singer–Cucuringu rigidity↔matrix-completion) as a **read-only prediction + analyst-side predictor ADDENDUM riding the SAME single decisive run that 2026-06-19-1 already specifies** — it commissions **NO new GPU job, NO model lever, NO new dataset, NO new learnable parameter**. It sharpens the *read-out* of 2026-06-19-1 and adds one read-only CPU artifact the analyst can run on EXISTING results today. If the supervisor's convergence directive is read strictly as "ground the user-mode prediction, then STOP proposing experiments," this is the *grounding of that prediction's mechanism*, not a new experiment — and it is explicitly framed so the experiment agent runs **nothing extra** for it.

- **Idea (paper-grade mechanism + sharper prediction, not a lever):** Rigidity theory supplies the *mechanism* XF 2026-06-19-1 (Liebig two-resource co-limitation) only labels, and upgrades its **monotone** user-mode prediction to a **sharp, falsifiable one**. The learned ID-embedding table is a low-rank factorization; by **Singer–Cucuringu (2010)** completing/pinning an item's `d`-dim vector is a *rigidity* problem in which **inner products play the role distances play in bar-frameworks** — the bipartite observation (user×item) graph must be **RIGID, not merely connected**, to fix the factor uniquely. Each observed interaction is a **bar-constraint**; **Maxwell–Calladine index counting** (`f − s = D·N − C`) says only **independent** constraints remove a floppy degree of freedom, while **redundant** constraints (extra interactions from users already constraining the item) add a **state of self-stress, not rigidity**. This is the rigorous reason the interaction-mode titration moved the HEAD but **not the tail**: raw interaction count on a tail item is mostly *redundant repeat-user self-stress* (and the campaign already measured tail-local raw count FLAT at the 5-core floor, 4/4/5, a pure-count theory cannot explain), whereas **distinct-user connectivity = independent-constraint supply** is what actually rigidifies. A tail item with too few distinct users sits **below its isostatic point → floppy zero-modes → high-variance untrustworthy ID vector → frozen-text scaffold complements it (tail win)**.

- **Source(s):** Singer & Cucuringu 2010, *Uniqueness of Low-Rank Matrix Completion by Rigidity Theory*, SIAM J. Matrix Anal. 31(4):1621–1641 (arXiv:0902.3846) — DOF `r(m+n−r)`, completion matrix = rigidity-matrix analogue, **rigid (not just connected) bipartite observation graph required**, **collaborative-filtering named as an application** (verified this cycle: inner-products↔distances, completion-matrix↔rigidity-matrix, bipartite observation graph). Maxwell 1864 (constraint counting); Calladine 1978 (`f−s=D·N−C` index theorem, self-stress=redundancy); Thorpe 1983 + Jacobs–Thorpe pebble-game 1995 (rigidity percolation / isostaticity in network glasses); rigidity-percolation-above-connectivity in **central-force/constraint networks** (arXiv:cond-mat/9807001 — *verified this cycle, with the honest caveat that the strict inequality is bonding-condition-dependent: it holds for central-force constraint networks, and collapses to equality once bending/angular constraints couple adjacent bonds*). Novelty check this cycle: 2024–2026 sequential-recsys cold-start work (content-init "Let It Go? Not Quite" arXiv:2507.19473; popularity-aware meta-learning arXiv:2411.11225; etc.) uses content/contrastive/meta methods — **none invoke rigidity/constraint-counting/floppy-modes**, confirming the framing is absent from the field.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** **(1) PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1 (Liebig two-resource co-limitation)** — same single decisive run, but it predicts only a *monotone* "tail Δ rises as users/item falls" and labels the two resources without a mechanism for *why raw count is wasted*. Rigidity says **which interactions count** (independent vs redundant), supplies the **self-stress** account of the flat-raw-count finding, and predicts a **SHARP isostatic KNEE** (not a slope) at a *pre-registered* `u*` that sits **ABOVE the connectivity-percolation point**. **(2) XF 2026-06-18-3 / the BBP-ρ work** — a *spectral/collective detectability* transition (signal eigenvalue vs MP bulk, a global order parameter `α`/`d·λ²`); rigidity is a **combinatorial/geometric per-item local-completability** transition (a single node's floppy modes, the completion-submatrix rank) that yields a **per-item `φ_i`** the global spectral view cannot. **(3) XF-17-5 connectivity percolation** — connectivity is *exactly the threshold rigidity exceeds*; the headline prediction (tail fails **while still connected**) is the content XF-17-5 lacks. No queued/on-disk artifact computes a per-item independent-constraint floppiness predictor or predicts a sharp knee above connectivity.

- **Mechanism / how to implement (NO `run_sasrec_sbert.py` change; rides 2026-06-19-1's run + one read-only CPU script):**
  1. **Sharper read-out spec for the user-mode titration (add to 2026-06-19-1's report; zero extra runs).** Pre-register, from the ID-table SVD spectrum, an **effective collaborative rank `r_eff`** (participation ratio `(Σσ_i)²/Σσ_i²`, or the Gavish–Donoho hard threshold — the campaign already measured `d_eff≈22–24`) and the **isostatic user-degree `u* ≈ r_eff`** (Singer–Cucuringu: ≳ `r_eff` independent bars per item to pin its factor). Prediction: VG's tail Δ stays ≈null while users/item > `u*` then **crosses null→positive ABRUPTLY (a knee, Hill slope `n≫1`)** as user-thinning drives users/item below `u*` — *distinguishable from XF-19-1's smooth monotone rise and from a per-item bias–variance crossover (smooth `n≈1`)*. Two discriminating teeth: **(a)** the knee should appear at a *higher* users/item than a naïve "graph disconnects" account (rigidity > connectivity); **(b)** check whether MI's 2.34 lands **below** `u*` and VG's 3.70 **above** — a *quantitative placement*, reported with a sensitivity band on `r_eff` (if `u*` brackets none of {MI 2.34, VG 3.70, Beauty 3.51} cleanly, fall back to the *ordering* claim — still publishable).
  2. **Self-stress interpretation of 2026-06-19-1's matched-R1 contrast (interpretation only).** At matched interactions/item the two modes differ only in *constraint independence*: user-thinning strips **independent** constraints (de-rigidifies → tail Δ moves), interaction-thinning strips mostly **redundant repeat-user** constraints (self-stress → tail Δ flat). So the predicted tail-Δ gap should point **in the direction of whichever mode removed more distinct users** — a strictly stronger, mechanism-level version of XF-19-1's prediction on the *same* run.
  3. **Parameter-free per-item floppiness predictor `φ_i` (analyst-side, read-only ≈30-line CPU, runnable on EXISTING runs TODAY — non-collapsible).** Per item: independent-constraint count `indep_i` = **number of distinct users who interacted with it** (train-only bipartite degree; sharper optional = generic rank of its stacked user-state rows = the Singer–Cucuringu completion-submatrix block); floppiness `φ_i = max(0, r_eff − indep_i)/r_eff ∈ [0,1]`. Testable now, no training: (i) `φ_i` should **correlate with per-item text−ID advantage better than raw frequency or users/item alone**; (ii) **tail-tercile mean `φ`** should be **high for MI, low for VG/Beauty**, tracking the tail-Δ ordering; (iii) within a dataset, items matched on raw count but differing in distinct-user count differ in text advantage in the `φ` direction.

Collapse-chi2/Crawford-labels/AIC still analyst-lane.** 2026-06-20]

> **READ THIS FIRST — role note (convergence-compliant; NOT a new experiment, NOT a lever).** Per SUPERVISOR cycle-7 (2026-06-19, AGENT_FEEDBACK) the lever/mechanism search is **CLOSED**, the user-mode titration (PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1, mechanism-sharpened by 2026-06-19-2) is **THE ONE FINAL sanctioned experiment**, and RESEARCHERS are retargeted to **paper-grade support only** ("turn the co-limitation framing into the (R1,R2) Tilman-plane figure caption + the threshold theory's predicted curve SHAPE for whichever branch the user-mode titration takes"). This block obeys that directive literally: it grounds cross-field idea **XF 2026-06-19-3** (cognitive-neuropsychology dissociation statistics + statistical-physics data-collapse quality) as the **read-only, analyst-side statistical-reporting protocol** for 2026-06-19-1's already-specified run. It commissions **NO new GPU job, NO model lever, NO new dataset, NO new learnable parameter, NO new run** — it specifies *how to read* the titration JSONs the experiment agent already produces, plus the figure caption/curve-shape the supervisor asked for. It is the convergence-phase deliverable, not a re-opening of the search.

- **Idea (paper-grade reporting protocol, not a mechanism or a lever):** The user-mode titration's pre-registered claim is a **double dissociation**: interaction-thinning (R1↓) moves the **HEAD not the tail** (already observed, ANALYSIS_LOG -7/-8); user-thinning (R2↓) is predicted to move the **TAIL not the head** (PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1). The single most likely *statistical* inflation in writing this up — the analogue of the val=0.0765 trap this pipeline exists to prevent — is to claim the dissociation from **two separate tests** ("user-mode tail Δ is significant; head Δ is not, therefore they dissociate"). That is the **Nieuwenhuis 2011 error** ("the difference between significant and non-significant is not itself significant"; 79/513 top-journal papers committed it). The correct, defensible object is a **single test on the INTERACTION** — a paired **difference-of-differences (here a triple difference: mode × stratum)** that must clear *its own* test, exactly as the rho=0.88 hump was (correctly) demoted under Bonferroni. This proposal pins down the three read-only statistics + the figure that convert the "double result" from inflatable into reviewer-proof, and the two pre-registered curve shapes the supervisor requested.

- **Source(s):** Nieuwenhuis, Forstmann & Wagenmakers 2011, *Nature Neuroscience* 14:1105–1107, "Erroneous analyses of interactions in neuroscience: a problem of significance" (a dissociation is established by **one interaction test**, never two separate sig/n.s. tests; 78 correct vs 79 incorrect of 513 audited; even more common in cellular/molecular) — *verified this cycle*. Crawford & Garthwaite single-case / dissociation methodology + Crawford, Garthwaite & Ryan 2011, *Cortex* (small-sample **classical vs strong** dissociation criteria; report the **standardized between-task difference** / BSDT with its CI, not two point estimates) — for the tiny ~30-hit (MI) / ~110-hit (VG) tail bases. Houdayer & Hartmann 2004 data-collapse **quality `S` = reduced-χ²** `S = (1/N) Σ (y−Y)²/(δy²+δY²)`, `S≈1` ⇒ genuine collapse, `S≫1` ⇒ wrong scaling variable (refining Kawashima–Ito; `pyfssa` implements it) — *verified this cycle (pyfssa docs)*. TOST equivalence testing (Lakens 2017, *Soc. Psych. Personality Sci.*; `statsmodels.stats.weightstats.ttost_ind`) for the powered-null reporting the bus already adopted for the VG tail (ANALYSIS_LOG: MDE₈₀ 0.00025 < MI's 0.00034) — *verified this cycle*. **Honest novelty caveat:** all four methods are textbook in their home fields (zero novelty in the ingredients); the narrow defensible contribution is **importing the dissociation-interaction discipline + the data-collapse-quality statistic into a sequential-recsys long-tail result** (recsys evaluates with overall-metric deltas and ad-hoc per-slice eyeballing; it has no culture of the interaction/difference-of-differences test or of quantifying curve-collapse). Frame it as the *reporting protocol*, not an invention.

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** **(1) PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1** specifies the *run* and a paired Δ-of-Δ-vs-full-density sign-test for the tail trend; it does **not** specify the *cross-mode INTERACTION* (mode × stratum) test that actually licenses the word "dissociation," nor a collapse-quality number for its (R1,R2) figure, nor small-sample dissociation labels. **(2) PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-2** supplies the rigidity *mechanism* and the per-item `φ_i` predictor; it predicts a sharp *knee* but does not give the *statistical test* that the predicted dissociation/knee is real vs noise. **(3) The on-bus statistical toolkit** already has paired Δ-of-Δ sign-tests (head-cause), a Bonferroni demotion (rho=0.88), and TOST/MDE (VG tail null) — but **no explicit difference-of-differences interaction test for a *dissociation*, no data-collapse-quality `S`, and no Crawford classical-vs-strong labels.** This is the reporting layer none of them provide; it is the measurement/honesty complement to 2026-06-19-1/-2, not a duplicate.

- **Mechanism / how to implement (NO `run_sasrec_sbert.py` change; one analyst-side read-only ≈40-line pandas/numpy script over the titration `results_*` JSONs; runs on CPU alongside any live GPU job):**
  1. **The interaction (dissociation) test — the load-bearing honesty guard.** Do **not** report "user-mode tail Δ>0 (sig) while head Δ≈0 (n.s.) ⇒ dissociation" (the Nieuwenhuis error). Instead, **per model seed `s`**, compute the paired **triple difference at the matched-R1 rung** (ρ=0.66, where user-mode and interaction-mode share interactions/item so only R2/connectivity differs): `DDD_s = (textΔ_tail^user − textΔ_head^user) − (textΔ_tail^inter − textΔ_head^inter)`, where each `textΔ_bucket^mode = NDCG_text − NDCG_idonly` on that bucket/mode/seed. Run the campaign's established **paired sign-test + paired t** on `{DDD_s}` (≥5 seeds). **Claim a dissociation ONLY if `DDD` clears its own test** (report mean, 95% CI, npos/N, t). Replicate on **HR@10** (the bus's metric-robustness discipline). This is the campaign's paired-Δ-of-Δ machinery, one level deeper.
  2. **The data-collapse number for the (R1,R2) Tilman-plane figure.** Plot **all rungs of BOTH titration modes** as (a) tail-Δ vs **users/item (R2)** and (b) tail-Δ vs **interactions/item (R1)**, and report the **Houdayer–Hartmann reduced-χ² `S`** (seed-std as δy; loess/spline master curve as Y) for each. The binding-resource claim is **confirmed iff `S(vs R2) ≈ 1 ≪ S(vs R1)`** (collapse-on-connectivity, divergence-on-count) — both modes' tail-Δ curves lie on ONE curve vs the *binding* resource and scatter vs the *non-binding* one. Converts "the curves line up" from an eyeball into a falsifiable statistic and writes the figure's caption.
  3. **Small-base dissociation reporting (Crawford–Garthwaite) + TOST for the ~30–110-hit tail.** Report the **standardized between-stratum difference** with a bootstrap/seed CI (BSDT spirit), and label explicitly: **strong** = both strata move in opposite, individually-reliable directions; **classical** = one stratum moves and the *difference* is reliable, the other merely not-detectably-moved; **none** = `DDD` CI includes 0. For any null stratum, give the **TOST** equivalence verdict against the ±MI-effect (0.00034) bound with its MDE₈₀ — so a null is stated as a *powered equivalence*, exactly as the VG-tail null already is.
  4. **Pre-registered curve shapes (the supervisor's explicit ask — discriminates the three live mechanisms on the SAME figure).** Pre-commit, before reading the user-mode JSONs, to three distinguishable tail-Δ-vs-R2 shapes: **(A) sharp isostatic KNEE** (Hill slope `n≫1`, abrupt null→positive near `u*≈r_eff≈22–24`, MI's 2.34 below / VG's 3.70 above) ⇒ rigidity/floppy-mode causal tail law (2026-06-19-2); **(B) smooth monotone rise** (`n≈1`, gradual crossing) ⇒ Liebig/Tilman two-resource co-limitation without a collective transition (2026-06-19-1), adjudicated by the AIC co-limitation-form fit; **(C) flat/no crossing** (CI straddles 0 across the ladder AND at matched-R1) ⇒ connectivity refuted too ⇒ MI tail win is **non-density / content-driven**, hardening the honest-negative map. Stating A/B/C in advance is what makes the run *discriminating* rather than a post-hoc story.

- **Exact command (executor runs this):** **NONE — no GPU run.** This rides PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1's `--subsample-mode user` titration verbatim; the experiment agent runs *nothing extra*. The only artifact is an **analyst-side read-only CPU script** (≈40 lines, pandas/numpy over the `results_USERTITR_*` and `results_TITR_*` JSONs) emitting: the per-seed `DDD` interaction test (NDCG + HR), the two reduced-χ² collapse numbers `S(vs R2)` / `S(vs R1)`, the Crawford classical-vs-strong label, and the TOST verdict per null stratum. No `--out` model JSON, no model invocation. Owned by the analyst; the in-field contribution is this *spec*.

- **Gate:** Not a SOTA/NDCG lever ⇒ the >0.0660 / >0.0760 escalation gates **do not apply**. Read-out gate: a **dissociation is reportable only if `DDD` clears its paired test** (≥5 seeds, CI excludes 0, replicated on HR); the **binding-resource claim only if `S(vs R2)≈1 ≪ S(vs R1)`**; otherwise report the **honest null** ("no reliable dissociation; `DDD` CI includes 0") quantified by the Crawford CI + the TOST equivalence + the collapse `S`. Curve-shape A/B/C is assigned by the pre-registered criteria, never reverse-engineered.

- **Kill criterion:** If, with only ~30–110 tail hits and ≥5 seeds, the triple-difference `DDD` test is too low-powered to resolve (wide CI spanning 0), DO NOT escalate to more seeds to "find" significance (the fishing the pipeline forbids) — report the powered null via TOST + the collapse `S`, and downgrade the dissociation claim to "not reliably detected at this base rate." Never claim a dissociation from sig-vs-n.s. (the Nieuwenhuis error). Do not assign curve-shape A (knee) unless the Hill-slope CI excludes `n≈1`.

- **Expected delta & rationale:** **No NDCG delta — this is the convergence-phase statistical-reporting + figure-caption deliverable the supervisor explicitly requested.** Its value is **defensive and decisive**: it pre-empts the single most likely *statistical* inflation in the paper's central tail-law section (claiming a dissociation from "sig vs n.s." rather than the interaction), gives the (R1,R2) Tilman-plane figure a falsifiable collapse statistic instead of an eyeball, reports the tiny tail base with the correct small-sample dissociation criteria, and pre-registers the three curve shapes so the user-mode titration is *discriminating*. The honest most-likely VG/Beauty outcome ("no reliable dissociation — `DDD` CI includes 0, tail equivalent-to-zero by TOST, `S` ambiguous") is exactly the branch the supervisor pre-registered, and this protocol lets the paper state it cleanly and quantitatively rather than fish a favorable slice. Read-only CPU, zero GPU, zero new params, zero new run ⇒ fully convergence-compliant; pairs with 2026-06-19-1 (the run + Tilman plane) and 2026-06-19-2 (the rigidity mechanism + `φ_i`) as the statistical-honesty layer of the same single decisive result.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-20-1 — Down-limb seed-replication of the user-mode titration: lock-or-refute the INVERTED-U (hormetic) connectivity law (Brain–Cousens peak at MI's users/item)  [status: DONE — FLOOR ARTIFACT, non-monotonicity REFUTED (2026-06-20). All 16 runs complete (s09–12 × rho0{50,40} × {text,idonly}, FULL eval n_eval=94,762 + tail_n=10,900 constant/frozen, no drift). 5-seed within-rung tail Δ(text−ID): ρ=0.50 = +0.000236 (4/5 pos) — STILL POSITIVE, no down-limb decline (the paper's single-seed s08 −0.000663 was the lone negative of 5); ρ=0.40 = −0.001545 (1/5 pos) but via TEXT-arm tail-absolute COLLAPSE (0.00218 vs 0.00494 anchor; HR 0.00422 vs 0.0100) = degenerate model on ~40% of users. Pre-registered rule fires the FLOOR-ARTIFACT branch (sign-inconsistent across down-limb rungs + tail absolutes collapse) ⇒ DELETE the hormesis/non-monotonicity claim (confirms supervisor cycle-10 REJECT of the hormesis headline). Load-bearing result UNCHANGED: ρ=0.66 5-seed peak (+0.000178 5/5; dd-vs-full +0.000326, t=3.47, CI excl 0) + matched-R1 double dissociation ⇒ connectivity = confirmed PARTIAL tail cause. §5.4.2 edited (FIX #2 closed). THE FINAL SANCTIONED EXPERIMENT — pipeline now FREEZES to paper-only.]

> **Convergence note (read first):** this is **NOT a new model lever** and does **NOT re-open the closed lever search.** It is a **data-ablation** — *more seeds of the ALREADY-SANCTIONED, ALREADY-IMPLEMENTED user-mode titration* (PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1, the supervisor's "ONE FINAL mechanism experiment") at two rungs that are currently single-seed. It fires the in-field loop's OWN pre-registered re-opening trigger (the 2026-06-19 HOLD note: *"a genuinely NEW [status: NEW] cross-field idea that is a read-only/CPU/data-ablation artifact (NOT a model lever)"*) and the cross-field XF-20-2 trigger (*"the user-mode titration runs and returns … non-monotone in R2"*). **It is supervisor-gated:** the go/no-go on spending ~2.4h of GPU belongs to the supervisor's next cycle; this block specifies the run so it is ready-to-fire if sanctioned, and the read-only fit (deliverable B) should proceed regardless.

- **Idea (novel):** The user-mode titration's tail advantage (text−ID NDCG@10 on the tail tercile) is **non-monotone in connectivity (users/item)** — it **rises to a positive peak at MI's native connectivity, then sign-flips negative below it.** Independently recomputed from the on-disk `results_USERTITR_*_VG.json` (n_eval=94,762 and tail-bucket n=10,900 **identical at every rung** ⇒ eval held FIXED, thinning is train-only): ρ=1.00 (users/item≈3.70) tail Δ −0.000148 (established VG null) → **ρ=0.66 (users/item≈2.44 ≈ MI's 2.34) tail Δ +0.000178 ± 0.000137, 5/5 seeds positive (the keystone crossing)** → ρ=0.50 (≈1.85) −0.000663 (1 seed) → ρ=0.40 (≈1.48) −0.002173 (1 seed). Head Δ stays large-positive at every rung (only the TAIL is biphasic). That trace is a textbook **inverted-U / hormetic dose–response** in a *data resource*. The peak landing essentially ON MI's native connectivity is the claim: **MI wins the tail because it sits near the resonant optimum of the connectivity axis — not because "less connectivity is monotonically better for text"** (the standard, monotone recsys framing, which I confirmed by literature scan is the only published view; no recsys result describes a non-monotone content-benefit peak at intermediate connectivity). **This experiment powers the DOWN-LIMB (ρ=0.50, ρ=0.40) from 1 seed to 5 seeds** to discriminate a *real* hormetic collapse from a *floor artifact* (two near-degenerate models differing by chance), and fits the curve with toxicology's biphasic models to extract a falsifiable peak location `x*` and up-limb effect size `f`.

- **Source(s):** Hormesis / biphasic dose–response — Calabrese & Baldwin, *Hormesis: the dose–response revolution*, Annu. Rev. Pharmacol. Toxicol. 2003 (PMID 12195028); Brain & Cousens 1989 (5-param log-logistic hormesis model; CRAN `drc::BC`); Cedergreen, Ritz & Streibig 2005, *Improved empirical models describing hormesis*; Modeling biphasic non-sigmoidal dose–response: Brain–Cousens vs Cedergreen, arXiv:2308.08618 (2023, PMC10462167) — both models fit inverted-U curves and compute "hormesis quantities" (peak/zero-crossing/up-limb size) via `scipy.optimize.curve_fit`. *Why* an interior optimum exists — **stochastic resonance** (Gammaitoni et al., *Stochastic Resonance*, Rev. Mod. Phys. 1998): in a threshold-ish nonlinear system, weak-signal detection is maximized at an INTERMEDIATE level of a second variable. Grounded from **CROSSFIELD_IDEAS.md XF 2026-06-20-3** (toxicology hormesis + stochastic resonance). Honest prior-art caveat: hormesis, Brain–Cousens, and stochastic resonance are textbook — **zero novelty in the ingredients**; the narrow defensible novelty is *identifying a biphasic/hormetic optimum in catalog connectivity as the mechanism of a recommender long-tail effect, and fitting it with toxicology's biphasic models.*

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** **PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1 (user-mode titration, DONE)** — it locked the ρ=0.66 PEAK at 5 seeds (+0.000178, 5/5) and the (mode×stratum) double-dissociation, but its down-limb rungs ρ=0.50/0.40 are **single-seed (s08 only)** and were read only as "non-monotone collapse below MI-connectivity ⇒ residual content factor." This proposal differs by (a) **powering exactly those two rungs to 5 seeds** (the missing replication that converts "suggestive collapse" into a locked-or-refuted limb), and (b) **fitting the full curve as a biphasic hormetic dose–response** (Brain–Cousens/Cedergreen) rather than reading a monotone trend — i.e. it tests a *fourth* curve shape (interior optimum + sign flip) that the pre-registered monotone shape-menu of PROPOSAL 2026-06-18-2 (A sharp knee / B smooth saturation / C flat null) structurally cannot represent. It is NOT the interaction-mode titration (CLOSED, REFUTED — that thinned R1/count, was flat-null on the tail; this thins R2/connectivity and is the arm that crossed positive). It is NOT a model lever (no flag, no parameter, no arch/loss/scoring change).

- **Mechanism / how to implement:** **No code change** — the flags are already implemented and smoke-tested (PROPOSAL 2026-06-19-1: `--subsample-train-frac`, `--subsample-mode user`, `--subsample-seed`; default `1.0` = bit-identical no-op; thins TRAIN interactions by dropping whole users so each surviving user keeps its full history (no sequence-length confound); eval users + conditioning histories + targets + full catalog FIXED; tail/mid/head terciles FROZEN on the FULL-density train). The protocol that produced the 5-seed ρ=0.66 peak varied the **model `--seed`** over {20260608…20260612} with **`--subsample-seed 0` held fixed** (same dropped-user subset; only model-init/training stochasticity varies). This proposal replicates that EXACT protocol at the two down-limb rungs: extend ρ=0.50 and ρ=0.40 to model seeds {20260609, 20260610, 20260611, 20260612}, both arms (TEXT = V2 stack; ID-only = `--no-sbert`, drop `--text-prototypes`/`--text-sim-bias`). s08 already on disk ⇒ **16 new runs** (2 rungs × 4 seeds × 2 arms), ~9 min/run FULL-eval ≈ **~2.4 h total.** **Deliverable B (read-only, no GPU, ANALYST-lane, do regardless of the GPU go/no-go):** fit Brain–Cousens (and Cedergreen) to the `(users/item, paired tail Δ)` points {3.70:−0.000148, 2.44:+0.000178, 1.85:…, 1.48:…} with `scipy.optimize.curve_fit` (~15 lines); report the fitted **peak location `x*`** (test: `x*` ≈ MI's 2.34?) and the **up-limb effect size `f` ± seed-bootstrap CI** (`f`≈0 ⇒ no hormesis ⇒ revert to the monotone read).

- **Exact command (executor runs this — loop over SEED ∈ {20260609,20260610,20260611,20260612} and FRAC ∈ {0.50,0.40}):**
  - TEXT arm: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Video_Games --epochs 40 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed <SEED> --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --subsample-mode user --subsample-train-frac <FRAC> --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_USERTITR_text_rho0<FF>_s<SS>_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_USERTITR_text_rho0<FF>_s<SS>_VG.log 2>&1`
  - ID-only arm: same with `--no-sbert` and WITHOUT `--text-sim-bias`/`--text-prototypes 512`: `… --encoder hstu --time-bias --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --no-sbert --subsample-mode user --subsample-train-frac <FRAC> --subsample-seed 0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_USERTITR_idonly_rho0<FF>_s<SS>_VG.json > _bestrec_run/run_USERTITR_idonly_rho0<FF>_s<SS>_VG.log 2>&1`
  (FF = 50 or 40; SS = 09…12. Verify each run reports n_eval=94,762 and tail n=10,900 — identical to the on-disk rungs — before trusting the delta.)

- **Gate:** smoke not needed (flags already smoke-clean per 2026-06-19-1); per-run sanity = n_eval=94,762 + tail n=10,900 + no NaN. This is a **mechanism experiment, NOT a SOTA attempt** — the 0.0660/0.0760 test gates DO NOT APPLY (the headline test metric is untouched by a data-ablation; expected Δ on overall NDCG = 0). Decision is on the **paired tail Δ sign/variance**, pre-registered below.
- **Pre-registered decision (binding):**
  - **LOCK the inverted-U / hormetic law** if at ≥4/5 seeds BOTH down-limb rungs are tail-Δ-negative, **sign-consistent**, with magnitude clearly exceeding the peak (|Δ| ≫ 0.000178) AND the paired Δ-of-Δ (down-limb vs ρ=0.66 peak) is significantly negative AND the Brain–Cousens fit gives `x*` ≈ MI's 2.34 with a significant up-limb `f`. ⇒ paper upgrades from "connectivity binds the tail (monotone crossing)" to "**the tail advantage is a biphasic/hormetic optimum in connectivity, peaking at MI's native users/item**" — a strictly stronger, more falsifiable claim that *explains* MI's specificity (dissolves the open "what MI-specific property drives the tail win?" question into "MI sits at the resonant peak").
  - **REFUTE → floor artifact** if the down-limb tail Δ is **sign-inconsistent / high-variance across the 5 seeds**, OR the per-arm tail ABSOLUTE NDCG/HR at ρ=0.40 collapses toward the floor for BOTH arms (the deflationary null the cross-field author named: at ρ=0.40 overall NDCG already fell to ~0.0535 ID / ~0.0589 text — both models are degrading, so a negative Δ may be "two near-broken models differ by chance," not a genuine resonance). ⇒ revert to the monotone connectivity-crossing framing; the 5-seed ρ=0.66 peak stands as the result; report the down-limb as a flagged, honestly-caveated observation. No harm, no inflation.
- **Kill criterion:** if ≥4 more seeds leave the down-limb tail-Δ band straddling 0 (sign-inconsistent) OR both arms' ρ=0.40 tail absolute NDCG drop below ~half the ρ=0.66 absolute (degeneracy), declare **floor artifact** — do NOT claim hormesis; ship the 5-seed peak + monotone framing. **Mandatory honest readout regardless of outcome:** report per-arm tail ABSOLUTE NDCG + HR (not only the delta) at each rung, so "both models degenerating" (floor) is always distinguishable from "text specifically mis-ranks the shredded tail" (real down-limb). Note for honesty: this protocol varies model-seed with `subsample_seed=0` fixed (matches the peak's 5/5 exactly, apples-to-apples) ⇒ it measures model-seed variance at a fixed dropped-user subset, NOT user-subset variance; a fully-powered limb would also vary `--subsample-seed`, flagged as optional hardening only.
- **Expected delta & rationale:** **ZERO on the headline test NDCG@10** (data-ablation; the model is unchanged). The value is **mechanistic and cheap-high-information**: ~2.4 h of the cheapest VG runs converts the paper's most novel contribution (the dataset-conditional tail law) from a *monotone connectivity-crossing* into either a *locked biphasic/hormetic optimum* (strictly stronger, falsifiable, with a fitted peak `x*`≈2.34 and up-limb `f`) or a *cleanly-refuted-down-limb + 5-seed-peak* result. Both branches are publishable, both END the mechanism search, and either way it pre-empts the reviewer who reads the single-seed down-limb as either over-claimed hormesis or unflagged noise. This is exactly the "cheap high-information probe" the in-field charter exempts from the <0.0760-ceiling kill — it is the last piece of evidence the tail law needs before paper-freeze.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1 — Cold-item leave-items-out split: the connectivity gate as a cold-start METHOD (USER-DIRECTED, re-opens the program)  [status: DONE 2026-06-23 — implemented `--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`/`by_coldstart` (leak-free, smoke-validated); 3-arm MI centerpiece (seed 20260608, frac 0.15, 8748 cold targets): cold NDCG@10 = 0.00000 (0 hits) for ALL 3 arms (ID-only / text-stack / conn-gate) — metric on the FLOOR at @10, zero resolving power. conn-gate alpha->0.00102 (voted OFF; no cold-item gradient by construction). Pre-registered novel test conn-gate>text-stack on cold = 0=0, CI incl 0 => PROVISIONAL Table-2 cold-start negative. COARSER-CUTOFF CONFIRM (2026-06-23 +1h) COMPLETE: re-ran 3 arms with NDCG/HR@{20,50,100}+MRR added to by_coldstart (eval-only superset; @10 keys + by_popularity bit-identical) => cold STILL floors at 0 hits through @100 for ALL arms; only cold MRR resolves (ID 0.0000725 / text 0.0000903 / conn-gate 0.0000903 ~ catalog-midpoint random rank). conn-gate cold MRR == text-stack to 6 dp (gate alpha=0.001023, OFF) => NOVEL test = 0 at every cutoff => Table-2 cold-start negative now ROBUST (not a @10-cutoff artifact). text > ID on cold MRR +0.0000179 (sanity direction, at noise floor). Did NOT 5-seed a floored metric. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-06-23 +1h. NEXT: PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1 cold-synth (only mechanism that can lift cold items off the floor).]
- **Idea (novel):** Build a genuine ITEM cold-start protocol in the sequential harness and test the connectivity gate where it should matter most — items with ZERO training interactions. The user-mode titration proved text's tail advantage is *caused by* low collaborative connectivity (users/item); cold-start is the users/item→0 limit, so `--conn-gate` (which maximally trusts text when item_users→0, by construction) is the mechanism-derived cold-start method. The 5-core "tail" (≥5 users) is a weak proxy; this measures the real thing.
- **Source(s):** internal — the user-mode titration double-dissociation (ANALYSIS/SOTA_VERDICT 2026-06-19/20; users/item is the causal tail axis). Cold-start hybrid lineage: DropoutNet (Volkovs 2017), CLCRec (Wei 2021) — already implemented here (`run_faithful_dropoutnet.py`, `run_faithful_clcrec.py`).
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** all prior gating (X1 js-shrink, Z1 fitness-gate, CF1 cue-fusion — all DEAD) gated on interaction-FREQUENCY and were tested on the warm 5-core tail. This (a) gates on CONNECTIVITY (users/item, the causal axis) and (b) tests on a TRUE cold-item split (0-interaction items), the regime those probes never touched and where text-vs-ID is a large absolute gap.
- **Mechanism / how to implement:** add `--cold-item-frac F` (e.g. 0.1–0.2) + `--cold-item-seed S` to run_sasrec_sbert.py. After `reindex()`: deterministically (seed S, numpy RandomState) sample F·n_items item ids as the COLD set. Remove every cold item from all TRAINING sequences (`user_seqs` filtering) so their `item_emb` never trains; the frozen `sbert_table` (content) remains for ALL items. Keep cold items as valid/test TARGETS. In `evaluate()`, alongside `by_popularity`, add `by_coldstart` = {cold, warm} keyed by whether the test target ∈ cold set; headline metric = cold-item NDCG@10/HR@10. LEAK-FREE: cold set from item ids only; `item_users`=0 for cold items (the gate's designed extreme — w_id→1−α, w_tx→1+α). One cold split shared across the 3 arms for a paired comparison.
- **Exact command (executor runs this — 3 arms, same `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, seed 20260608 first, then 5-seed if the gap is real):** `… Musical_Instruments … --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 [ARM] --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_COLD_<arm>_MI_seed20260608.json` where [ARM] ∈ { `--no-sbert` (ID-only), `` (text-stack), `--conn-gate` (text+gate) }. (Standard prefix: `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py … --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine`.)
- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; confirm cold items absent from train, present as targets, by_coldstart populated) → full 20ep. Primary read = cold-item NDCG: text-stack ≫ ID-only is expected (sanity); the NOVEL test is **conn-gate > text-stack on cold items**. If conn-gate beats text-stack on cold items by a paired, ≥5-seed, CI-excludes-0 margin ⇒ escalate to VG + Beauty + the CLCRec/DropoutNet head-to-head (the SOTA-stretch).
- **Kill criterion:** conn-gate ≤ text-stack on cold items at 5 seeds (CI includes 0) ⇒ the gate adds nothing beyond the additive text path on cold items ⇒ DEAD as a method (but the cold-item characterization of text-vs-ID still ships as a finding).
- **Expected delta & rationale:** honest — text-stack will beat ID-only on cold items by a LARGE margin (ID is untrained there); that part is near-certain but not novel. The novel, uncertain part is whether *explicitly* down-weighting the untrained ID and up-weighting text (the gate) beats the additive baseline on cold items. Plausible (the additive path still adds the noisy ID vector; the gate removes it for cold items) but must be shown. ~50/50 it confirms; if it does, it's a clean, mechanism-derived cold-start method with a legible cold-item win — the single highest-value run in the escalation.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1 — Synthetic connectivity: parameter-free text-kNN aggregation of LEARNED warm-item ID embeddings for zero-interaction cold items  [status: DONE 2026-06-23 — GATE PASSED (cold-start mechanism contribution; NOT an absolute SOTA). Implemented `--cold-synth-knn`/`--cold-synth-temp` (default-OFF zero-init no-op; precompute top-K WARM frozen-text neighbors per cold item, softmax(cos/temp) weights; item_features() torch.where-replaces cold ID rows with the learned-warm-neighbor weighted sum, warm rows verbatim; py_compile OK, 2ep smoke no-NaN). **5-seed paired (cold-synth − text-stack, IDENTICAL `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, MI, seeds 20260608–12, 8,748 cold targets): cold NDCG@10 Δ = +0.000380 ± 0.000023, 95% CI [+0.000352,+0.000408], t=+37.5, 5/5 pos, CI EXCLUDES 0; cold MRR Δ = +0.000989 ± 0.000028, CI [+0.000954,+0.001024], t=+78.9, 5/5 pos, CI EXCLUDES 0.** text-stack/conn-gate/ID-only ALL = 0 hits@10 every seed (structural floor); cold-synth = 7–8 hits@10 every seed → FIRST arm to make true-cold (item_users=0) items rankable AT ALL. Warm/overall NOT degraded (warm NDCG@10 0.0438 ≈ text-stack 0.0430; overall 0.037 ≈ 0.0365) ⇒ cold-only no-op verified. **Passes the pre-registered anti-fishing gate ⇒ the connectivity mechanism IS actionable on true cold items via explicit kNN ID transfer (where the learnable conn-gate, no-cold-gradient, was DEAD) — a mechanism+method §5.5 contribution.** HONESTY: absolute cold NDCG@10 0.00038 (~8/8748 hits) is a PROOF-OF-MECHANISM, NOT a deployable cold-start SOTA — far below the LC2C/z-fusion incumbent (~0.057 MI) and overall 0.037 ≪ 0.0760 (hardware-blocked); no SOTA_ACHIEVED. Per the gate's escalation clause, the cold-start-SOTA stretch (VG + Beauty replication + protocol-matched LC2C head-to-head) is the SUPERVISOR-directed next step. results: `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}.json` + paired `results_COLD_text_MI_seed{08–12}.json`. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-06-23 (+2h/+3h).]
- **Idea (novel):** A cold item has 0 collaborative connectivity (users/item = 0), so its ID embedding is untrained random noise; the campaign's user-mode titration proved *collaborative connectivity* is the binding tail/cold resource (dd +0.000326, t=3.47, CI excl 0). Instead of GATING ID-vs-text (conn-gate — DEAD) or ROUTING cold→text (Z1/2026-06-17-1 — DEAD), **manufacture synthetic connectivity**: for each cold item, replace its untrained ID row with a text-similarity-weighted average of the *already-learned* ID embeddings of its top-K nearest WARM items in frozen-SBERT space. The cold item thereby inherits a real collaborative embedding borrowed from connected, text-similar warm neighbors — the mechanism-derived way to give a 0-connectivity item the resource the paper proved it lacks. Parameter-free (no learnable scalar that can be voted off, unlike conn-gate α→0.0011 / W1 β→−7.42 / X1 c≈0 / Y1 T→0), un-nullable, and active ONLY on item_users==0 cold items (warm/overall = exact no-op).
- **Source(s):** "Let It Go? Not Quite: Content-Based Initialization for Item Cold Start in Sequential Recommendation" (arXiv:2507.19473, RecSys 2025); CCFCRec "Contrastive Collaborative Filtering for Cold-Start Item Recommendation" (Zhou & Zhang, WWW 2023, arXiv:2302.02151); DropoutNet (Volkovs 2017, NeurIPS); MWUF meta scaling/shifting (arXiv:2105.04790). Internal: user-mode titration double-dissociation (SOTA_VERDICT/ANALYSIS 2026-06-19/20 — connectivity = causal tail/cold axis).
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (a) **conn-gate (DEAD)** — a *learnable scalar* reweighting ID vs text by connectivity; voted itself OFF (α→0.0011) and was null on warm-tail MI. This SYNTHESIZES a collaborative embedding from neighbors (a kNN-attention operation in text→CF space), is parameter-free (cannot be voted off), and targets the true 0-interaction cold split, not the warm 5-core tail conn-gate was tested on. (b) **Z1 / exposure-gated routing (2026-06-17-1, DEAD)** — routed warm-tail cold items ONTO TEXT, discarding the useful sparse ID, and crashed the tail −75%. That failure mode CANNOT occur here: (i) on a 0-interaction item there is no useful ID to discard (it never trained), and (ii) it routes onto a *collaborative* neighbor-borrowed embedding (not text), and (iii) it touches only item_users==0 items, never the warm tail. (c) **CF1 cue-fusion (DEAD)** — reliability-weighted ID×text fusion on warm data; this is neighbor-CF synthesis, cold-only. (d) **Published "Let It Go" (2507.19473)** adds a trainable delta to frozen *content* embeddings; mine borrows learned *collaborative* embeddings from text-neighbors — an orthogonal mechanism (could even stack with it). (e) **CCFCRec / DropoutNet** train a content→CF *generator network* (which "blurs" collaborative embeddings, the very problem CCFCRec fights, and needs extra training); mine is a *parameter-free inference-time kNN aggregation* of warm IDs — no generator, no blur, no extra trained module. No kNN-CF-transfer probe exists anywhere in the ledger.
- **Mechanism / how to implement:** Depends on PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1's `--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed` cold-item split (run AFTER it lands as the 4th arm). Add flag `--cold-synth-knn K` (default 0 = OFF, exact no-op) and `--cold-synth-temp τ` (default 0.07). (1) PRECOMPUTE (one-time, leak-free, reuses the existing `--cue-fusion-nbr` chunked top-K cosine machinery at `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1782` and the TRAIN-only `item_users` buffer at `:1725`): for each COLD item (item_users==0), compute cosine over frozen L2-normed `sbert_table` against the WARM candidate set (item_users>0) ONLY, take top-K, weights w_ik = softmax(cos_ik/τ); store `cold_nbr_idx` (n_cold×K long) + `cold_nbr_w` (n_cold×K float) as non-persistent buffers. (2) FORWARD, in `item_features()` (the same hook as conn-gate, `:709`): for the subset of `item_ids` that are cold, set ê = Σ_k cold_nbr_w · item_emb(cold_nbr_idx) (an index_select + weighted sum / bmm over the *current* learned warm ID rows), and use ê in place of the cold item's own `item_emb` row; the frozen text path (`sbert_proj(sbert_table)`) is added on top UNCHANGED. Warm items: untouched. Math: e_cold = (Σ_k w_ik · item_emb[n_ik]) + sbert_proj(sbert_table[cold]); gradients flow into the warm neighbor rows (mild beneficial extra signal), the cold row is bypassed. (3) ZERO-INIT NO-OP: `--cold-synth-knn 0` → branch skipped → plain additive behavior; and with no `--cold-item-frac`, no item has item_users==0 (5-core ⇒ all warm) ⇒ inert even if the flag is on. Double-safe, non-learnable (un-nullable). py_compile + 2ep smoke (no NaN; confirm cold rows use neighbor-synth, warm rows identical to baseline) before full.
- **Exact command (executor runs this — 4th arm of the PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1 cold-item protocol, SAME `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, seed 20260608 first then 5-seed if the gap is real):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` (paired against the PROPOSAL 2026-06-22-1 `text-stack` arm `results_COLD_text_MI_seed20260608.json` and `conn-gate` arm on the IDENTICAL cold split/seed; report cold-item NDCG@10/HR@10 + warm/overall sanity + the cold hit-count).
- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; cold rows neighbor-synth, warm rows bitwise-identical to text-stack baseline, by_coldstart populated) → full 20ep MI. Primary read = cold-item NDCG@10. NOVEL test = **cold-synth > text-stack AND > conn-gate on cold items**, paired same-split/seed. If cold-synth beats text-stack on cold items by a paired ≥5-seed (20260608–12) CI-excludes-0 positive margin ⇒ escalate to VG + Beauty + the CLCRec/DropoutNet head-to-head (the cold-start-SOTA stretch). Report cold hit-count every run (tiny-base honesty).
- **Kill criterion:** cold-synth ≤ text-stack on cold items at 5 seeds (CI includes 0) ⇒ neighbor-borrowed collaborative signal adds nothing beyond the additive frozen-text path on cold items ⇒ DEAD as a method → Table 2 (§5.5) negative row ("synthetic-connectivity kNN-CF transfer · content-CF blending · MI cold-item split · cold Δ +<x> (5, CI incl 0) · parameter-free, no scalar to vote off · DEAD — the connectivity mechanism is descriptive, not actionable even via explicit neighbor transfer"), strengthening the conn-gate KILL into a two-method "connectivity is not actionable" result. The cold-item characterization of text-vs-ID still ships regardless.
- **Expected delta & rationale:** honest. text-stack ≫ ID-only on cold items is near-certain (ID untrained) — sanity, not novel. The novel uncertain question: does injecting a *collaborative* embedding (the paper's confirmed binding resource) borrowed from text-similar warm neighbors beat the additive frozen-text path on 0-connectivity items? Plausible-positive because the additive path gives cold items only content, never collaborative signal, and the titration proved connectivity is what the tail/cold regime is starved of — so manufacturing it from connected neighbors directly supplies the missing resource. But realistic ceiling: connectivity is a *partial* cause (residual content), the cold base is tiny (~tens of hits/seed — report hit-count), and conn-gate's null warns the additive text path is hard to beat ⇒ ~50/55% it confirms. EITHER outcome is high-information and publishable: a positive = a legible, parameter-free, mechanism-derived cold-start method (the user's ~85% "publishable mechanism+method" branch, with the cold-item-split being where the ~15-25% strict-SOTA line could live); a null = a clean second "connectivity is descriptive, not actionable" negative complementing conn-gate. NOT aimed at the hardware-blocked VG-overall 0.0760 — this is cold-item-split-scoped, exactly the regime the user re-open identified as where a defensible cold-start win can live. Pure-PyTorch, ~same runtime as conn-gate (well <1h/20ep MI), zero overall-metric risk (cold-only no-op).
