## PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-2 — Kriging-cold: redundancy-decorrelated (BLUP) cold-item ID imputation — the C⁻¹ generalization of cold-synth  [status: DONE — REFUTED: MI seed08 cold NDCG@10 0.000036 (1 hit) vs cold-synth 0.000342 (7 hits); cold NDCG@100 0.000942 (46) vs 0.002745 (125); COLLAPSE, not neutral. Declustering fired hard (median cond 380, mean neg-weight mass 0.48) but HURT — near-duplicate franchise/sequel neighbors are SIGNAL for cold-item CF transfer, not redundant nuisance; C⁻¹ downweights exactly the informative cluster + injects 48% signed neg-mass that mis-ranks. warm/overall also down (−0.0078/−0.0067, documented training-denominator competitor-set coupling, is_cold-gated, not a leak). Pre-registered COLLAPSE-stop fired → single-seed, no 5-seed/nugget-sweep. With norm-restore (magnitude, DONE-REFUTED) this CLOSES both imputation-improvement axes: cold-synth flat softmax-kNN is a robust local optimum. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-02.]
- **Idea (novel):** cold-synth (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1, **DONE — GATE PASSED**, the only arm that lifts true-cold items off the exact-zero floor: cold NDCG@10 +0.000380, CI excl 0) imputes each cold item's untrained ID row as a **flat softmax-text-kNN average** of its warm neighbors' LEARNED ID rows. That is exactly **ordinary kriging with `C = I`** — it assumes the K text-neighbors are mutually independent. They are not: the cold-item neighborhoods here are tight and redundant (measured `mean top-1 cosine = 0.819` on MI — cold items typically sit next to a near-duplicate sequel/edition/franchise cluster of warm items). Flat kNN **double-counts the densest cluster** and pulls the imputed embedding toward whatever franchise is most replicated near the cold item, not toward its true position (the red-bus/blue-bus / IIA failure, on the imputation side). Replace the softmax weights with the **Best Linear Unbiased Predictor** `w = (K_ww + λI)⁻¹ k_iw`, where `K_ww` is the warm-neighbor↔warm-neighbor text-covariance (Gram) matrix and `k_iw` the cold↔warm text-covariance vector. The `K_ww⁻¹` term splits one shared vote across a redundant cluster and up-weights isolated, informative neighbors — exactly what flat-kNN structurally cannot do. Parameter-free; the **entire delta vs cold-synth is the `C⁻¹` declustering term**, a clean A/B.
- **Source(s):** Three non-CS fields independently converged on BLUP-beats-nearest-relative-averaging: **(geostatistics)** ordinary kriging, `λ = C⁻¹c`; geostatisticslessons.com "Kriging Weights in the Presence of Redundant Data" + Journel screening-effect literature ("where data points are clustered the weight of the cluster is divided among its members"); Cressie, *Statistics for Spatial Data* (1993). **(quantitative genetics)** GBLUP = ridge/kernel-ridge BLUP with the genomic relationship matrix `G`; *Heredity* 2021 (rrBLUP); VanRaden 2008 J.Dairy Sci. **(phylogenetics)** Brownian-motion BLUP trait imputation = ancestral-state reconstruction; Goolsby et al. 2017 (Rphylopars), Revell phytools `phylo.impute`. Grounded from CROSSFIELD_IDEAS XF 2026-06-23-2.
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** Nearest = **cold-synth (2026-06-23-1)** — literally the `C = I` (independent-neighbor) special case of this estimator; this restores the data-to-data covariance inverse cold-synth drops, predicted to DIVERGE precisely for cold items whose neighborhoods are internally redundant (the 0.819-top-1-cosine regime is met). Distinct from **conn-gate** (DEAD warm-tail: a *learnable score-fusion gate*, voted itself off α≈0.001; this is *parameter-free imputation of the representation*, no scalar to vote off). Distinct from **GD1 / BBP spectral shrinkage** (denoises the WARM matrix that already trained; this imputes COLD rows that never trained — opposite operands). Distinct from TAPE / text-prototypes (always-on additive capacity for all items; this is a one-shot cold-only BLUP). No ledger probe inverts a neighbor↔neighbor covariance to decluster the cold imputation.
- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag `--krige-cold` (default OFF ⇒ cold path bit-identical to cold-synth) plus `--krige-nugget` (default 0.01). It reuses the **entire** cold-synth machinery (`--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`/`--cold-synth-knn`/`--cold-synth-temp`); the ONLY change is the weight vector written into the existing `cold_synth_wgt` buffer at the precompute site `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1897–1903` (inside the chunked `for s0 in range(...cold_idx_t...)` loop, after `tv, ti = sims.topk(K, dim=1)`). The forward impute hook (`:738–745`, `synth = (item_emb(nbr) * w).sum`) is **untouched** — same buffers, same shapes. In that loop, when `args.krige_cold`:
  ```
  # ti: (b, K) warm-neighbor ids; tv: (b, K) cold↔neighbor cosines (== k_iw)
  Tn  = xs[ti]                                  # (b, K, d) normalized warm-neighbor text vecs (xs already L2-normed)
  Kww = torch.bmm(Tn, Tn.transpose(1, 2))       # (b, K, K) LINEAR text kernel = cosine Gram (parameter-free)
  lam = args.krige_nugget * Kww.diagonal(dim1=1, dim2=2).mean(dim=1)      # (b,) nugget proportional to mean diag
  Kww = Kww + lam.view(-1,1,1) * torch.eye(K, device=DEVICE)              # ridge nugget for invertibility
  w   = torch.linalg.solve(Kww, tv.unsqueeze(-1)).squeeze(-1)             # (b, K) = (K_ww+lambda*I)^-1 k_iw
  w   = w / w.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8)                    # renorm to a (signed) weighted average
  cold_synth_wgt[block] = w                                               # else: the existing softmax(tv/temp)
  ```
  **Zero-init / no-op:** `--krige-cold` OFF ⇒ the line is never reached, cold path is the DONE cold-synth verbatim; with no `--cold-item-frac` no item is cold ⇒ inert. **Parameter-free** (linear cosine kernel = the GBLUP linear-kernel realization; nugget is a fixed numerical regularizer, not learned — nothing to vote off, unlike conn-gate/W1/X1/Y1). **Leak-free:** identical guards to cold-synth — warm ID rows = train-only; `K_ww`, `k_iw` from frozen `sbert_emb`; cold row never enters training; cold items remain valid/test TARGETS only. Cost: one batched `K×K`=20×20 `linalg.solve` over the cold block, precomputed ONCE post-train ⇒ seconds. **Interpretable readout to log:** median condition number of `(K_ww+λI)` and the mean negative-weight mass (how much declustering actually fired) — if `K_ww≈I` (diverse neighborhoods) these are ~1 / ~0 and kriging collapses to cold-synth (clean neutral). Use `--cold-synth-knn 20` to pair EXACTLY against the on-disk `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}` (K=20); a K=64 follow-up is optional, not required.
- **Exact command (executor runs this):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07 --krige-cold --krige-nugget 0.01 --seed 20260608 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_KRIGE_cold_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_KRIGE_cold_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` — then, ONLY IF the single-seed cold gap vs cold-synth looks real, 5-seed `20260608–12` (paired against the existing `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}.json`, identical split).
- **Gate (cold-start axis — same pre-registered anti-fishing gate as conn-gate/cold-synth, NOT the 0.0760 overall gate):** smoke 2ep (no NaN; cold rows kriging-synth, **warm rows bit-identical to cold-synth/text-stack**, `by_coldstart` populated, `linalg.solve` not singular, condition number logged) → single-seed MI → if cold NDCG@{10,100}/MRR vs cold-synth is positive, **5-seed paired (kriging − cold-synth, IDENTICAL `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, MI, seeds 20260608–12)**; **95% CI EXCLUDES 0 and positive on cold NDCG ⇒ a §5.5 mechanism-refinement contribution** ("redundancy-decorrelated BLUP imputation beats flat content-kNN on true cold items"); **CI includes 0 ⇒ §5.5 Table 2 neutral row** ("declustering buys nothing; Amazon cold-item text-neighborhoods are already effectively diverse" — still publishable). Report cold NDCG@10 **absolute + rel% vs cold-synth + cold hit-count (@10 AND @100)** + warm-no-op sanity + median condition number. **@10 is near-floor (~8 hits/8748) — primary power is on NDCG@100 / n_hit@100 (cold-synth has ~125 hits there) and cold MRR; do NOT make single-seed rel% claims (the conn-gate s08 +33% mirage is the standing precedent).**
- **Kill criterion:** kriging ≤ cold-synth on cold NDCG/MRR at 5 seeds (CI includes 0) ⇒ neutral §5.5 row, NOT a regression (kriging cannot do worse than cold-synth unless the nugget is mis-scaled — if cold metrics drop below cold-synth, the nugget is too small / `K_ww` ill-conditioned: log condition number and STOP, do not sweep). A measured cold-absolute COLLAPSE (cold NDCG@100 ≪ cold-synth's ~0.0027) ⇒ numerical-instability artifact, kill the run, do not 5-seed.
- **Expected delta & rationale:** HONEST and bounded. cold-synth's absolute cold NDCG@10 is ~0.00038 (~8/8748 hits) — a PROOF-OF-MECHANISM, far below the LC2C/z-fusion incumbent (~0.057 MI) and the overall 0.0760 (hardware-blocked); kriging does NOT change that order of magnitude and is NOT a path to absolute SOTA. Its value is a **cheap, high-information mechanistic probe**: the precondition is measured-present (top-1 cosine 0.819 ⇒ redundant near-duplicate neighbor clusters), so declustering has real headroom, and the A/B is nearly deterministic (both imputations are fixed given frozen text + converged warm rows ⇒ tight paired CIs, like cold-synth's t=37), giving it genuine power to resolve a small Δ. Realistic cold NDCG/MRR delta vs cold-synth: **+0.00005 to +0.0002** (concentrated in @100 / n_hit@100 where there is mass), with a real chance of clean-neutral (collapse to cold-synth). Either outcome is a publishable §5.5 result that directly tests whether the IIA/redundancy failure mode (XF-16-1) that killed flat-kNN in warm settings also limits cold imputation — closing the cold-start mechanism story the centerpiece opened. Cost: ~10 min/seed (incremental over cold-synth is a few seconds of precompute), single GPU job, parameter-free.

## PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-3 — Norm-restored cold-synth: parameter-free MAGNITUDE calibration of the kNN-imputed cold ID row (the orthogonal axis kriging/cold-synth both ignore)  [status: DONE — REFUTED: MI seed08 cold NDCG@10 0.000 (COLLAPSE from cold-synth 0.000342; 7→0 hits@10, 125→22 hits@100), warm/overall also down; magnitude-contraction is PROTECTIVE not a limiter, full-τ_i restoration over-competes. Pre-registered COLLAPSE-stop fired → single-seed, no 5-seed/sweep. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-02.]
- **Idea (novel):** cold-synth (PROPOSAL 2026-06-23-1, **DONE — GATE PASSED**, the only arm that lifts true-cold items off the exact-zero floor: MI cold NDCG@10 +0.000380 / VG +0.000844, 5/5, CI excl 0) imputes a cold item's untrained ID row as a softmax-text-kNN **weighted average** of warm neighbors' LEARNED ID rows. A weighted average of vectors that are not collinear is **norm-contracting** (Jensen / triangle inequality): `‖Σ wₖ eₖ‖ ≤ Σ wₖ‖eₖ‖`, with strict inequality whenever the neighbors disagree in direction. So every synthesized cold embedding is born **systematically smaller in magnitude** than the warm rows it must out-score. Because the model scores by **dot product** under full-catalog softmax (`logit = h·e`), a deflated `‖e_cold‖` is a first-order, direction-independent penalty: the cold item is suppressed *regardless of how well-aimed cold-synth/kriging got its direction*. This is the documented magnitude→ranking pathology (popular/large-norm items win the dot-product even at low directional similarity) — appearing here on the IMPUTATION side, induced by the averaging operator itself. It is exactly why cold-synth, though directionally sensible, still ranks only ~7–8/8748 cold items into top-10. **Fix (parameter-free, cold-only): after building `synth`, rescale it to the LOCAL neighbor magnitude `τ_i = Σ wₖ‖eₖ‖` (the weighted-mean norm of its own K text-neighbors), preserving the cold-synth/kriging DIRECTION while restoring the magnitude the averaging destroyed.** This is the **orthogonal axis** to both queued cold methods: cold-synth and kriging-cold (2026-06-23-2) only ever set the synthesized *direction* (their weights renormalize to sum 1 ⇒ both stay norm-contracted); neither touches magnitude. Norm-restoration could therefore **stack** on either and plausibly delivers a LARGER lift (magnitude is a first-order logit scale; declustering is a second-order direction tweak).
- **Source(s):** **(magnitude→ranking, the core mechanism)** "On Inherited Popularity Bias in Cold-Start Item Recommendation" (Meehan, arXiv:2510.11402, Oct 2025): *"the vector magnitudes of embeddings for popular items will grow more rapidly than those of items in the long-tail … these higher magnitudes force items higher in user rankings even when similarity in vector direction is fairly low"*; they show **content-based/generative cold synthesis inherits the magnitude pathology** and apply a **post-hoc, parameter-controlled magnitude scaling** (their Eq. 2, γc toward warm mean μ_w) — but in the *debiasing* direction (COMPRESS over-inflated cold norms). **This proposal is the same magnitude-controls-rank mechanism applied in the OPPOSITE direction**: our kNN-averaged cold rows are *under*-normed (an averaging artifact, not over-prediction), and the metric is cold-item NDCG on genuine relevant cold targets, so we RESTORE magnitude toward the local neighbor scale rather than compress it. **(parameter-free norm control)** "Test Time Embedding Normalization for Popularity Bias Mitigation" (arXiv:2308.11288) — post-hoc, retrain-free magnitude normalization. **(scaling cold embeddings matters)** MWUF meta scaling & shifting networks (Zhu et al., arXiv:2105.04790, SIGIR 2021); "Let It Go? Not Quite" (arXiv:2507.19473, RecSys 2025) makes the **norm of its content-init delta its main hyperparameter** ("too small ⇒ not enough flexibility; too large ⇒ embeddings move too far ⇒ worse cold") — independent confirmation that cold-item magnitude is load-bearing. Internal: cold-synth (2026-06-23-1, the confirmed lever this refines); the user-mode titration (connectivity = causal cold axis).
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (a) **cold-synth (2026-06-23-1)** — sets direction via softmax-kNN average; leaves the contracted norm as-is. This restores that norm; **the entire delta is the magnitude rescale, a clean A/B vs the on-disk `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}`.** (b) **kriging-cold (2026-06-23-2, QUEUED)** — changes the neighbor *weights* (declustering = direction), then renormalizes weights to sum 1 ⇒ output STILL norm-contracted; it is **orthogonal** to this (direction vs magnitude) and the two could stack. (c) **X1 James–Stein (DEAD, c≈0)** — *shrinks* warm ID norms TOWARD the cluster mean (reduces magnitude, a regularizer voted off on warm items); this is the OPPOSITE operation (restores/increases magnitude) on COLD-only synthesized rows, parameter-free (nothing to vote off). (d) **GD1 spectral-shrink (DEAD)** — denoises the WARM Gram matrix; this rescales COLD imputed rows (different operand, different sign). (e) **Meehan 2510.11402** — compresses over-inflated *generative-model* cold norms for popularity FAIRNESS; this restores under-inflated *kNN-averaged* cold norms for cold-item ACCURACY (opposite direction, different estimator, different objective). **No ledger probe — and no cited paper — applies a parameter-free norm-restoration to a kNN-imputed cold ID row in sequential rec.** Genuinely new.
- **Mechanism / how to implement:** New flag `--cold-synth-norm` (`store_true`, default OFF ⇒ cold path **bit-identical to cold-synth**). It reuses the ENTIRE cold-synth machinery (`--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`/`--cold-synth-knn`/`--cold-synth-temp`; precompute buffers `cold_synth_nbr`/`cold_synth_wgt`/`cold_synth_is_cold` at `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1893–1906` UNCHANGED). The ONLY change is **3 lines inserted in `item_features()` immediately after `synth` is built (`:743`) and before the `torch.where` (`:744`)**:
  ```
  synth = (self.item_emb(nbr) * w.unsqueeze(-1)).sum(dim=-2)        # (..., d)  [existing :743]
  if getattr(self, "cold_synth_norm", False):                      # NEW, cold-only
      nbr_norm = self.item_emb(nbr).norm(dim=-1)                    # (..., K) warm-neighbor ID norms (current learned rows)
      tgt = (nbr_norm * w).sum(dim=-1, keepdim=True)               # (..., 1) weighted-mean neighbor norm = local target scale
      synth = synth * (tgt / synth.norm(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-8))   # restore magnitude, keep direction
  e = torch.where(is_cold.unsqueeze(-1), synth, e)                 # [existing :744]
  ```
  (Plumb `cold_synth_norm=args.cold_synth_norm` through the model constructor next to `cold_synth_knn`; add the argparse line next to `--cold-synth-knn` at `:1675`.) Math: cold-synth gives `s = Σ wₖ eₖ` with `‖s‖ ≤ τ_i = Σ wₖ‖eₖ‖`; the rescale sets `e_cold = s·(τ_i/‖s‖)` ⇒ same unit direction `s/‖s‖`, magnitude lifted to the local neighbor scale `τ_i`. **Zero-init / no-op:** `--cold-synth-norm` absent ⇒ branch skipped ⇒ cold-synth verbatim; with no `--cold-item-frac` no item is cold ⇒ inert. **Parameter-free** (`τ_i` is a deterministic function of frozen text-weights + current warm rows — nothing learned, nothing to vote off, unlike conn-gate α/W1 β/X1 c/Y1 T). **Leak-free:** identical guards to cold-synth — warm ID rows are train-only; weights from frozen `sbert_emb`; cold rows removed from training, valid/test TARGETS only; warm-item scoring byte-identical (branch is `is_cold`-gated). Cost: two extra reductions over the (b·K·d) neighbor block at eval — milliseconds. **Interpretable readout to log every run (decides the mechanism regardless of Δ):** mean `‖s‖` (pre-restore synth norm), mean `τ_i` (target), their ratio `r = mean‖s‖/mean τ_i`, and mean warm-item ID norm `μ_w`. If `r < 1` (predicted) the contraction pathology is real and restoration has headroom; if `r ≈ 1` the neighborhoods are near-collinear, restoration ≈ no-op, and it collapses to cold-synth (clean neutral). Also log mean cold-item logit before/after to confirm the magnitude lift raises cold scores.
- **Exact command (executor runs this — refines the DONE cold-synth arm; SAME `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07` so it pairs EXACTLY against the on-disk `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}`, seed 20260608 first then 5-seed if the cold gap looks real):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cold-synth-knn 20 --cold-synth-temp 0.07 --cold-synth-norm --seed 20260608 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_COLDSYNTHNORM_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_COLDSYNTHNORM_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` — then, ONLY IF the single-seed cold gap vs cold-synth is positive, 5-seed `20260608–12` paired against the existing `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}.json` (identical split). (Optional follow-up: stack on kriging by adding `--krige-cold` once 2026-06-23-2 lands — magnitude × direction; NOT required for this proposal.)
- **Gate (cold-start axis — the SAME pre-registered anti-fishing gate as conn-gate/cold-synth/kriging, NOT the 0.0760 overall gate):** smoke 2ep (no NaN; cold rows norm-restored, **warm rows bit-identical to cold-synth/text-stack**, `by_coldstart` populated, the `r = mean‖s‖/mean τ_i` ratio logged) → single-seed MI → if cold NDCG@{10,100}/MRR vs cold-synth is positive, **5-seed paired (norm-restored − cold-synth, IDENTICAL `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, MI, seeds 20260608–12)**; **95% CI EXCLUDES 0 and positive on cold NDCG ⇒ a §5.5 mechanism+method contribution** ("magnitude restoration of the kNN-imputed cold row beats flat-norm content-CF transfer — the averaging-induced norm-contraction is a real, fixable cold-start pathology"); **CI includes 0 ⇒ §5.5 Table 2 neutral/negative row** ("norm restoration buys nothing on cold; cold-synth's contracted magnitude is not the binding limiter" — still publishable, and the logged `r` ratio explains why). Report cold NDCG@10 **absolute + rel% vs cold-synth + cold hit-count (@10 AND @100)** + warm-no-op sanity + the `r`/μ_w norm readouts. **@10 is near-floor (~8 hits/8748) — primary statistical power is on NDCG@100 / n_hit@100 (cold-synth has ~125 hits there) and cold MRR; do NOT make single-seed rel% claims (the conn-gate s08 +33% mirage is the standing precedent).**
- **Kill criterion:** norm-restored ≤ cold-synth on cold NDCG/MRR at 5 seeds (CI includes 0) ⇒ neutral/negative §5.5 row, NOT a regression. Guardrails: if the logged `r ≈ 1` (neighborhoods near-collinear ⇒ no contraction to fix) the run is a near-exact no-op vs cold-synth ⇒ expected clean-neutral, stop (do not sweep). If cold metrics COLLAPSE below cold-synth (over-restoration pushing cold rows to dominate and mis-rank), the local-`τ_i` target is too aggressive — log it, STOP, do NOT escalate to a global-`μ_w` target or sweep (one parameter-free setting only; a magnitude sweep would re-enter the killed "tune a scalar on V2" axis).
- **Expected delta & rationale:** HONEST and bounded. This does NOT change the order of magnitude of the absolute cold result (~0.0004 MI, a PROOF-OF-MECHANISM ≪ LC2C ~0.057 and the hardware-blocked 0.0760) and is NOT a path to absolute SOTA. Its value: it attacks the **first-order** scoring axis (magnitude directly scales every logit) that cold-synth and kriging both leave on the table (both are direction-only, norm-contracted), so its expected lift is **plausibly larger than kriging's second-order declustering** — realistic cold NDCG@10/@100/MRR delta vs cold-synth **+0.0001 to +0.0006** (could roughly double the cold hit-count 8→15–30 at @10 and lift @100 more), concentrated where there is mass (@100, MRR). The precondition is mechanistically near-certain to hold (`r<1` by Jensen unless neighborhoods are collinear — and the kriging proposal already measured tight, redundant-but-not-identical neighborhoods, top-1 cosine 0.819 ⇒ genuine directional spread ⇒ genuine contraction), and the A/B is near-deterministic (both imputations fixed given frozen text + converged warm rows ⇒ tight paired CIs like cold-synth's t=37), giving real power to resolve a small Δ. **Either outcome is a clean, publishable §5.5 result** that isolates DIRECTION vs MAGNITUDE in content-CF cold imputation — and the `r`/μ_w readout is itself a reportable finding (is kNN cold imputation magnitude-limited?). Pure-PyTorch, ~same runtime as cold-synth (a few ms extra at eval), single GPU job, parameter-free, cold-only no-op ⇒ zero warm/overall-metric risk.

## PROPOSAL 2026-07-02-1 — Cold-item ID-DROP (text-fallback): zero the untrained noise ID row for zero-interaction items and let the trained additive text projection carry them — the DROP alternative to cold-synth's IMPUTE  [status: DONE — REFUTED (COLLAPSE): MI seed08 cold NDCG@10 0.000000 (0 hits@10), NDCG@100 0.000282 (15 hits@100), MRR 0.000351 — WORSE than cold-synth (0.000342 / 7 / 125) and ~35× below the standalone --sbert-only-HSTU ceiling (0.003108 / 66 / 536). overall/warm intact (0.03694 ≈ cold-synth 0.03717 = competitor-set band, warm sanity PASS). MECHANISM CORRECTION: the "additive-wiring failure → drop recovers the text-alone ceiling" hypothesis is REFUTED — sbert_proj in the full stack is trained as a RESIDUAL on top of the (trained warm) item_emb, NOT a standalone encoder, so zeroing the cold ID row at eval leaves a residual text projection that does NOT stand alone in the full-stack geometry. The --sbert-only ceiling (0.0031) came from sbert_proj TRAINED standalone; it is unreachable by an eval-time drop. cold-synth (IMPUTE) remains the best true-cold arm. Pre-registered COLLAPSE-stop fired → single-seed, no 5-seed. Per kill criterion, do NOT chain a train-time text-anchored variant without a SUPERVISOR directive (CONVERGENCE RULE). Implemented --cold-id-drop default-OFF (constructor cold_id_drop + item_features torch.where zeros the cold ID row BEFORE the cold-synth block; is_cold mask built from cold_items even at --cold-synth-knn 0). py_compile OK; 2ep smoke no-NaN. Files: results_COLDIDDROP_MI_seed20260608.json. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-02. 2026-07-02]

- **⚠ GROUNDING = a fresh on-disk NUMBER, not a hunch (read this first).** The SUPERVISOR-sanctioned `--sbert-only` cold diagnostic HAS NOW RUN (single seed, MI, `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, seed 20260608) and it **overturns the pipeline's headline cold mechanism.** I recomputed from disk (read-only venv), `by_coldstart.cold`, n=8748 cold targets:
  - **cold-synth** (best imputation arm): cold NDCG@10 = **0.000342**, hit@10 = **7**, hit@100 = **125**.
  - **norm-restore** (2026-06-23-3, DONE-REFUTED): cold NDCG@10 = **0.000**, hit@10 = **0**, hit@100 = **22** (magnitude-fix backfired).
  - **`--sbert-only` (SASRec/default enc):** cold NDCG@10 = **0.001627**, hit@10 = **37**, hit@100 = **378**.
  - **`--sbert-only` (`--encoder hstu`):** cold NDCG@10 = **0.003108**, hit@10 = **66**, hit@100 = **536**.
  ⇒ scoring a cold item by **text alone** beats the best *imputation* arm by **~5×–9×** on @10 and ~3×–4× on @100. Interpretation (ANALYST owns the formal verdict): the cold-zero of the additive stack is an **additive-WIRING failure** — the untrained noise `item_emb` row (`~N(0,0.02)`, never gradient-updated) is ADDED to `sbert_proj(text)` (`run_sasrec_sbert.py:817`) and its random direction drowns the real text signal — NOT "frozen text is fundamentally insufficient on cold" (the analyst's predicted mechanism, hereby REFUTED by this number). The winning cold representation is the **text projection itself**, so the whole impute-a-CF-row family (cold-synth → kriging → norm-restore) has been chasing an *inferior* representation. The obvious, untried, parameter-free lever is to **DROP the noise row for cold items** instead of imputing it.

- **Idea (novel):** New default-OFF flag `--cold-id-drop`. For TRUE-COLD items only (`item_users==0`, the `--cold-item-frac` hold-out set — ID row is pure untrained noise), **zero the learned `item_emb` row** in `item_features()`, leaving the item represented by the *already-trained* additive frozen-text path `sbert_proj(sbert_table(i))` (+ the TAPE prototype term `m`, if on). Warm items are byte-untouched. This is the **DROP** counterpart to cold-synth's **IMPUTE**: same hook, same cold mask, same protocol — a clean A/B that isolates "remove the corrupting noise row" from "reconstruct a CF row from warm neighbors." The diagnostic says removing beats reconstructing.

- **Source(s):** DropoutNet (Volkovs, Yu, Poutanen, NeurIPS 2017) — *training-time* random dropout of the CF/preference input to force content reliance; the conceptual ancestor. "Let It Go? Not Quite: Addressing Item Cold Start in Sequential Recommendations with Content-Based Initialization" (arXiv:2507.19473, RecSys 2025) — content *initialization* + a small trainable delta; explicitly reports that *directly using frozen content embeddings is suboptimal* and that *fine-tuning drifts cold items*. In-house grounding: the on-disk `results_COLD_sbertonly_{,hstu_}MI_seed20260608.json` vs `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260608.json` numbers above. (Cross-field's teed-up Procrustes/Helmert direction-registration idea, CROSSFIELD_IDEAS XF-2026-07-02-2, attacks the SAME direction axis via a learned-free rotation and stays HELD under the supervisor rate-limit; this drop-lever is the cheaper, more-direct first test of the same "the residual gap is representation/direction, not magnitude" hypothesis the norm-restore refutation surfaced.)

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (1) **cold-synth (2026-06-23-1, DONE):** replaces the cold ID row with a frozen-text-kNN weighted sum of *warm* ID rows (`item_features():758-771`). This DROPS the ID row to zero instead — a strictly simpler, parameter-free operation predicted (by the sbert-only number) to beat it, and it is a clean same-hook A/B. (2) **Z1 fitness-gate (DEAD, tail −75%):** a *soft, frequency-graded* damping `g = 1 − α·σ(...)` applied to the ID channel of *all low-frequency items INCLUDING WARM-TAIL* items (`:788-791`) — it crashed because it discarded the *real (if sparse) ID signal* warm-tail items still carry. This is fundamentally different: it acts ONLY on `item_users==0` items whose ID row is **pure noise with ZERO real signal to lose** — a hard binary drop on cold, not a graded damp on warm-tail. (3) **conn-gate / CF1 cue-fusion (DEAD both regimes):** learnable/soft ID⊕text *blends* across all items (`:797-814`); this is a parameter-free *hard binary* drop on the cold subset only. (4) **`--sbert-only`:** applies text-only to the WHOLE catalog (collapses warm CF) — a diagnostic, not deployable; this keeps warm items on the full stack and is deployable.

- **Mechanism / how to implement (where the flag goes; the math; zero-init no-op):**
  1. **Constructor:** add `cold_id_drop=False` arg (mirror the `sbert_only` plumbing at `:327,349`); store `self.cold_id_drop = bool(cold_id_drop)`.
  2. **Cold mask:** the `cold_synth_is_cold` boolean buffer (True for `item_users==0` cold items) is currently registered ONLY when `cold_synth_knn>0` (`:1936-1939`, built from `cold_idx_t`). Relax that guard so the buffer is built whenever `cold_items is not None AND (args.cold_id_drop OR args.cold_synth_knn>0)`, and register it (as `cold_synth_is_cold`, reuse the name) so `item_features` can read it.
  3. **`item_features()` — insert immediately after `e = self.item_emb(item_ids)` (`:741`), BEFORE the cold-synth block:**
     ```
     if getattr(self, "cold_id_drop", False):
         is_cold = self.cold_synth_is_cold[item_ids]            # (...,) bool
         if bool(is_cold.any()):
             e = torch.where(is_cold.unsqueeze(-1), torch.zeros_like(e), e)
     ```
     The existing additive-text line `e = e + self.sbert_proj(s)` (`:817`) then gives each cold item `e_cold = 0 + sbert_proj(text) (+ m TAPE)`; warm items keep `e = item_emb + sbert_proj(text)`. **Mutually exclusive with cold-synth** (both write the cold slot) — run with `--cold-synth-knn 0` (default). Applied in BOTH train (cold rows are negative candidates in the full-catalog softmax denominator, `:~934`, so the drop makes cold negatives = their text projection, consistent with eval) and eval — exactly as cold-synth's `torch.where` already behaves.
  4. **Zero-init / no-op:** flag OFF ⇒ block skipped ⇒ **bit-identical** to the text-stack baseline. Flag ON with `--cold-item-frac 0` ⇒ `is_cold` all-False ⇒ `torch.where` is an exact no-op. No new learnable parameter (nothing to "vote itself off" — the ledger's recurring failure mode for α/β/c/T scalars).
  5. **Leak-free:** the cold set is defined purely by TRAIN frequency (`--cold-item-frac`/`--cold-item-seed`, `:1774-1788`) — identical leak-free construction cold-synth already passed; masking a cold item's ID row consults NO val/test target.

- **Exact command (executor runs this VERBATIM):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cold-id-drop --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_COLDIDDROP_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_COLDIDDROP_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1`

- **Gate (cold-start axis — the SAME pre-registered anti-fishing gate as cold-synth/kriging, NOT the 0.0760 overall gate):** smoke 2ep (no NaN; **warm NDCG@10 within ±0.001 of the paired text-stack run** — NOT bit-identical, per cycle-16 FIX #1: zeroed cold rows still shift the training softmax competitor-set; `by_coldstart` populated) → single-seed MI → **IF cold NDCG@{10,100}/MRR beats cold-synth (0.000342 / 0.002745 / 0.001058)**, run **5-seed paired (cold-id-drop − cold-synth, IDENTICAL `--cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1`, MI, seeds 20260608–12)** → **95% CI EXCLUDES 0 and positive ⇒ §5.5 mechanism+method contribution** ("dropping the noise ID row beats reconstructing it — the cold-zero is an additive-wiring pathology, and text-fallback is the strongest true-cold arm found"); **CI includes 0 ⇒ §5.5 neutral row.** Report cold NDCG@10 **absolute + rel% vs cold-synth + hit-count @10 AND @100** + the warm ±0.001 sanity. **No single-seed rel% claim** (the conn-gate s08 mirage precedent). Escalate to strict review ONLY if 5-seed overall crosses 0.0760 (it will not).

- **Kill criterion:** cold-id-drop ≤ cold-synth on cold NDCG/MRR at 5 seeds (CI incl 0) ⇒ neutral/negative §5.5 row (NOT a regression). If cold metrics = 0 / collapse (worse than cold-synth), the additive `sbert_proj` — trained as a *residual* on top of warm `item_emb`, not as a standalone representation — does NOT stand alone in the full-stack geometry: record that as the informative negative (it would then motivate a train-time text-anchored variant, but do NOT chain one without a SUPERVISOR directive — the CONVERGENCE RULE bounds the cold arms).

- **Expected delta & rationale (HONEST, bounded, and directly quantified by the diagnostic):** the on-disk `--sbert-only` numbers (cold NDCG@10 0.001627 SASRec / 0.003108 HSTU) are the approximate CEILING this lever reaches for — cold-id-drop removes exactly the corrupting term (`item_emb` noise) that separates the additive stack (cold=0) from text-alone. Realistic cold NDCG@10 vs cold-synth: **+0.0003 to +0.0025 (≈2×–9×), hit@10 7→~15–60, hit@100 125→~300–500.** **The one real caveat, stated plainly:** `sbert_proj` was trained as an *additive residual* on top of the (trained, warm) `item_emb`, NOT as a standalone encoder like the sbert-only run's `sbert_proj` was — so the full-stack cold-id-drop may recover LESS than the text-only-trained ceiling; that recovery gap is itself a reportable readout (does the additive text channel stand alone?). Bounded honesty: even at the HSTU ceiling 0.0031, this is **≪ LC2C ~0.057 and ≪ the hardware-blocked 0.0760 overall — a proof-of-mechanism + the strongest true-cold arm, NOT a deployable cold-start SOTA; no SOTA_ACHIEVED.** Value = (a) very likely the new best cold arm by a multiple, (b) a mechanism CORRECTION that reframes the §5.5 cold-start story (additive-wiring failure, not frozen-text-insufficiency; impute-the-row was the wrong axis), (c) a cheap (~10 min/seed MI) high-information probe. **Note for EXPERIMENT/SUPERVISOR (sequencing, not defiance of the rate-limit):** this is NOT a 4th *imputation* spec — it is the actionable read of the sanctioned `--sbert-only` diagnostic, which resolved 5–9× ABOVE floor (so the cycle-17 NO-PIVOT "all three near-floor" condition is already broken by real signal). Its diagnostic-implied EV exceeds kriging's (kriging is a *second-order declustering of the same inferior imputed-row family* whose ceiling is cold-synth's ~0.0003; this targets the text-alone ~0.003 ceiling). Single GPU job; run when the card is free; ordering vs kriging is EXPERIMENT/SUPERVISOR's call. ⇒ zero warm/overall-metric risk.

## PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1 — Credibility-routed dual-space merge: rank each item in the scoring space its observation-count makes CREDIBLE (Bühlmann Z=n/(n+k) over a scale-EQUATED CF-space + homogeneous-text-space), non-learnable — the score-level exploit of the sbert-only(66)-vs-cold_id_drop(0) double-dissociation  [status: DONE-REFUTED (2026-07-03, EXPERIMENT cycle) — train-consistent --cred-route-train IMPLEMENTED + RAN (MI seed08, HSTU, cold-frac 0.15). DEPLOYABLE @10 GATE FAILS: cold hits@10 = 3 (NOT > 7 cold-synth ceiling); warm N@10 = 0.04369 PASS (>=0.040). Per the pre-registered rule (BOTH required to escalate), a @10 floor => REFUTED, single-seed, NO 5-seed => cold-start branch DEFINITIVELY CLOSED. STRONGER partial-positive than eval-only though: BEST warm-preserving arm at deep-K — cold hits@100 236 (~1.9x cold-synth's 125, > eval-only 163), hits@50 85, cold N@100 0.004736, cold MRR 0.001318 (all best of the warm-preserving arms), warm fully preserved 0.0437. => the impossibility boundary the supervisor pre-described: even the parameter-free joint-routing exploit (score+loss level) cannot rank cold to TOP-10 while preserving warm in a pure sequential stack; a co-trained architecture would be required to reach sbert-only's 66@10 or LC2C ~0.057 (out of CONVERGENCE-RULE scope, needs a fresh SUPERVISOR directive). No SOTA (overall 0.037 << 0.0760; cold << LC2C 0.057). Cold-start experimental program COMPLETE => revert to PAPER-FINISHING. Ledger: NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-03 (train-consistent entry).]
- **Idea (novel):** The cold-start program has mapped a clean, 5-seed **double-dissociation** (ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-2; numbers re-verified from disk this cycle): the warm-trained **full-stack CF space** wins WARM (N@10 0.0438) but floors COLD (0 hits@10); the **homogeneous frozen-text space** (`--sbert-only`) wins COLD (N@10 0.00295±0.0001, **64.8±1.9 hits@10, 5/5 seeds**) but destroys WARM (0.016). Neither model is globally best — the two channels' reliabilities **cross over with observation count**. The analyst concluded this is a "structural tension … needs a fundamentally different architecture (joint text-CF co-training)." This proposal is the **constructive test** of that claim: instead of one model doing both, **rank each candidate item in the space its own train-count makes credible** — warm items by the CF score, cold items by the (scale-equated) text score, in ONE merged list — via a **non-learnable actuarial credibility weight `Z_j=n_j/(n_j+k)`**. Two variants, cheap→headline: (1) an **eval-only** re-rank of an already-trained full-stack model (≈free); (2) a **train-consistent credibility-routed dual-space softmax** that trains `sbert_proj` to *score standalone* for low-count items — the parameter-free realization of the analyst's prescribed co-training, in ONE run, with no learned gate.
- **Source(s):** Actuarial credibility theory — Bühlmann (1967), `Z=n/(n+k)`, `k=EPV/VHM` (Loss-Data-Analytics Ch.9; SOA STAM). Psychometric test-equating — equipercentile / IRT common-item linking (Kolen & Brennan; Spearman–Brown 1910 lineage; ETS Livingston). Grounds CROSSFIELD **XF 2026-07-03-1**. CS prior-art vetted-against: MWUF meta scale/shift (arXiv:2105.04790), adversarial-VAE cold warm-up (arXiv:2302.14395) — both LEARNED representation-level single-space maps; none computes two scores in two spaces and psychometrically equates + count-routes them.
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (a) **`--zfusion-eval`** (already in code, `run_sasrec_sbert.py:1362–1381`) fuses the full-stack score with a **user mean-text-profile COSINE**, **flat 50/50** `_zc(final)+_zc(content)` for ALL items. This differs on BOTH axes: the text channel is the **item's homogeneous-text projection** `h @ sbert_proj(sbert_table).T` (= the sbert-only scorer that demonstrably ranks 66 cold items), NOT a user-profile cosine; and the weight is **per-item Bühlmann `Z_j=n_j/(n_j+k)`**, NOT flat. (b) **conn-gate / Z1 / CF1 / X1** (DEAD, L803–836) all fuse channels **inside ONE score/space at the representation level with a learned/freq-damped scalar** — the `cold_id_drop`(0)-vs-`sbert-only`(66) contrast is direct evidence that class *structurally* floors (it never escapes the CF-space softmax denominator dominated by warm high-norm rows, and never equates scales). This is the FIRST **score-level, dual-space, scale-equated, count-routed** merge. (c) **cold-synth / kriging / norm / ID-DROP** (imputation family, near-floor) all rewrite the cold **ROW** inside the CF space; this leaves rows alone and fixes the **ranking SPACE**. (d) **c4 ensemble** (DEAD as single-model headline) averaged *homogeneous* models for overall NDCG; this is a *heterogeneous* (CF vs text) per-item-credibility route targeting the cold/warm dissociation. Critically, conn-gate/CF1's death does **not** bind: they were tuned on the WARM tail (n>0, ID row has signal) *before* the cold split existed; the fresh sbert-only 5-seed proves that at the TRUE-COLD endpoint (n=0) the text-only regime — this router's `Z→0` limit — wins ~9×.
- **Mechanism / how to implement (pure-PyTorch; NON-learnable; flag `--credibility-route`, default OFF ⇒ scoring bit-identical):** two catalog score vectors, both already constructible. `s_full = h @ all_item_features().T` (existing CF space `item_emb+sbert_proj+m`, eval scorer L1360). `s_txt = h @ Ptext.T`, `Ptext = sbert_proj(sbert_table(arange(n_items)))` (the homogeneous text space = the `sbert_only` branch L751-753; a second projection of the SAME `h`, precompute `Ptext` once per eval like `all_items`). **(1) Scale-EQUATE per query (test-equating step, parameter-free):** `z_full=(s_full−μ_full)/σ_full`, `z_txt=(s_txt−μ_txt)/σ_txt`, moments over the `n_items` candidates (reuse the existing `_zc()` helper at L1378). **(2) Credibility-route per candidate (Bühlmann):** `Z_j=item_freq_j/(item_freq_j+k)` from the train-only `item_freq` buffer (register it unconditionally when the flag is on; `item_freq` already built at L636/669, leak-free), `k` a single non-learnable hyperparameter (`--cred-k`, default 2.0; swept `{0.5,1,2,5}` at eval for free). **Merged score `s_j = Z_j·z_full_j + (1−Z_j)·z_txt_j`.** At `n_j=0` ⇒ `Z_j=0` ⇒ pure equated-text ranking (= the sbert-only regime, 66 cold hits); at large `n_j` ⇒ `Z_j→1` ⇒ CF space (warm 0.044). **EVAL-ONLY variant (cheap first probe):** apply the merge at the eval scorer ONLY (insert after L1360, guarded by the flag, exactly where `--zfusion-eval` injects at L1370) — a pure inference-time re-rank of the already-trained model; training untouched. **TRAIN-CONSISTENT variant (headline, `--cred-route-train`):** also route the training loss so `sbert_proj` gets gradient to score standalone for low-Z targets while `item_emb` trains for high-Z targets — the fix ANALYSIS 2026-07-02-2 Finding 2 says is required (in the additive stack `sbert_proj` is only ever a *small residual correction*, so an eval-time text channel may not stand alone). Cheapest faithful realization inside `chunked_full_softmax_loss` (L947+): a per-target credibility-weighted sum of two CE terms `L = mean_t[ Z_{tgt_t}·CE(s_full,tgt_t) + (1−Z_{tgt_t})·CE(s_txt,tgt_t) ]` — cold/low-count targets supervise the text space, warm targets the CF space; no learned scalar to vote off (contrast conn-gate α≈0.001, X1 c≈0, Y1 T→0, W1 β=−7.42 — every learnable cold/tail scalar collapsed; `Z=n/(n+k)` cannot). **ZERO-INIT NO-OP:** flag OFF ⇒ eval + loss untouched ⇒ bit-identical; and with no `--cold-item-frac` all items are warm (large `n_j`) ⇒ `Z_j≈1` ⇒ merge ≈ `z_full` ⇒ warm-safe even when ON. **LEAK-FREE:** `n_j` train-only `item_freq`; z-standardization is over candidates (no label consulted); text frozen; and the router *down-weights* the ID channel for LOW-count items (`Z_j` small) — the OPPOSITE of head/popularity inflation, so it cannot exploit the full-catalog-NDCG popularity-hit reward the supervisor's eval-touching caution warns about (report warm/overall as the sanity guard).
- **Exact command (executor runs this — EVAL-ONLY probe FIRST, cheapest/most-reversible, MI, single-seed; paired against on-disk `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260608.json` cold 0.000342/7-hit and `results_COLD_sbertonly_hstu_MI_seed20260608.json` cold 0.003108/66-hit):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --credibility-route --cred-k 2.0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_CREDROUTE_evalonly_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_CREDROUTE_evalonly_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` — then, **only if the eval-only cold beats the cold-synth ceiling (>7 hits@10 / >125 hits@100) without denting warm (>0.040)**, escalate to the TRAIN-CONSISTENT headline (add `--cred-route-train`, new `--out …results_CREDROUTE_train_MI_seed20260608.json`); then 5-seed `20260608–12` if the single-seed clears the gate.
- **Gate:** smoke 2ep (no NaN; flag OFF ⇒ bit-identical; flag ON + no `--cold-item-frac` ⇒ warm N@10 within ±0.001 of text-stack, competitor-set shift not bit-identical per cycle-16 FIX #1; `by_coldstart` populated; `Ptext` matmul + per-query moments finite) → EVAL-ONLY single-seed MI (sweep `--cred-k {0.5,1,2,5}`, ≈free) → **IF cold NDCG@{10,100}/MRR beats cold-synth (0.000342 / 0.002745 / 0.001058) with warm ≥0.040** ⇒ TRAIN-CONSISTENT single-seed → **IF cold approaches the sbert-only ceiling (~0.003 / ~66 hits@10) with warm within noise of full-stack (~0.044)** ⇒ **5-seed paired vs BOTH `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed{08–12}` AND `results_COLD_sbertonly_hstu_MI_seed{08–12}`**, 95% CI, report cold **absolute + rel% + hit-count(@10 AND @100) + warm/overall no-regression**. This is the **cold-start axis** gate (as cold-synth/kriging), NOT the 0.0760 overall gate; escalate to strict review only if 5-seed overall crosses 0.0760 (it will not — this targets the cold axis). **No single-seed rel% claim** (conn-gate s08 mirage precedent).
- **Kill criterion:** if BOTH eval-only AND train-consistent single-seed land cold ≤ the cold-synth ceiling (≤7 hits@10, ≤125 hits@100), OR warm collapses below ~0.040 (text-primary contamination of the warm regime) ⇒ the linear z-equate cannot co-rank the two spaces in one model ⇒ **DEAD**; record the honest §5.5 characterization ("the channel-reliability crossover is real but NOT exploitable by a linear score-level route — the double-dissociation stands as the finding; a single deployable model needs full text-CF co-training"). Pre-vetted successor (do NOT chain without a SUPERVISOR directive per the CONVERGENCE RULE): **equipercentile/rank equating** (the non-linear psychometric upgrade, same dual-space frame).
- **Expected delta & rationale (HONEST, bounded):** the frontier this reaches on cold is **bounded by the on-disk sbert-only endpoint** (cold N@10 0.00295, 64.8±1.9 hits@10) — at `n_j=0` the merge *is* the equated text ranking, and the train-consistent variant trains that space to score. Best case = a **single deployable model that keeps full-stack warm (~0.044) AND recovers most of sbert-only's cold (~0.003, ~60 hits@10)** — a ~9× lift over the warm-preserving cold-synth ceiling (7 hits) with NO warm sacrifice, which would **directly refute the analyst's "structural tension is unavoidable"** by achieving both endpoints with a parameter-free actuarial router instead of full co-training. Realistic per-seed prediction: cold N@10 **+0.0003→+0.0027 vs cold-synth, hit@10 7→~15–60**, warm within ±0.003 of 0.044. **Expected-negative case (per Finding 2, honestly likely for the EVAL-ONLY probe):** the additive `sbert_proj` direction is a weak residual and, even magnitude-equated, floors cold like ID-DROP did — that is the *informative* negative that motivates the train-consistent escalation (whose whole point is to fix the training regime). Either way publishable. **Bounded honesty:** even at the sbert-only ceiling 0.003, this is **≪ LC2C ~0.057 and orthogonal to the hardware-blocked 0.0760 (warm/overall) — a mechanism + frontier result, NOT a deployable cold-start SOTA; no SOTA_ACHIEVED.** Value = (a) the constructive test of the campaign's own impossibility claim (exploitable-in-one-model?), (b) the double-dissociation figure is itself the paper-grade cold-start finding, (c) cheap (eval-only ≈free; train-consistent ~10 min/seed MI). **Sequencing note for EXPERIMENT/SUPERVISOR (not defiance of the rate-limit):** this is NOT a new *imputation* spec — it is the actionable exploit of the sanctioned `--sbert-only` diagnostic, which resolved 5–9× ABOVE floor (so cycle-17's NO-PIVOT "all three near-floor" condition is broken by real signal, and my XF reopening condition fired). It stays inside the bounded cold-start program (tests the mapped dissociation, opens no new axis). Single GPU job; run when the card is free; ordering vs any remaining kriging follow-up is EXPERIMENT/SUPERVISOR's call.

## PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-2 — Hui–Walter conditional-independence dual-head: an error-DECORRELATION penalty that turns the co-trained text head into a STANDALONE-calibrated cold scorer (the missing term that floored `--cred-route-train`), then route by observation-count credibility  [status: DONE — FLOOR/DEAD. MI single-seed λ-frontier {0.3,1.0,3.0 pearson; 1.0 hsic} ALL floor cold hits@10 ≤ 5 ≪ kriging's 12 (pearson 0.3/1.0=1, 3.0=0, hsic=5; parent cred-route-train=3) while warm N@10 stays ~0.044. Per pre-registered kill criterion (FLOOR at every swept λ ⇒ DEAD): forced conditional-independence between two co-trained heads CANNOT recover cold @10 — the co-training door is CLOSED, the cold-start program is terminal. Nuance: hsic (nonlinear) recovers cold hits@100=225 > kriging's 141 = some coarse standalone-text signal, but it never sharpens to @10. WIN gate NOT met, NO escalation to 5-seed/VG. NO SOTA. Kriging remains the confirmed warm-preserving cold arm. See NOVEL_ALGO_PLAN.md 2026-07-03.]

- **Idea (novel):** The credibility-router was REFUTED (PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1: cold hits@10 = 3 < the cold-synth ceiling of 7, ≪ sbert-only's 66) for one pinpointed reason (cross-field post-mortem XF 2026-07-03-2 / cycle-19): the frozen-text channel `s_txt = h @ Ptext.T` is a **trained RESIDUAL head** — even under `--cred-route-train`'s two-head deep-supervision loss `L = Z·CE(s_full) + (1−Z)·CE(s_txt)`, the two heads *co-adapt into correlated errors* (the text head drifts back to being the ID head's shadow), so at the `Z→0` cold limit there is no standalone-calibrated text ordering to route into. Deep-supervision makes each head *individually scored*; it does **not** make their errors *conditionally independent*. This proposal adds the one missing term — a **conditional-independence / error-decorrelation penalty** (Hui–Walter latent-class two-imperfect-tests; co-training theory's enforced view-independence; realized as a batch Pearson→HSIC decorrelation of the two heads' per-example errors given the target). Forcing the two channels toward conditionally-independent errors turns the text head into a genuinely standalone relevance metric (the sbert-only regime that scores 64.8 cold), so the already-built count-credibility eval route (`--credibility-route`, `Z=n/(n+k)`) finally has a *calibrated* text space to land the cold limit in — targeting sbert-only's cold ordering WITHOUT the ~60–70% warm collapse, in ONE deployable model.

- **Source(s):** Hui & Walter (1980), latent-class evaluation of two imperfect diagnostic tests with **no gold standard** under conditional independence given true status (nature.com/articles/srep11861; NCBI PMC4493568; WOAH Bayesian-LCM notes). Co-training conditional-independence assumption — Blum & Mitchell (1998); "must be enforced, rarely holds" (arXiv:1708.04403). HSIC as the differentiable decorrelation measure — Gretton et al., "Measuring Statistical Dependence with Hilbert–Schmidt Norms." Deep supervision (each branch carries its own task loss) — dl.acm.org/doi/10.1145/3698767. Actuarial credibility `Z=n/(n+k)` — Bühlmann (1967). Grounds CROSSFIELD **XF 2026-07-03-3 → XF 2026-07-03-4** (green-lit by SUPERVISOR cycle-19). CS prior-art vetted-against (novelty-diff below): MAMEX (arXiv:2508.08042), CMCLRec (SIGIR'24, dl.acm.org/10.1145/3626772.3657839), SaviorRec (arXiv:2508.01375), CFSM (PMC11397954), generic two-tower-gating (ContextGNN arXiv:2411.19513).

- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (1) **`--cred-route-train` (PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1, DONE-REFUTED, cold hits@10 = 3):** the DIRECT parent — it already ships the two-head deep-supervision loss and computes both heads' per-position CE (`chunked_full_softmax_loss` L1091–1101) plus the eval route. It floors cold because it has **deep supervision but NO decorrelation** — the exact gap this fills; this proposal is literally `--cred-route-train` **+ one added loss term**. (2) **In-field's own pre-registered successor (this file, line 868):** a credibility-WEIGHTED joint loss `L = mean_t[Z·CE(s_CF) + (1−Z)·CE(s_text)]` — that is *identical to `--cred-route-train`* (deep-supervision + count-weighting only) and therefore inherits its co-adaptation failure; this proposal ADDS the conditional-independence term that spec lacks. (3) **CF1 cue-fusion / conn-gate / Z1 / X1 (DEAD):** all fuse channels inside ONE space with a learned/freq-damped scalar; none trains two *standalone* metrics and none decorrelates their errors. (4) **the credibility-router eval (REFUTED):** z-equated a residual head at eval; this manufactures the standalone head the router assumed existed. **The novel axis = a conditional-independence error-decorrelation co-training constraint; nothing in the ~60-probe ledger decorrelates two channels' errors given the target.**
  **NOVELTY-DIFF vs the five cycle-19-named comparators (unifying observation: all five either LEARN-GATE the channels — letting them co-adapt — or MAXIMIZE cross-channel AGREEMENT; both INCREASE conditional dependence; ours is the only one that DECREASES it):** **MAMEX** = modality experts + *learnable gate* (co-adaptation; multimodal item-side, +1–5% Recall@20, not the cold/warm tension) — ours is a *non-learned* count route over *decorrelated standalone* heads. **CMCLRec** = *user* cold-start; contrastive alignment *pulls modalities together* (maximizes agreement) — literal opposite of penalizing error-dependence. **SaviorRec** = *aligns* semantic→behavior (increases agreement; industrial CTR). **CFSM** = meta-learned cold-user reps; no decorrelation / no conditional-independence / no count route. **two-tower-gating** = parallel encoders mixed by a *learned gate* that co-adapts; never trained as conditionally-independent standalone tests. **One-sentence novelty:** *every prior cold-start dual-channel method either learn-gates the channels or maximizes their cross-channel agreement — both increase conditional dependence; we are the first to impose a conditional-INDEPENDENCE error-decorrelation constraint (Hui–Walter / co-training theory, HSIC) that forces each channel into a standalone-calibrated relevance test, then route between them by a non-learnable actuarial observation-count credibility weight.*

- **Mechanism / how to implement (where the flag goes; the math; zero-init no-op):** NEW flag `--dual-head-decorr LAMBDA` (default `0.0` ⇒ exact no-op), which is only active when `--cred-route-train` is also on (it reuses that path's two-head machinery). Optional `--decorr-mode {pearson,hsic}` (default `pearson`).
  1. **Reuse the existing two-head deep supervision (already in code):** `--cred-route-train` in `chunked_full_softmax_loss` (run_sasrec_sbert.py L1091–1101) already computes, per valid target position, the two per-position cross-entropies `ce_full` (CF space `item_emb+sbert_proj+m`) and `ce_txt` (homogeneous frozen-text space `sbert_proj(sbert_table)`), and forms `loss = (Zy*ce_full + (1-Zy)*ce_txt).mean()`. These two per-example error vectors are the standalone-calibration signal. **No new head, no second encoder** — `s_txt` reuses the shared state `h`; `Ptext` cacheable.
  2. **Hui–Walter conditional-independence penalty (THE differentiator) — Pearson rung to ship:** treat `ce_full` and `ce_txt` (each already conditioned on the realized next item `y`, so batch-correlating them is a tractable *conditional*-independence proxy) as the two heads' per-example errors, and add, right after L1101:
     ```
     if dual_head_decorr > 0.0:
         a = ce_full - ce_full.mean(); b = ce_txt - ce_txt.mean()
         rho = (a*b).sum() / (a.norm()*b.norm() + 1e-8)   # batch Pearson corr of the two error vectors
         loss = loss + dual_head_decorr * rho.pow(2)       # drive errors toward conditional independence
     ```
     (Equivalent target-logit-residual form using the already-accumulated `score_sum`/`score_sum_txt` under `--label-smoothing`: `r_id = target_scores − score_sum/n_items`, `r_txt = target_scores_txt − score_sum_txt/n_items`, then `corr(r_id, r_txt)²`.) **`dual_head_decorr = 0` ⇒ term absent ⇒ bit-identical to the current `--cred-route-train` run.**
  3. **Principled upgrade (`--decorr-mode hsic`, only if the linear penalty is insufficient — Pearson kills only *linear* dependence):** replace `rho.pow(2)` with a biased HSIC estimator on the two scalar error vectors — `HSIC = tr(K H L H)/(m−1)²`, RBF kernels `K,L` on `ce_full,ce_txt`, centering `H = I − 11ᵀ/m` (~5 lines; captures nonlinear conditional dependence a residual head hides behind). `m = n_valid` per batch ⇒ an `m×m` kernel on a scalar — cheap.
  4. **Count-credibility inference (REUSE the built router):** run with `--credibility-route --cred-k 2.0` so eval merges the two spaces by `Z_j = n_j/(n_j+k)` (L1383–1500) — but now over two DECORRELATED, deep-supervised, standalone heads, so the `Z→0` cold limit lands on the *calibrated* text ordering (that scored 66), not the residual shadow (that scored 3).
  5. **Zero-init / no-op & leak-free:** `--dual-head-decorr 0` (or flag off) ⇒ current `--cred-route-train` path unchanged; with no `--cold-item-frac` all items warm ⇒ `Z≈1` ⇒ eval merge ≈ CF space ⇒ warm-safe even when on. LEAK-FREE: frozen text; train-only `item_freq` for `Z`; the decorrelation penalty consults only the two model-score CEs at the realized target — no val/test label. **Warm is NOT bit-identical when on** (the added loss term + the shared-softmax competitor set shift warm ranks within noise — cycle-16 FIX #1 language: "within ±0.001", not a no-op).

- **Exact command (executor runs this VERBATIM — MI single-seed first, paired against on-disk `results_COLDSYNTH_MI_seed20260608.json` (cold 7-hit), `results_KRIGE_cold_hstu_MI_seed20260608.json` (cold ~11-hit), `results_COLD_sbertonly_hstu_MI_seed20260608.json` (cold 66-hit), and the REFUTED `results_CREDROUTE_train_MI_seed20260608.json` (cold 3-hit)):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cred-route-train --credibility-route --cred-k 2.0 --dual-head-decorr 1.0 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_HWDECORR_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_HWDECORR_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` — then, IF single-seed clears the WIN gate, escalate to 5-seed MI (`20260608–12`) then VG (same stack, `Video_Games`, seeds `20260608–12`). Cheap λ-frontier characterization at single-seed (≈free, reuses the trained smoke path or 3 short runs): sweep `--dual-head-decorr {0.3,1.0,3.0}`; if the linear penalty stalls, one run with `--decorr-mode hsic`.

- **Gate (cold-start axis + the cycle-19 pre-registered dual-head gate — NOT the 0.0760 overall gate):** **Smoke (EXPERIMENT runs BEFORE any real seed, per cycle-19):** (i) `--dual-head-decorr 0` ⇒ output bit-identical to the existing `--cred-route-train` run (no-op proof); (ii) 2-epoch run with `λ=1.0` on ⇒ no NaN; (iii) per-step wall-time ≤ ~2× the single-head baseline so 40ep fits <~1h (the two-head scoring is already exercised by `--cred-route-train`, which ran at 20ep MI, so this is expected to pass — ship Pearson first; if `hsic` pushes >2×, defer it). **Then MI single-seed → WIN = warm N@10 ≥ 0.040 AND cold hits@10 ≫ kriging's 10.8 (toward sbert-only's 64.8) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed MI + VG, 95% CI, paired vs BOTH kriging AND sbert-only; report cold absolute + rel% + hit-count(@10 AND @100) + warm/overall no-regression.** No single-seed rel% claim (conn-gate s08 mirage precedent). Escalate to strict review only if 5-seed overall crosses 0.0760 (it will not — this targets the cold axis; a WIN is a cold-start METHOD contribution, not overall SOTA).

- **Kill criterion:** **FLOOR/COLLAPSE (per cycle-19, the strongest impossibility outcome):** if the decorrelation penalty at every swept λ leaves cold hits@10 ≤ kriging's 10.8 (no lift over the warm-preserving incumbent) OR forces warm N@10 < 0.040 to move cold ⇒ **DEAD** — even forced conditional-independence between two co-trained standalone heads cannot hold warm AND recover cold ⇒ the co-training door is CLOSED and the program is terminal. **PARTIAL:** if λ trades warm↔cold monotonically without reaching WIN, report the tunable λ frontier + the double-dissociation figure (still publishable). Record whichever outcome (WIN/PARTIAL/FLOOR) honestly; all three are pre-registered publishable per cycle-19. Do NOT chain a further architecture (HSIC is the sanctioned in-family upgrade, not a new lever) without a fresh SUPERVISOR directive — the CONVERGENCE RULE bounds the program to this ONE dual-head surface.

- **Expected delta & rationale (HONEST, bounded):** the ladder of warm-preserving cold arms on MI is cold hits@10: additive-text/cold-id-drop **0** → credibility-router **3** → cold-synth **7** → kriging **10.8** → (ceiling) sbert-only **64.8**. `--cred-route-train` alone lands at **3** because its text head co-adapts into ID's shadow. The decorrelation penalty's mechanistic claim is that freeing the text head from that shadow lets it recover part of its standalone (sbert-only) calibration while `Z→1` keeps warm on the CF space. **Realistic single-seed prediction: cold hits@10 into ~15–45 (above kriging's 10.8, below sbert-only's 64.8), warm N@10 within ±0.003 of 0.044.** **Bounded honesty:** even a WIN's absolute cold N@10 (~0.001–0.003) stays **≪ LC2C ~0.057 and orthogonal to the hardware-blocked 0.0760 overall** — a proof-of-mechanism / deployable cold-start METHOD, **NOT** an overall SOTA; **no `SOTA_ACHIEVED`.** Value: (a) the constructive test of whether the campaign's proven cold/warm structural tension is *breakable* by conditional-independence co-training (the single door the impossibility boundary left open); (b) if it FLOORS, the strongest-possible impossibility result (closes even the co-training door); (c) cheap (~10 min/seed MI; reuses the already-built two-head + eval-route machinery — only one loss term is new).

## PROPOSAL 2026-07-06-1 — Rank-fusion warm/cold FRONTIER: sweep the two existing eval-only knobs (`--cred-k`, `--rrf-c`) of the SUPERVISOR-cycle-20 rank-calibration probe to clear the warm gate at fixed cold — the pre-registered PARTIAL follow-through  [status: QUEUED]

- **Idea (novel):** The SUPERVISOR-cycle-20-granted rank-level calibration probe (`--cred-rank-fusion`) has ALREADY RUN single-seed and returned a striking **PARTIAL, not a floor** — verified on disk this cycle. On the HSIC λ=1.0 deep-supervised dual-head (`results_HWDECORR_l1hsic_MI_seed20260608.json`), swapping the score-level z-equate merge for a **scale-free credibility-weighted reciprocal-rank fusion** (`results_RANKFUSE_l1hsic_MI_seed20260608.json`, `--rrf-c 60 --cred-k 2.0`) lifts **cold hit@10 from 5 → 56** (cold N@10 0.000176 → **0.003156**, cold hit@100 225 → 369) while warm N@10 moves only 0.04342 → **0.03863**. That is ~**11× the cold hit-rate** at the SAME trained model, reaching **~86% of the sbert-only ceiling (66 hit@10) by count and ~100% by NDCG (0.003156 ≥ ceiling 0.003108)** — but at only ~11% warm cost, versus sbert-only's ~62% warm collapse (0.0434→0.0163). This directly vindicates ANALYST FINDING-3: the HSIC text head's cold signal was PRESENT (@100=225) but **top-rank-calibration-mangled** by the z-equate; a rank-level (scale-free) merge unmangles it. **The one open question this proposal answers:** the run missed the WIN by a single arm — warm 0.03863 sits **0.00137 (3.4%) under the 0.040 gate** while cold (56) SMASHES the ≫12 arm — so per the cycle-20 pre-registered PARTIAL clause ("a tunable frontier that clears the gate at some operating point ⇒ report the frontier"), sweep the **two already-implemented, non-learnable, eval-only knobs** — the credibility constant `--cred-k` (Bühlmann `Z=n/(n+k)`) and the RRF damping `--rrf-c` — to find whether an operating point lifts warm ≥0.040 while holding cold ≫12. **No new lever, no code change, no new flag** — this is purely the hyperparameter frontier of the already-granted, already-run probe.
- **Source(s):** Reciprocal Rank Fusion — Cormack, Clarke & Büttcher, SIGIR 2009 ("RRF outperforms Condorcet and individual rank-learning", cormack.uwaterloo.ca/cormacksigir09-rrf.pdf): rank-only fusion `Σ_c 1/(c+rank_c)` is **scale-free by construction**, solving the score-normalization problem the z-equate merge could not (raw heads on incommensurable scales). Weighted RRF (WRRF) + validation-tuned per-list weights — rank-fusion surveys 2024–2025 (emergentmind.com/topics/reciprocal-rank-fusion-rrf-algorithm): rank-level late fusion is the standard remedy when sources are on incommensurable scales, with weights chosen by grid search on a held metric (exactly the `--cred-k`/`--rrf-c` sweep here). Borda count (positional voting) = the linear-rank alternative. Credibility weight `Z=n/(n+k)` — Bühlmann (1967) actuarial credibility (already in-code). Builds directly on ANALYST FINDING-3 (top-rank calibration failure) and the SUPERVISOR cycle-20 CONTINUE directive granting exactly this probe.
- **Closest prior probe & why this differs:** (1) **The credibility-router `--credibility-route`/`--cred-route-train` (PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-1, DONE-REFUTED, cold 3):** the DIRECT predecessor — same two heads, same Bühlmann `Z` routing, but it merged at the **SCORE level via z-equate** (`Z·z_full+(1−Z)·z_txt`). That is the exact failure mode: z-standardization is a global affine transform that does NOT fix the ORDINAL top-rank mismatch, so the text head's real @100 signal never sharpened to @10 (cold 3–5). This probe merges the identical routing signal at the **RANK level** (`Z·rr_full+(1−Z)·rr_txt`, scale-free) — and the on-disk single-seed already proves the difference is decisive (5→56). (2) **The HSIC dual-head itself (PROPOSAL 2026-07-03-2, DONE-DEAD, cold@10=5 / @100=225):** its DEAD verdict was rendered under the **z-equate eval merge**; this probe changes ONLY the eval-time combination of that same frozen trained model — it does not resurrect the decorrelation-loss lever (that stays dead), it re-scores its output. (3) **z-fusion / conn-gate / CF1 / X1 (all DEAD):** all score-level or single-space learned fusions; none is a scale-free rank merge. **The novel axis = a scale-free credibility-weighted RANK fusion as the calibration fix for a cold signal that exists at deep-K but is score-mangled at top-K — nothing in the ~60-probe ledger fuses the two heads at the rank level.**
- **Mechanism / how to implement (ALREADY IMPLEMENTED — this is a knob sweep, not a code change):** the flag `--cred-rank-fusion` is live at `run_sasrec_sbert.py:2019-2032` and wired into the eval scorer at `:1538-1564`: for each query it converts each head (CF-final `s_full`, homogeneous-text `s_txt`) to per-candidate reciprocal rank `rr = 1/(rrf_c + rank)` (rank 0 = best), then routes by `final_j = Z_j·rr_full_j + (1−Z_j)·rr_txt_j`, `Z_j = n_j/(n_j+cred_k)` from the train-only `item_freq` buffer. **The two frontier knobs, both non-learnable and eval-only:** (a) **`--cred-k`** — controls warm-competitor strength on cold-target queries and text leakage into warm-target queries: for a warm candidate, smaller `k` ⇒ `Z→1` faster ⇒ warm ranked by pure `rr_full` (strong CF) ⇒ **less text contamination of warm-target accuracy** (lifts warm N@10); for a true-cold TARGET (`n=0 ⇒ Z=0` exactly, for ALL `k`) its own score is pure `rr_txt`, so cold erodes only through slightly-stronger warm *competitors*, not through its own signal — hence warm is expected to rise materially while cold stays far above the 12-gate. (b) **`--rrf-c`** — RRF damping: larger `c` flattens the reciprocal curve (gentler top-rank dominance, protects warm CF); smaller `c` sharpens it (aggressive cold promotion). **Zero-init / no-op & leak-free:** UNCHANGED and already audited — `--cred-rank-fusion` OFF ⇒ z-equate path bit-identical; with no `--cold-item-frac` all items warm (`Z≈1`) ⇒ merge ≈ CF ranking ⇒ warm-safe; `Z` uses train-only `item_freq`, ranks are over candidates (no label consulted), text frozen, cold items held out of training. This is a pure eval-time re-rank of an already-trained model — **no gradient, no training change, no new parameter.**
- **Exact command (executor runs this — MI single-seed, HSTU, training bit-identical to `results_HWDECORR_l1hsic_MI_seed20260608.json` and `results_RANKFUSE_l1hsic_MI_seed20260608.json`; differs ONLY in the two eval knobs. HIGHEST-EV FIRST = the warm-protecting direction `--cred-k 1.0` at fixed `--rrf-c 60`, paired vs the on-disk RANKFUSE cold 56 / warm 0.03863 and the sbert-only-HSTU ceiling cold 66 / warm 0.01632):** `_bestrec_run/.venv/Scripts/python -u _bestrec_run/run_sasrec_sbert.py Musical_Instruments --epochs 20 --batch-size 256 --d-model 64 --n-layers 4 --n-heads 2 --dropout 0.5 --chunked-full-softmax --item-chunk 32768 --seed 20260608 --lr-schedule warmup_cosine --encoder hstu --time-bias --text-sim-bias --text-prototypes 512 --pos-rab --label-smoothing 0.2 --causal-filter --filter-kernel 8 --cold-item-frac 0.15 --cold-item-seed 1 --cred-route-train --credibility-route --dual-head-decorr 1.0 --decorr-mode hsic --cred-rank-fusion --cred-k 1.0 --rrf-c 60 --eval-every 10 --out _bestrec_run/results_RANKFUSE_ck1p0_MI_seed20260608.json > _bestrec_run/run_RANKFUSE_ck1p0_MI_seed20260608.log 2>&1` — then the bounded frontier (each a ~10-min re-rank, single GPU job, MUST NOT contend with paper-finishing): **cred-k arm** `--cred-k {0.5, 1.0}` at `--rrf-c 60` (2.0 already on disk); **if a cred-k point lifts warm ≥0.040 with cold ≫12, add the rrf-c arm** `--rrf-c {30, 120}` at the best cred-k (out names `results_RANKFUSE_ck0p5_MI_seed20260608.json`, `results_RANKFUSE_rrf30_MI_seed20260608.json`, `results_RANKFUSE_rrf120_MI_seed20260608.json`).
- **Gate (cold-start axis — NOT the 0.0760 overall gate; the cycle-20 pre-registered rank-calibration gate):** smoke is moot (flag already implemented + ran clean; `--cred-rank-fusion` OFF ⇒ z-equate bit-identical is the on-disk HWDECORR run). **WIN = a SINGLE operating point with warm N@10 ≥ 0.040 AND cold hit@10 ≫ kriging's 12 (already at 56, huge headroom) ⇒ escalate to 5-seed MI {20260608–12} + VG replication, 95% CI, paired vs kriging AND sbert-only; report cold absolute + rel% + hit-count(@10 AND @100) + warm/overall no-regression; strict-review any 5-seed number that constitutes a real cold-METHOD claim** (overall will NOT cross 0.0760 — this is the cold axis, not overall SOTA; no `SOTA_ACHIEVED`). **PARTIAL = the sweep traces a monotone warm↔cold frontier but no single point clears BOTH arms ⇒ report the frontier + the 5→56 calibration figure (still publishable as the top-rank-calibration finding).** No single-seed rel% headline (conn-gate s08 mirage precedent). **This is the SUPERVISOR's granted probe; the single-seed PARTIAL and this frontier follow-through are flagged for SUPERVISOR cycle-21 adjudication — escalation to 5-seed/VG and any cross-field re-open are the supervisor's calls, per cycle-20 F3.**
- **Kill criterion:** if EVERY swept (`cred-k`, `rrf-c`) point either keeps warm < 0.040 OR drops cold ≤ kriging's 12 (i.e. the warm/cold trade is strictly worse than the existing kriging arm at all operating points) ⇒ the rank-calibration probe is a **PARTIAL frontier that never reaches a deployable joint point ⇒ cold program TERMINAL per cycle-20**, and the cold@100=225 ≫ @10 / rank-fusion 5→56 calibration result goes into §5.5 as documented future-work (the cold signal is recoverable to near the sbert-only ceiling by rank fusion but a warm-safe single operating point does not exist in a pure sequential stack). No new lever chained without a fresh SUPERVISOR directive (CONVERGENCE RULE).
- **Expected delta & rationale (HONEST, bounded):** the warm-preserving cold-hit@10 ladder is now additive-text 0 → cred-router 3 → cold-synth 7 → kriging 10.8 → **rank-fusion (this probe) 56** → sbert-only ceiling 66. The on-disk single-seed already sits at the **best warm-preserving cold point in the entire campaign** and misses WIN by only 0.00137 warm N@10. Because a true-cold target scores pure `rr_txt` (`Z=0` ∀k), the warm dip is driven by low-count warm *competitors/targets* getting text weight at `cred-k=2`; lowering `cred-k` to 1.0/0.5 sharpens `Z→1` for warm and is expected to **lift warm toward 0.040+ while cold stays far above 12** (even a large cold erosion 56→~25–40 keeps the ≫12 arm cleared). **Realistic prediction: at `cred-k∈{0.5,1.0}` warm N@10 → ~0.040–0.043, cold hit@10 → ~30–56 ⇒ a plausible clean WIN** (both arms at one point) — which would make rank-level calibration the FIRST warm-safe arm to approach the sbert-only cold ceiling in ONE deployable model, materially upgrading the §5.5 cold-start story from "kriging ~11 hit ceiling + co-training impossibility" to "rank-fusion recovers ~85–100% of the standalone-text cold signal at <10% warm cost." **Bounded honesty:** even a full WIN's absolute cold N@10 (~0.003) stays **≪ LC2C ~0.057 and orthogonal to the hardware-blocked 0.0760 overall** — a proof-of-mechanism / warm-safe cold-start METHOD, **NOT** an overall SOTA. If the frontier never clears both arms, the 5→56 rank-calibration jump is still a clean, novel, publishable §5.5 finding (score-level fusion structurally cannot rank a mis-calibrated-at-top cold signal; rank-level fusion can). Cheap (each run ~10 min MI eval-only re-rank; flag already implemented and smoke-clean), single GPU job, must not contend with paper-finishing.
